# Evaluating Datalog over Semirings: A Grounding-based Approach

评估半环上的Datalog：一种基于接地的研究方法

HANGDONG ZHAO, University of Wisconsin-Madison, USA

赵杭栋，美国威斯康星大学麦迪逊分校

SHALEEN DEEP, Microsoft Gray Systems Lab, USA

沙林·迪普，美国微软格雷系统实验室

PARASCHOS KOUTRIS, University of Wisconsin-Madison, USA

帕拉肖斯·库特里斯，美国威斯康星大学麦迪逊分校

SUDEEPA ROY, Duke University, USA

苏迪帕·罗伊，美国杜克大学

VAL TANNEN, University of Pennsylvania, USA

瓦尔·坦嫩，美国宾夕法尼亚大学

Datalog is a powerful yet elegant language that allows expressing recursive computation. Although Datalog evaluation has been extensively studied in the literature, so far, only loose upper bounds are known on how fast a Datalog program can be evaluated. In this work, we ask the following question: given a Datalog program over a naturally-ordered semiring $\sigma$ ,what is the tightest possible runtime? To this end,our main contribution is a general two-phase framework for analyzing the data complexity of Datalog over $\sigma$ : first ground the program into an equivalent system of polynomial equations (i.e. grounding) and then find the least fixpoint of the grounding over $\sigma$ . We present algorithms that use structure-aware query evaluation techniques to obtain the smallest possible groundings. Next, efficient algorithms for fixpoint evaluation are introduced over two classes of semirings: (1) finite-rank semirings and (2) absorptive semirings of total order. Combining both phases, we obtain state-of-the-art and new algorithmic results. Finally, we complement our results with a matching fine-grained lower bound.

Datalog是一种强大而优雅的语言，能够表达递归计算。尽管文献中对Datalog评估已有广泛研究，但迄今为止关于Datalog程序的最快评估速度仅存在宽松的上界。本研究提出核心问题：给定自然序半环$\sigma$上的Datalog程序，其最紧致的运行时是多少？我们的主要贡献是提出一个通用的两阶段框架来分析Datalog在$\sigma$上的数据复杂性：首先将程序接地为等价多项式方程组（即接地阶段），然后在$\sigma$上求接地系统的最小不动点。我们提出采用结构感知查询评估技术来获得最小可能接地规模的算法。接着针对两类半环引入高效的不动点评估算法：（1）有限秩半环；（2）全序吸收半环。结合这两个阶段，我们获得了最先进的算法结果及新发现。最后通过匹配的细粒度下界对研究结果进行了补充。

CCS Concepts: - Theory of computation $\rightarrow$ Database query processing and optimization (theory).

CCS概念：- 计算理论$\rightarrow$数据库查询处理与优化（理论）

Additional Key Words and Phrases: Datalog, Semirings, Evaluation, Grounding

附加关键词：Datalog，半环，评估，接地

## 1 INTRODUCTION

## 1 引言

Datalog is a recursive query language that has gained prominence due to its expressivity and rich applications across multiple domains, including graph processing [43], declarative program analysis [42, 44], and business analytics [23]. Most of the prior work focuses on Datalog programs over the Boolean semiring (this corresponds to the standard relational join semantics): popular examples include same generation [5], cycle finding, and pattern matching. Many program analysis tasks such as Dyck-reachability [31, 32, 41], context-free reachability [36], and Andersen's analysis [4] can also be naturally cast as Datalog programs over the Boolean domain. However, modern data analytics frequently involve aggregations over recursion. Seminal work by Green et al. [24] established the semantics of Datalog over the so-called $K$ -relations where tuples are annotated by the domain of a fixed semiring. Recently,Abo Khamis et al. [28] proposed Datalog ${}^{ \circ  }$ ,an elegant extension of Datalog, and established key algebraic properties that governs convergence of Datalog ${}^{ \circ  }$ . The authors further made the observation that the convergence rate of Datalog can be studied by confining to the class of naturally-ordered semirings (formally defined in Section 2).

Datalog作为一种递归查询语言，因其表达能力和在图处理[43]、声明式程序分析[42,44]及商业分析[23]等领域的广泛应用而备受关注。现有研究主要聚焦布尔半环上的Datalog程序（对应标准关系连接语义），典型应用包括同代计算[5]、环路检测和模式匹配。程序分析任务如Dyck可达性[31,32,41]、上下文无关可达性[36]和安德森分析[4]也可自然地表述为布尔域上的Datalog程序。然而现代数据分析常涉及递归聚合操作。Green等人[24]的开创性工作建立了所谓$K$关系上的Datalog语义，其中元组通过固定半环的域进行标注。近期Abo Khamis等人[28]提出Datalog${}^{ \circ  }$这一优雅扩展，并确立了控制Datalog${}^{ \circ  }$收敛性的关键代数性质。作者进一步指出，通过限定在自然序半环类（第2节正式定义）可研究Datalog的收敛速率。

A parallel line of work has sought to characterize the complexity of Datalog evaluation. The general data complexity of Datalog is P-complete [11, 20], with the canonical P-complete Datalog

另一系列研究致力于刻画Datalog评估的复杂性。Datalog的通用数据复杂度是P完全[11,20]，其典型P完全程序：

program:

程序：

$$
T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  U\left( {x}_{1}\right) .\;T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \land  T\left( {x}_{3}\right)  \land  R\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{1}}\right) . \tag{1}
$$

Some fragments of Datalog can have lower data complexity: the evaluation for non-recursive Datalog is in ${\mathrm{{AC}}}_{0}$ ,whereas evaluation for Datalog with linear rules is in $\mathrm{{NC}}$ and thus efficiently parallelizable $\left\lbrack  {3,{45}}\right\rbrack$ . However,all such results do not tell us how efficiently we can evaluate a given Datalog program. As an example, the program (1) can be evaluated in linear time with respect to the input size $m$ [22]. A deeper understanding of precise upper bounds for Datalog is important, given that it can capture practical problems (all of which are in P) across various domains as mentioned before.

某些Datalog片段具有更低的数据复杂度：非递归Datalog评估属于${\mathrm{{AC}}}_{0}$，而线性规则Datalog评估属于$\mathrm{{NC}}$，因此可高效并行化$\left\lbrack  {3,{45}}\right\rbrack$。然而这些结果均未说明如何高效评估特定Datalog程序。例如程序(1)可根据输入规模$m$在线性时间内完成评估[22]。鉴于Datalog能捕获前文所述各领域的实际问题（均属P类），深入理解其精确上界具有重要意义。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

Unfortunately, even for the Boolean semiring, the current general algorithmic techniques for Datalog evaluation typically aim for an imprecise polynomial bound instead of specifying the tightest possible exponent. Semi-naïve or naïve evaluation only provides upper bounds on the number of iterations, ignoring the computational cost of each iteration. For example, program (1) can be evaluated in at most $O\left( m\right)$ iterations,but the cost of an iteration can be as large as $\Theta \left( m\right)$ . Further, going beyond the Boolean semiring, obtaining tight bounds for evaluating general Datalog programs over popular semirings (such as absorptive semirings with a total order which are routinely used in data analytics $\left\lbrack  {{39},{40}}\right\rbrack$ ) is unclear. In particular,proving correctness of program evaluation is not immediate and the impact of the semiring on the evaluation time remains unknown.

遗憾的是，即便对于布尔半环而言，当前Datalog求值的通用算法技术通常追求的是不精确的多项式界限，而非确定最紧致的可能指数。半朴素或朴素求值仅提供了迭代次数的上界，忽略了每次迭代的计算成本。例如程序(1)最多可在$O\left( m\right)$次迭代内求值，但单次迭代成本可能高达$\Theta \left( m\right)$。此外，在布尔半环之外，如何为常用半环（如数据分析中常规使用的全序吸收半环$\left\lbrack  {{39},{40}}\right\rbrack$）上的通用Datalog程序求值获取紧致界限尚不明确。特别是，程序求值的正确性证明并非显而易见，且半环对求值时间的影响仍属未知。

Endeavors to pinpoint the exact data complexity for Datalog fragments have focused on the class of Conjunctive Queries (CQs) [26, 29, 37] and union of CQs [8], where most have been dedicated to develop faster algorithms. When recursion is involved, however, exact runtimes are known only for restricted classes of Datalog. Seminal work of Yannakakis [50] established a $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ runtime for chain Datalog programs (formally defined in Section 2.3),where $n$ is the size of the active domain. Such programs have a direct correspondence to context-free grammars and capture a fundamental class of static analysis known as context-free reachability (CFL). When the chain Datalog program corresponds to a regular grammar,the runtime can be further improved to $O{\left( m \cdot  n\right) }^{1}$ . An $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ algorithm was proposed for the Datalog program that captures Andersen’s analysis [36]. Recently, Casel and Schmid [9] studied the fine-grained complexity of evaluation, enumeration, and counting problems over regular path queries (also a Datalog fragment) with similar upper bounds. However, none of the above techniques generalize to arbitrary Datalog programs.

针对Datalog片段精确数据复杂度的研究主要集中在合取查询(CQs)[26,29,37]及CQs联合[8]类别，其中多数致力于开发更快速算法。然而涉及递归时，精确时间复杂度仅在某些受限Datalog类别中已知。Yannakakis开创性工作[50]为链式Datalog程序（2.3节正式定义）建立了$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$运行时间，其中$n$表示活跃域大小。此类程序与上下文无关文法直接对应，并捕获了称为上下文无关可达性(CFL)的基础静态分析类别。当链式Datalog程序对应正则文法时，运行时间可进一步优化至$O{\left( m \cdot  n\right) }^{1}$。针对实现Andersen分析的Datalog程序[36]，研究者提出了$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$算法。近期Casel与Schmid[9]研究了具有类似上界的正则路径查询（亦属Datalog片段）在求值、枚举和计数问题上的细粒度复杂度。但上述技术均无法推广至任意Datalog程序。

Our Contribution. In this paper,we ask: given a Datalog program $P$ over a naturally-ordered semiring $\sigma$ ,what is the tightest possible runtime as a function of the input size $m$ and the size of the active domain $n$ ? Our main contributions are as follows.

本文贡献。本文探讨：给定自然序半环$\sigma$上的Datalog程序$P$，作为输入规模$m$与活跃域大小$n$的函数，其最紧致运行时是多少？我们的主要贡献如下。

We propose a general,two-phased framework for evaluation of $P$ over $\sigma$ . The first phase grounds $P$ into an equivalent system of polynomial equations $G$ . Though constructing groundings naïvely is rather straightforward, we show that groundings of smaller size are attainable via tree decomposition techniques. The second phase evaluates the least fixpoint of $G$ over the underlying semiring $\sigma$ . We show that finite-rank semirings and absorptive semirings with total order,two routinely-used classes of semirings in practice, admit efficient algorithms for least fixpoint computation. We apply our framework to prove state-of-the-art and new results for practical Datalog fragments (e.g. linear Datalog).

我们提出一个通用的两阶段框架用于$\sigma$上$P$的求值。第一阶段将$P$接地为等价多项式方程组$G$。虽然朴素构建接地过程较为直接，但我们证明通过树分解技术可获得更小规模的接地。第二阶段在半环$\sigma$上计算$G$的最小不动点。我们证明实践中常用的两类半环——有限秩半环与全序吸收半环，均支持高效的最小不动点计算算法。应用该框架，我们为实用Datalog片段（如线性Datalog）证明了前沿结论与新结果。

Further, we establish tightness of our results by demonstrating a matching lower bound on the running time (conditioned on the popular min-weight $\ell$ -clique conjecture) and size of the grounded program (unconditional) for a class of Datalog programs.

此外，通过展示某类Datalog程序在运行时间（基于流行的min-weight$\ell$-团猜想）与接地程序规模（无条件）上的匹配下界，我们确立了研究结果的紧致性。

## 2 PRELIMINARIES

## 2 预备知识

### 2.1 Datalog

### 2.1 Datalog

We review standard Datalog [1] that consists of a set of rules over a set of extensional and intensional relations (simply referred to as EDB and IDB respectively, henceforth). EDBs correspond to relations in a given database, each comprising a set of EDB tuples, whereas IDBs are derived by the rules. The head of each rule is an IDB, and the body consists of zero or more EDBs and IDBs as a conjunctive query defining how the head IDB is derived. We illustrate with the example of transitive closure on a binary EDB $R$ denoting edges in a directed graph,a single IDB $T$ ,and two rules:

我们回顾标准Datalog[1]，其由一组建立在扩展关系与内涵关系（下文简称EDB与IDB）上的规则构成。EDB对应数据库中给定的关系，每个EDB包含一组元组，而IDB通过规则推导得出。每条规则头部是一个IDB，主体由零个或多个EDB与IDB构成，作为定义头部IDB如何推导的合取查询。我们以表示有向图边的二元EDB$R$、单个IDB$T$和两条规则为例，说明传递闭包：

$$
T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,\;T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right)  \land  R\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{2}}\right)  \tag{2}
$$

---

${}^{1}m$ denotes the size of the input data and $n$ denotes the size of the active domain for a Datalog program throughout the paper.

全文范围内，${}^{1}m$表示Datalog程序的输入数据规模，$n$表示活跃域大小。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

In standard Datalog, IDBs are derived given EDBs (equivalently, evaluation is over the Boolean semiring), whereas as discussed in Section 1, the evaluation can also be considered over other semirings where the EDBs are annotated with elements of that semiring.

在标准Datalog中，IDB（可推导关系）由EDB（基本关系）派生而来（等价于在布尔半环上进行求值），而如第1节所述，求值过程也可在其他半环上考虑，此时EDB会被标注为该半环的元素。

### 2.2 Semirings and Their Properties

### 2.2 半环及其性质

Semirings. A tuple $\sigma  = \left( {D,\oplus ,\otimes ,0,1}\right)$ is a semiring if $\oplus$ and $\otimes$ are binary operators over $D$ for which:

半环。若$\oplus$和$\otimes$是定义在$D$上的二元运算符，且满足以下条件，则称元组$\sigma  = \left( {D,\oplus ,\otimes ,0,1}\right)$构成一个半环：

(1) $\left( {D,\oplus ,0}\right)$ is a commutative monoid with identity0(i.e., $\oplus$ is associative and commutative, and $a \oplus  0 = a$ for all $a \in  D$ );

(1) $\left( {D,\oplus ,0}\right)$是关于运算符$\oplus$的交换幺半群（即$\oplus$满足结合律与交换律，且对所有$a \in  D$有$a \oplus  0 = a$）；

(2) $\left( {D,\otimes ,1}\right)$ is a monoid with identity1for $\otimes$ ;

(2) $\left( {D,\otimes ,1}\right)$是关于运算符$\otimes$的幺半群，其单位元为1；

(3) $\otimes$ distributes over $\oplus$ ,i.e., $a \otimes  \left( {b \oplus  c}\right)  = \left( {a \otimes  b}\right)  \oplus  \left( {a \otimes  c}\right)$ for $a,b,c \in  D$ ; and

(3) $\otimes$对$\oplus$满足分配律，即对$a,b,c \in  D$有$a \otimes  \left( {b \oplus  c}\right)  = \left( {a \otimes  b}\right)  \oplus  \left( {a \otimes  c}\right)$；

(4) $a \otimes  \mathbf{0} = \mathbf{0}$ for all $a \in  \mathbf{D}$ .

(4) 对所有$a \in  \mathbf{D}$有$a \otimes  \mathbf{0} = \mathbf{0}$。

$\sigma$ is a commutative semiring if $\otimes$ is commutative. Examples include the Boolean semiring $\mathbb{B} =$ (\{false,true\}, $\vee  , \land$ ,false,true),the natural numbers semiring $\left( {\mathbb{N},+,\cdot ,0,1}\right)$ ,and the real semiring $\left( {\mathbb{R},+,\cdot ,0,1}\right)$ .

当$\otimes$满足交换律时，$\sigma$称为交换半环。实例包括布尔半环$\mathbb{B} =$（\{假,真\}, $\vee  , \land$, 假, 真）、自然数半环$\left( {\mathbb{N},+,\cdot ,0,1}\right)$以及实数半环$\left( {\mathbb{R},+,\cdot ,0,1}\right)$。

Naturally-Ordered Semirings. In a semiring $\sigma$ ,the relation $x \sqsubseteq  y$ (or $y \sqsupseteq  x$ ),defined as $\exists z$ : $x \oplus  z = y$ ,is reflexive and transitive,but not necessarily anti-symmetric. If it is anti-symmetric,then $\left( {D, \sqsubseteq  }\right)$ is a poset (0is the minimum element since $\mathbf{0} \oplus  a = a$ ) and we say that $\sigma$ is naturally-ordered. For example, $\mathbb{N}$ is naturally-ordered with the usual order $\leq$ . However, $\mathbb{R}$ is not naturally-ordered, since $x \sqsubseteq  y$ for every $x,y \in  \mathbb{R}$ . We focus on naturally-ordered commutative semirings in this paper (simply referred to as semirings from now).

自然序半环。在半环$\sigma$中，若关系$x \sqsubseteq  y$（或$y \sqsupseteq  x$）定义为$\exists z$：$x \oplus  z = y$，则该关系具有自反性和传递性，但不一定具备反对称性。若具备反对称性，则$\left( {D, \sqsubseteq  }\right)$构成偏序集（由于$\mathbf{0} \oplus  a = a$，0是最小元素），此时称$\sigma$为自然序半环。例如，$\mathbb{N}$在常规序$\leq$下是自然序半环。但$\mathbb{R}$不是自然序半环，因为对所有$x,y \in  \mathbb{R}$都有$x \sqsubseteq  y$。本文聚焦于自然序交换半环（下文简称为半环）。

$\omega$ -complete & $\omega$ -continuous Semirings. An $\omega$ -chain in a poset is a sequence ${a}_{0} \sqsubseteq  {a}_{1} \sqsubseteq  \ldots$ with elements from the poset. A naturally-ordered semiring $\sigma$ is $\omega$ -complete if every (infinite) $\omega$ -chain ${a}_{0} \sqsubseteq  {a}_{1} \sqsubseteq  \cdots$ has a least upper bound sup ${a}_{i}$ ,and is $\omega$ -continuous if also $\forall a \in  D,a \oplus  \sup {a}_{i} =$ $\sup \left( {a \oplus  {a}_{i}}\right)$ and $a \otimes  \sup {a}_{i} = \sup \left( {\mathrm{a} \otimes  {\mathrm{a}}_{\mathrm{i}}}\right)$ .

$\omega$完全与$\omega$连续半环。偏序集中的$\omega$链是指元素取自该偏序集的序列${a}_{0} \sqsubseteq  {a}_{1} \sqsubseteq  \ldots$。若自然序半环$\sigma$中每个（无限）$\omega$链${a}_{0} \sqsubseteq  {a}_{1} \sqsubseteq  \cdots$都存在最小上界sup${a}_{i}$，则称其为$\omega$完全半环；若还满足$\forall a \in  D,a \oplus  \sup {a}_{i} =$$\sup \left( {a \oplus  {a}_{i}}\right)$和$a \otimes  \sup {a}_{i} = \sup \left( {\mathrm{a} \otimes  {\mathrm{a}}_{\mathrm{i}}}\right)$，则称其为$\omega$连续半环。

Rank. The rank of a strictly increasing $\omega$ -chain ${a}_{0} \sqsubset  {a}_{1} \sqsubset  \cdots  \sqsubset  {a}_{r}$ is $r\left( {x \sqsubset  y\text{if}x \sqsubseteq  y\text{and}x \neq  y}\right)$ . We say that a semiring $\sigma$ has rank $r$ if every strictly increasing sequence has rank at most $r$ . Any semiring over a finite domain has constant rank (e.g. B has rank 1).

秩。严格递增$\omega$链${a}_{0} \sqsubset  {a}_{1} \sqsubset  \cdots  \sqsubset  {a}_{r}$的秩定义为$r\left( {x \sqsubset  y\text{if}x \sqsubseteq  y\text{and}x \neq  y}\right)$。若半环$\sigma$中所有严格递增序列的秩都不超过$r$，则称该半环具有$r$秩。有限域上的任何半环都具有恒定秩（例如布尔半环的秩为1）。

We highlight two special classes of naturally-ordered semirings to be studied in this paper: dioids and absorptive semirings.

本文重点研究两类特殊的自然序半环：双半环与吸收半环。

Dioids. A dioid $\sigma$ is a semiring where $\oplus$ is idempotent,i.e. $a \oplus  a = a$ for any $a \in  D$ . A dioid is naturally ordered,i.e. $a \sqsubseteq  b$ if $a \oplus  b = b$ . The natural order satisfies the monotonicity property: for all $a,b,c \in  D,a \sqsubseteq  b$ implies $a \oplus  c \sqsubseteq  b \oplus  c$ and $a \otimes  c \sqsubseteq  b \otimes  c$ .

双半环。双半环$\sigma$是指加法$\oplus$具有幂等性的半环，即对任意$a \in  D$满足$a \oplus  a = a$。双半环具有自然序性质：当$a \oplus  b = b$时成立$a \sqsubseteq  b$。该自然序满足单调性：对所有$a,b,c \in  D,a \sqsubseteq  b$都有$a \oplus  c \sqsubseteq  b \oplus  c$与$a \otimes  c \sqsubseteq  b \otimes  c$成立。

Absorptive Semirings. A semiring $\sigma$ is absorptive if $1 \oplus  a = 1$ for every $a \in  D$ . An absorptive semiring is also called 0 -stable in [28]. An absorptive semiring is a dioid and $\mathbf{0} \sqsubseteq  a \sqsubseteq  \mathbf{1}$ ,for any $a \in  D$ . An equivalent characterization of absorptiveness is in Lemma A.1. Simple examples of absorptive semirings are the Boolean semiring and the tropical semiring ${\operatorname{Top}}^{ + } = \left( {{\mathbb{R}}_{ + }\cup \{ \infty \} ,\min ,+,\infty ,0}\right)$ (that can be used to measure the shortest path),where its natural order $x \sqsubseteq  y$ is the reverse order $x \geq  y$ on ${\mathbb{R}}_{ + } \cup  \{ \infty \}$ .

吸收半环。若半环$\sigma$对所有$a \in  D$满足$1 \oplus  a = 1$，则称其为吸收半环（文献[28]中亦称0-稳定半环）。吸收半环必为双半环，且对任意$a \in  D$有$\mathbf{0} \sqsubseteq  a \sqsubseteq  \mathbf{1}$。吸收性的等价刻画见引理A.1。典型例子包括布尔半环和热带半环${\operatorname{Top}}^{ + } = \left( {{\mathbb{R}}_{ + }\cup \{ \infty \} ,\min ,+,\infty ,0}\right)$（用于计算最短路径），其自然序$x \sqsubseteq  y$实为${\mathbb{R}}_{ + } \cup  \{ \infty \}$上的逆序$x \geq  y$。

### 2.3 Datalog over Semirings

### 2.3 半环上的Datalog

Next we describe the syntax of Datalog over a semiring $\sigma$ . Here EDBs are considered as $\sigma$ -relations, i.e.,each tuple in each EDB $R$ is annotated by an element from the domain of $\sigma$ . Tuples not in the EDB are annotated by 0 implicitly. Standard relations are essentially $\mathbb{B}$ -relations. When a Datalog program is evaluated on such $\sigma$ -relations,IDB $\sigma$ -relations are derived. Here,conjunction $\left( \land \right)$ is interpreted as $\otimes$ ,and disjunction as $\oplus$ of $\sigma$ ,as discussed in [24].

接下来描述半环$\sigma$上Datalog的语法。此处将EDB视为$\sigma$关系，即每个EDB$R$中的元组都由$\sigma$的域中元素标注，未出现的元组隐式标注为0。标准关系本质上是$\mathbb{B}$关系。如文献[24]所述，当Datalog程序在此类$\sigma$关系上求值时，将推导出IDB$\sigma$关系，其中合取$\left( \land \right)$解释为半环乘法$\otimes$，析取解释为半环加法$\oplus$。

Sum-product Queries. The Datalog program (2) has two rules of the same head, thus an IDB tuple can be derived by either rule (i.e. a disjunction over rules). Following Abo Khamis et al. [28], we combine all rules with the same IDB head into one using disjunction. Hence the program under a semiring $\sigma$ is written as:

和积查询。Datalog程序(2)包含两条同头规则，因此IDB元组可通过任一规则推导（即规则间的析取）。参照Abo Khamis等人[28]的方法，我们将所有同头IDB规则用析合并为一条。因此在半环$\sigma$下的程序可表示为：

$$
T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \oplus  \left( {{\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  R\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{2}}\right) }\right) . \tag{3}
$$

where $\oplus$ corresponds to alternative usage (or disjunction) [24] and the implicit $\exists {x}_{3}$ in the second rule of (2) is made explicit by ${\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}$ . Let $\ell$ be the number of variables ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{\ell }$ in a Datalog program as in Eqn. (3). For $J \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ ,where $\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   = \{ 1,2,\ldots ,\ell \} ,{\mathbf{x}}_{J}$ denotes the set of variables $\left\{  {{x}_{j} \mid  j \in  J}\right\}$ . In the rest of the paper,w.l.o.g.,we consider a Datalog program as a set of rules of distinct IDB heads, where each rule has the following form:

其中$\oplus$表示替代用法（或析取）[24]，而式(2)第二条规则中的隐式$\exists {x}_{3}$通过${\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}$显式表达。设$\ell$为Datalog程序中变量的数量${x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{\ell }$，如式(3)所示。对于$J \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$，其中$\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   = \{ 1,2,\ldots ,\ell \} ,{\mathbf{x}}_{J}$表示变量集合$\left\{  {{x}_{j} \mid  j \in  J}\right\}$。本文后续部分，在不失一般性的前提下，我们将Datalog程序视为具有不同IDB头规则的集合，每条规则形式如下：

$$
T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \leftarrow  {\varphi }_{1}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \oplus  {\varphi }_{2}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \oplus  \ldots  \tag{4}
$$

Here ${\mathbf{x}}_{H}$ denotes the set of head variables. The body of a rule is a sum (i.e., $\oplus$ ) over one or more sum-product queries (defined below) ${\varphi }_{1}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) ,{\varphi }_{2}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) ,\ldots$ corresponding to different derivations of the IDB $T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ over a semiring $\sigma$ . Formally,a sum-product (in short,sum-prod) query $\varphi$ over $\sigma$ has the following form:

此处${\mathbf{x}}_{H}$表示头部变量集合。规则体是一个或多个和积查询（定义见后）${\varphi }_{1}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) ,{\varphi }_{2}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) ,\ldots$的求和运算（即$\oplus$），对应半环$\sigma$上IDB$T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$的不同推导形式。形式上，半环$\sigma$上的和积（简称sum-prod）查询$\varphi$具有以下形式：

$$
\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  : \;\bigoplus {\bigotimes }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  } \smallsetminus  H}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)  \tag{5}
$$

where (1) ${\mathbf{x}}_{H}$ is the set of head variables,(2) each ${T}_{J}$ is an EDB or IDB predicate,and (3) $\left( {\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is the associated hypergraph of $\varphi$ with hyperedges $\mathcal{E} \subseteq  {2}^{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$ and vertex set as ${\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$ .

其中(1)${\mathbf{x}}_{H}$是头部变量集合，(2)每个${T}_{J}$是EDB或IDB谓词，(3)$\left( {\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$是$\varphi$对应的超图，其超边为$\mathcal{E} \subseteq  {2}^{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$，顶点集为${\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$。

For every Datalog program in the sum-product query form, we will assume that there is a unique IDB that we identify as the target (or output) of the program. We use arity(P)to denote the maximum number of variables contained in any IDB of a program $P$ .

对于采用和积查询形式的每个Datalog程序，我们假定存在唯一被标识为程序目标（或输出）的IDB。用arity(P)表示程序$P$中任意IDB包含变量的最大数量。

Monadic,Linear &Chain Datalog. We say that a Datalog program $P$ is monadic if every IDB is unary (i.e. arity $\left( P\right)  = 1$ ); a Datalog program is linear if every sum-prod query in every rule contains at most one IDB (e.g., the program in Eq. (3) is linear). A chain query is a sum-prod query over binary predicates as follows:

一元、线性与链式Datalog。若程序中每个IDB均为一元（即元数$\left( P\right)  = 1$），则称该Datalog程序$P$为一元的；若每条规则中的每个和积查询至多含一个IDB（例如式(3)的程序），则称该程序为线性的。链式查询是建立在二元谓词上的和积查询，形式如下：

$$
\varphi \left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{k + 1}}\right)  : \;{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{\{ 2,\ldots ,k\} }}{T}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  \cdots  \otimes  {T}_{k}\left( {{x}_{k},{x}_{k + 1}}\right) .
$$

A chain Datalog program is a program where every rule consists of one or a sum of multiple chain queries. A chain Datalog program corresponds to a Context-Free Grammar (CFG) [50].

链式Datalog程序是指每条规则由一个或多个链式查询求和构成的程序。链式Datalog程序对应上下文无关文法(CFG)[50]。

Least Fixpoint Semantics. The least fixpoint semantics of a Datalog program $P$ over a semiring $\sigma$ is defined as the standard Datalog,through its immediate consequence operator (ICO). An ICO applies all rules in $P$ exactly once over a given instance and uses all derived facts (and the instance) for the next iteration. The naive evaluation algorithm iteratively applies ICO until a least fixpoint is reached (if any). In general,naive evaluation of Datalog over a semiring $\sigma$ may not converge, depending on $\sigma$ . Kleene’s Theorem [13] shows that if $\sigma$ is $\omega$ -continuous and the semiring $\sigma$ is $\omega$ - complete, then the naive evaluation converges to the least fixpoint. Following prior work [16, 24], we assume that $\sigma$ is $\omega$ -continuous and $\omega$ -complete.

最小不动点语义。半环$\sigma$上Datalog程序$P$的最小不动点语义通过其直接后果算子(ICO)定义为标准Datalog语义。ICO在给定实例上应用$P$中所有规则一次，并将所有派生事实（及该实例）用于下次迭代。朴素评估算法会迭代应用ICO直至达到最小不动点（若存在）。一般而言，半环$\sigma$上的Datalog朴素评估可能不收敛，这取决于$\sigma$。Kleene定理[13]表明，若$\sigma$是$\omega$-连续且半环$\sigma$是$\omega$-完备的，则朴素评估收敛于最小不动点。遵循先前研究[16,24]，我们假定$\sigma$满足$\omega$-连续性和$\omega$-完备性。

$\sigma$ -equivalent. Two Datalog programs $P,G$ are $\sigma$ -equivalent if for any input EDB instance annotated with elements of a semiring $\sigma$ ,the targets of $P$ and $G$ are identical $\sigma$ -relations when least fixpoints are reached for both.

$\sigma$等价性。两个Datalog程序$P,G$是$\sigma$等价的，当对于任何用半环$\sigma$元素标注的输入EDB实例，在达到最小不动点时，$P$和$G$的目标是相同的$\sigma$关系。

Parameters &Computational Model. We use $m$ to denote the sum of sizes of the input EDB $\sigma$ - relations to a Datalog program (henceforth referred to as the "input size is $m$ "). We use $n$ to denote the size of the active domain of EDB $\sigma$ -relations (i.e.,the number of distinct constants that occur in the input). If arity $\left( P\right)  = k$ ,then $n \leq  m \cdot  k$ . We assume data complexity [47],i.e. the program $P$ size (the total number of predicates and the variables) is a constant. We use the standard word-RAM model with $O\left( {\log m}\right)$ -bit words and unit-cost operations [10] for all complexity results. $\widetilde{O}$ hides poly-logarithmic factors in the size of the input.

参数与计算模型。我们用$m$表示Datalog程序输入EDB$\sigma$关系的大小总和（以下简称"输入大小为$m$"）。用$n$表示EDB$\sigma$关系的活跃域大小（即输入中出现的不同常量数量）。若元数$\left( P\right)  = k$，则$n \leq  m \cdot  k$。我们采用数据复杂度[47]假设，即程序$P$大小（谓词和变量的总数）是常数。所有复杂度结果均基于标准字RAM模型，采用$O\left( {\log m}\right)$位字长和单位成本操作[10]。$\widetilde{O}$隐藏了输入大小的多对数因子。

## 3 FRAMEWORK AND MAIN RESULTS

## 3 框架与主要成果

This section presents a general framework to analyze the data complexity of Datalog programs. We show how to use this framework to obtain both state-of-the-art and new algorithmic results.

本节提出分析Datalog程序数据复杂度的通用框架，展示如何运用该框架获得最先进算法成果及新发现。

A common technique to measure the runtime of a Datalog program is to multiply the number of iterations until the fixpoint is reached with the cost of each ICO evaluation. Although this method can show a polynomial bound in terms of data complexity, it cannot generate the tightest possible upper bound. Our proposed algorithmic framework allows us to decouple the semiring-dependent fixpoint computation from the structural properties of the Datalog program. It splits the computation into two distinct phases, where the first phase concerns the logical structure of the program, and the second the algebraic structure of the semiring.

测量Datalog程序运行时的常用技术是将达到不动点所需的迭代次数与每次ICO评估成本相乘。虽然该方法能证明数据复杂度的多项式界限，但无法生成最紧上界。我们提出的算法框架将半环依赖的不动点计算与程序结构特性解耦，将计算分为两个阶段：第一阶段关注程序的逻辑结构，第二阶段处理半环的代数结构。

Grounding Generation. In the first phase,the Datalog program is transformed into a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounded program, where each rule contains only constants. A grounding of a grounded Datalog program is a system of polynomial equations where we assign to each grounded EDB atom a distinct coefficient $e$ in the semiring and to each grounded IDB atom a distinct variable $x$ . Our goal in this phase is to construct a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding $G$ of the smallest possible size $\left| G\right|$ . The size of $G$ is measured as the total number of coefficients and variables in the system of polynomial equations.

基础生成。第一阶段将Datalog程序转换为$\sigma$等价的基础程序，其中每条规则仅含常量。基础Datalog程序的基化是多项式方程组，为每个基化EDB原子分配半环中的唯一系数$e$，为每个基化IDB原子分配唯一变量$x$。本阶段目标是构建$\sigma$等价的最小规模基化$G$，其大小由方程组中系数和变量总数衡量。

Grounding Evaluation. In the second phase, we need to deal with evaluating the least fixpoint of $G$ over the semiring $\sigma$ . The fixpoint computation now depends on the structure of the semiring $\sigma$ ,and we can completely ignore the underlying logical structure of the program. Here,we need to construct algorithms that are as fast as possible w.r.t. the size of the grounding $\left| G\right|$ . For example,it is known that over the Boolean semiring,the least fixpoint of $G$ can be computed in time $O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$ [22].

基化求值。第二阶段需计算半环$\sigma$上$G$的最小不动点。此时不动点计算取决于半环$\sigma$结构，可完全忽略程序的底层逻辑结构。需针对基化规模$\left| G\right|$设计最快算法。例如在布尔半环上，$G$的最小不动点可在$O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$时间内计算[22]。

### 3.1 Grounding Generation

### 3.1 基础生成

The first phase of the framework generates a grounded program.

框架首阶段生成基础程序。

Example 3.1. We will use as an example the following variation of program (3), over an arbitrary semiring $\sigma$ :

示例3.1. 我们以任意半环$\sigma$上的程序(3)变体为例：

$$
T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \oplus  \left( {{\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  U\left( {x}_{3}\right)  \otimes  R\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{2}}\right) }\right) .
$$

We introduced an unary atom $U$ to capture properties of the nodes in the graph. Consider an instance where $R$ contains two edges,(a,b)and(b,c),and $U$ contains three nodes, $a,b,c$ . One possible $\sigma$ -equivalent grounded Datalog program (over any semiring $\sigma$ ) is:

引入一元原子$U$捕捉图中节点属性。设实例中$R$含两条边(a,b)和(b,c)，$U$含三个节点$a,b,c$。其$\sigma$等价的基础Datalog程序（适用于任意半环$\sigma$）为：

$$
T\left( {a,b}\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {a,b}\right)  \oplus  T\left( {a,a}\right)  \otimes  U\left( a\right)  \otimes  R\left( {a,b}\right) ,
$$

$T\left( {a,a}\right)  \leftarrow  0$

$$
T\left( {a,c}\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( {a,b}\right)  \otimes  U\left( b\right)  \otimes  R\left( {b,c}\right) ,
$$

$$
T\left( {b,c}\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {b,c}\right) \text{.}
$$

This corresponds to the following system of polynomial equations:

对应多项式方程组如下：

$$
{x}_{ab} = {e}_{ab} \oplus  {x}_{aa} \otimes  {f}_{a} \otimes  {e}_{ab}
$$

$$
{x}_{aa} = 0
$$

$$
{x}_{ac} = {x}_{ab} \otimes  {f}_{b} \otimes  {e}_{bc}
$$

$$
{x}_{bc} = {e}_{bc}.
$$

表1. 基础化结果概要。$\widetilde{O}$符号隐藏了$m$（总输入规模）和$n$（活跃域大小）的多对数因子。$k$表示程序元数。fhw、subw定义见第6节。

<!-- Media -->

As there is a one-to-one correspondence between a grounded program and its grounding, we will only use the term grounding from now on. The naive way to generate a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounded program is to take every rule and replace the variables with all possible constants. However, this may create a grounding of very large size. Our key idea is that we can optimize the size of the generated grounding by exploiting the logical structure of the rules.

由于基础程序与其基础化之间存在一一对应关系，下文将统一使用"基础化"这一术语。生成$\sigma$等价基础程序的朴素方法是将所有规则中的变量替换为所有可能的常量，但这可能导致基础化规模过大。我们的核心思想是通过利用规则的逻辑结构来优化生成的基础化规模。

Upper Bounds. Our first main result (Section 5) considers the body of every sum-product query in the Datalog program is acyclic (formally Definition 5); we call such a program rulewise-acyclic

上界。我们的第一个主要结果（第5节）要求Datalog程序中每个求和-乘积查询的体部都是无环的（正式定义见定义5），这类程序称为规则无环程序。

THEOREM 3.2. Let $P$ be a rulewise-acyclic Datalog program over some semiring $\sigma$ ,with input size $m$ ,active domain size $n$ ,and arity $\left( P\right)  \leq  k$ . Then,we can construct a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding in time (and has size) $O\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right) }\right)$ .

定理3.2。设$P$是定义在半环$\sigma$上的规则无环Datalog程序，其输入规模为$m$，活跃域大小为$n$，元数为$\left( P\right)  \leq  k$。那么我们可在$O\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right) }\right)$时间内（且生成规模为）构造出$\sigma$等价基础化。

A direct result of the above theorem is that for monadic Datalog,where $\operatorname{arity}\left( P\right)  = 1$ ,we obtain a grounding of size $O\left( m\right)$ ,which is essentially optimal. The main technical idea behind Theorem 3.2 is to construct the join tree corresponding to each rule, and then decompose the rules following the structure of the join tree.

该定理的直接推论是：对于元数$\operatorname{arity}\left( P\right)  = 1$的单态Datalog程序，我们能获得规模为$O\left( m\right)$的基础化，这本质上是最优的。定理3.2背后的核心技术是为每个规则构建对应的连接树，并沿连接树结构分解规则。

It turns out that, in analogy to conjunctive query evaluation, we can also get good bounds on the size of the grounding by considering a width measure of tree decompositions of the rules. We show in Section 6 the following theorem, which relates the grounding size to the maximum submodular width (Section 6) across all rules.

与合取查询评估类似，通过考虑规则的树分解宽度度量，我们也能获得基础化规模的良好上界。第6节将证明以下定理，该定理将基础化规模与所有规则的最大子模宽度（第6节）相关联。

THEOREM 3.3. Let $P$ be a Datalog program where arity $\left( P\right)  \leq  k$ ,subw is the maximum submodular width across all rules of $P$ ,and $\sigma$ is a dioid and suppose the input size is $m$ ,and the active domain size is n. Then,we can construct a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding in time (and has size) $\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right) }\right)$ .

定理3.3。设$P$是元数为$\left( P\right)  \leq  k$的Datalog程序，subw表示所有规则的最大子模宽度，$\sigma$为双环且输入规模为$m$，活跃域大小为n。那么我们可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right) }\right)$时间内（且生成规模为）构造出$\sigma$等价基础化。

The above theorem requires the semiring to be idempotent w.r.t. the $\oplus$ operation (i.e. a dioid). For a general semiring, the best we show is that we can replace subw with the weaker notion of fractional hypertree width (Proposition 6.4). Sections 5 and 6 show refined constructions that improve the grounding size when the Datalog program has additional structure (e.g., linear rules); we summarize these results in Table 1.

上述定理要求半环对$\oplus$运算满足幂等性（即双环）。对于一般半环，我们证明可用更弱的分数超树宽度替代subw（命题6.4）。第5、6节展示了当Datalog程序具有附加结构（如线性规则）时改进基础化规模的优化构造，表1汇总了这些结果。

Lower Bounds. One natural question is: do Theorem 3.2 and 3.3 attain the best possible grounding bounds? This is unlikely to be true for specific Datalog fragments (e.g. linear Datalog has a tighter grounding as shown in Proposition 6.3); however, we can show optimality for a class of programs (see Appendix D for the proof).

下界。一个自然问题是：定理3.2和3.3是否达到了最佳可能的基础化边界？虽然特定Datalog片段（如命题6.3所示线性Datalog）可能有更紧致的边界，但我们可以证明某类程序的最优性（证明见附录D）。

THEOREM 3.4. Take any integer $k \geq  2$ and any rational number $w \geq  1$ such that $k \cdot  w$ is an integer. There exists a (non-linear) Datalog program $P$ over the tropical semiring ${\operatorname{Trop}}^{ + }$ with arity $\left( P\right)  \leq  k$ , such that:

定理3.4。任取整数$k \geq  2$和有理数$w \geq  1$使得$k \cdot  w$为整数。存在定义在热带半环${\operatorname{Trop}}^{ + }$上、元数为$\left( P\right)  \leq  k$的（非线性）Datalog程序$P$满足：

(1) subw $\left( P\right)  = w$ ,

(1) 子模宽度subw$\left( P\right)  = w$，

(2) any ${\operatorname{Trop}}^{ + }$ -equivalent grounding has size $\Omega \left( {n}^{k - 1 + {kw}}\right)$ ,

(2) 任何${\operatorname{Trop}}^{ + }$等价基础化规模为$\Omega \left( {n}^{k - 1 + {kw}}\right)$，

(3) under the min-weight $\ell$ -Clique hypothesis [33],no algorithm that evaluates $P$ has a runtime of $O\left( {n}^{k - 1 + {kw} - o\left( 1\right) }\right)$ .

(3) 在最小权重$\ell$-Clique假设[33]下，不存在运行时间为$O\left( {n}^{k - 1 + {kw} - o\left( 1\right) }\right)$的$P$评估算法。

### 3.2 Grounding Evaluation

### 3.2 基础化评估

Given a grounding $G$ for a Datalog program,we now turn to the problem of computing the (least fixpoint) solution for this grounding. In particular,we study how fast can we evaluate $G$ under different types of semirings as a function of its size. Ideally, we would like to have an algorithm that computes the fixpoint using $O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$ semiring operations; however,this may not be possible in general. In Section 4, we will show fast evaluation strategies for two different classes of semirings: (i) semirings of finite rank, and (ii) semirings that are totally ordered and absorptive. Our two main results can be stated as follows.

给定一个Datalog程序的基例$G$，我们现在转向计算该基例的（最小不动点）解的问题。具体而言，我们研究在不同类型的半环下，如何能以其规模为函数快速评估$G$。理想情况下，我们希望有一种算法，使用$O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$次半环运算来计算不动点；然而，这在一般情况下可能无法实现。在第4节中，我们将展示针对两类半环的快速评估策略：（i）有限秩半环，以及（ii）全序且吸收的半环。我们的两个主要结果可表述如下。

THEOREM 3.5. We can evaluate a grounding $G$ over any semiring of rank $r$ using $O\left( {r \cdot  \left| G\right| }\right)$ semiring operations.

定理3.5. 我们可以在任何秩为$r$的半环上，使用$O\left( {r \cdot  \left| G\right| }\right)$次半环运算来评估基例$G$。

THEOREM 3.6. We can evaluate a grounding $G$ over any absorptive semiring with total order using $O\left( {\left| G\right| \log \left| G\right| }\right)$ semiring operations.

定理3.6. 我们可以在任何全序吸收半环上，使用$O\left( {\left| G\right| \log \left| G\right| }\right)$次半环运算来评估基例$G$。

Many semirings of practical interest are captured by the above two classes. The Boolean semiring in particular is a semiring of rank 1. Hence, we obtain as a direct corollary the result in [22]:

许多实际应用中的半环都包含在上述两类中。特别地，布尔半环是秩为1的半环。因此，我们直接得出文献[22]中的结果：

COROLLARY 3.7. We can evaluate a grounding $G$ over the Boolean semiring in time $O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$ .

推论3.7. 我们可以在$O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$时间内评估布尔半环上的基例$G$。

The set semiring $\left( {{2}^{K},\cup ,\cap ,\varnothing ,K}\right)$ for a finite set $K$ has rank $\left| K\right|$ . In fact,all bounded distributive lattices have constant rank. As another example,the access control semiring $(\{ P,C,S,T,0\}$ ,min, max, $0,P)\left\lbrack  {19}\right\rbrack$ employs a constant number of security classifications,where $P =$ PUBLIC, $C =$ CONFIDENTIAL, $S =$ SECRET, $T =$ TOP-SECRET and $P \subset  C \subset  S \subset  T \subset  0$ is the total order for levels of clearance. For all these semirings,their grounding can be evaluated in time $O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$ by Theorem 3.5. The class of absorptive semiring with total order contains ${\mathrm{{Trop}}}^{ + }$ that has infinite rank. Hence Theorem 3.5 cannot be applied,but by Theorem 3.6,we can evaluate $G$ over ${\operatorname{Trop}}^{ + }$ in time $O\left( {\left| G\right| \log \left| G\right| }\right)$ .

对于有限集合$K$的集合半环$\left( {{2}^{K},\cup ,\cap ,\varnothing ,K}\right)$，其秩为$\left| K\right|$。事实上，所有有界分配格都具有恒定秩。另一个例子是访问控制半环$(\{ P,C,S,T,0\}$，它采用恒定数量的安全分类，其中$P =$为公开，$C =$为机密，$S =$为秘密，$T =$为绝密，且$P \subset  C \subset  S \subset  T \subset  0$是权限级别的全序。对于所有这些半环，根据定理3.5，它们的基例可在$O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$时间内评估。具有全序的吸收半环类包含秩无限的${\mathrm{{Trop}}}^{ + }$。因此定理3.5不适用，但根据定理3.6，我们可以在$O\left( {\left| G\right| \log \left| G\right| }\right)$时间内评估${\operatorname{Trop}}^{ + }$上的$G$。

### 3.3 Applications

### 3.3 应用

Finally, we discuss algorithmic implications of our general framework. In particular, we demonstrate that our approach captures as special cases several state-of-the-art algorithms for tasks that can be described in Datalog. Table 2 summarizes some of our results that are straightforward applications of our framework.

最后，我们讨论我们通用框架的算法意义。特别是，我们展示了我们的方法作为特例涵盖了Datalog中可描述的若干最先进算法。表2总结了直接应用我们框架的一些结果。

To highlight some of our results, Dikjstra's algorithm for single-source shortest path is a special case of applying Theorem 3.6 after we compute the grounding of the program. As another example, Yannakakis [50] showed that a binary (i.e. EDB/IDB arities are at most 2) rulewise-acyclic programs can be evaluated in time $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ . By Theorem 3.2 (let $k = 2$ ),an equivalent grounding of size $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ can be constructed in time $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ for such programs. Then by Corollary 3.7,we can evaluate the original program in $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ time,thus recovering the result of Yannakakis.

重点介绍部分结果：在计算程序的基例后，Dijkstra的单源最短路径算法是应用定理3.6的一个特例。另一个例子是，Yannakakis[50]证明了二元（即EDB/IDB元数最多为2）规则无环程序可在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内评估。根据定理3.2（设$k = 2$），此类程序可在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内构造大小为$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$的等价基例。然后根据推论3.7，我们可在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内评估原程序，从而重现Yannakakis的结果。

If the binary rulewise-acyclic Datalog program is also linear,it can be a evaluated in $O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ time [50]. We generalize this result to show that the $O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ bound holds for any linear rulewise-acyclic Datalog program with IDB arity at most 2, even if the EDB relations are of higher arity (see Table 2, Proposition 5.4).

若该二元规则非循环Datalog程序同时满足线性条件，则其可在$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$时间内完成求值[50]。我们将此结论推广至证明：对于IDB元数不超过2的任意线性规则非循环Datalog程序，即使EDB关系具有更高元数，$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$时间复杂度上界依然成立（参见表2，命题5.4）。

As another corollary of Theorem 3.3 and Corollary 3.7, monadic Datalog can be evaluated in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{\text{subw }}\right)$ . This is surprising in our opinion,since $\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{\text{subw }}\right)$ is the best known runtime for Boolean CQs. Hence, this result tells us that the addition of recursion with unary IDB does not really add to the runtime of evaluation!

作为定理3.3与推论3.7的另一推论，一元Datalog可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{\text{subw }}\right)$时间内完成求值。这一发现令人惊讶，因为$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{\text{subw }}\right)$是目前布尔CQs的最佳已知运行时。因此该结果表明：引入一元IDB递归机制实际上并未增加求值运行时开销！

## 4 GROUNDING EVALUATION

## 4 基础求值

This section presents algorithms for evaluating a grounding over two types of commonly-used semirings $\left\lbrack  {{28},{39},{40}}\right\rbrack$ . First, Section 4.1 presents a procedure that transforms the grounding to one with a more amenable structure, called a 2-canonical grounding. Then, we present evaluation algorithms for semirings of rank $r$ (Section 4.2),and for absoprtive semirings that are totally ordered (Section 4.3).

本节提出在两类常用半环$\left\lbrack  {{28},{39},{40}}\right\rbrack$上进行基础求值的算法。首先，第4.1节介绍将基础转换为具有更优结构（称为2-规范基础）的预处理流程。随后分别给出秩为$r$的半环求值算法（第4.2节）及全序吸收半环的求值算法（第4.3节）。

### 4.1 2-canonical Grounding

### 4.1 2-规范基础

We say that a grounding $G$ is 2-canonical if every equation in $G$ is of the form $y = x \oplus  z$ or $y = x \otimes  z$ . As a first step of our evaluation, we first transform the given grounding into a 2-canonical form using the following Lemma 4.1.

称基础$G$为2-规范的，若其中每个方程均具有$y = x \oplus  z$或$y = x \otimes  z$形式。作为求值第一步，我们首先借助下述引理4.1将给定基础转换为2-规范形式。

LEMMA 4.1. A grounding $G$ can be transformed into a $\sigma$ -equivalent 2-canonical grounding of size at most $4\left| G\right|$ in time $O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$ .

引理4.1. 基础$G$可在$O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$时间内转换为规模不超过$4\left| G\right|$的$\sigma$等价2-规范基础。

Proof. The construction works in two steps. In the first step, we rewrite all monomials. Consider a monomial $\mathbf{m} = {x}_{1} \otimes  {x}_{2} \otimes  \ldots {x}_{n}$ . If $n = 1$ ,then simply let $\mathbf{m} = {x}_{1} \otimes  \mathbf{1}$ . Otherwise $\left( {n \geq  2}\right)$ ,we rewrite $\mathbf{m}$ as follows. We introduce $n - 1$ new variables ${y}_{1},\ldots ,{y}_{n - 1}$ ,replace $\mathbf{m}$ with ${y}_{n - 1}$ and add the following equations in the system:

证明. 构造过程分两步。第一步重写所有单项式。考虑单项式$\mathbf{m} = {x}_{1} \otimes  {x}_{2} \otimes  \ldots {x}_{n}$。若$n = 1$，则直接令$\mathbf{m} = {x}_{1} \otimes  \mathbf{1}$；否则$\left( {n \geq  2}\right)$时，按如下方式重写$\mathbf{m}$：引入$n - 1$个新变量${y}_{1},\ldots ,{y}_{n - 1}$，将$\mathbf{m}$替换为${y}_{n - 1}$并在系统中添加下列方程：

$$
{y}_{1} = {x}_{1} \otimes  {x}_{2},\;{y}_{2} = {y}_{1} \otimes  {x}_{3},\;\ldots \;{y}_{n - 1} = {y}_{n - 2} \otimes  {x}_{n}
$$

<!-- Media -->

Table 2. Summary of runtime results. The $\widetilde{O}$ notation hides polylog factors in $m$ (total input size) and $n$ (size of the active domain). $k$ denotes the arity of the program. See Section 6 for the definition of subw.

表2. 运行时结果概要。记号$\widetilde{O}$隐藏了$m$（总输入规模）与$n$（活跃域规模）中的多对数因子。$k$表示程序元数。子线宽定义见第6节。

<table><tbody><tr><td>任务</td><td>半环(Semiring)</td><td>程序</td><td>运行时</td></tr><tr><td>全对最短路径(APSP) $\left\lbrack  {{17},{49}}\right\rbrack$</td><td>热带(Tropical)</td><td>$T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \oplus$ ${\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  R\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{3}}\right)$</td><td>$\widetilde{O}\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$</td></tr><tr><td>单源最短路径(SSSP) [15]</td><td>热带(Tropical)</td><td>$T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  U\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \oplus$ ${\bigoplus }_{{x}_{2}}T\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \otimes  R\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{1}}\right)$</td><td>$\widetilde{O}\left( m\right)$</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">上下文无关语言可达性(CFL reachability) [50]</td><td>布尔(Boolean)</td><td>链式程序</td><td>$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$</td></tr><tr><td>布尔(Boolean)</td><td>IDB元数$\leq  2\&$ 规则无环</td><td>$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$</td></tr><tr><td>正则路径查询全对 [6, 9]</td><td>布尔(Boolean)</td><td>线性链式程序</td><td>$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$</td></tr><tr><td>上下文无关语言全对最短路径(CFL-APSP) [46, 50]</td><td>热带(Tropical)</td><td>链式程序</td><td>$\widetilde{O}\left( {n}^{3}\right)$</td></tr><tr><td>一元无环Datalog $\left\lbrack  {{21},{22}}\right\rbrack$</td><td>布尔(Boolean)</td><td>一元规则无环</td><td>$O\left( m\right)$</td></tr><tr><td>一元数据 $\log \left\lbrack  {{21},{22}}\right\rbrack$</td><td>布尔(Boolean)</td><td>一元</td><td>$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{subw}\right)$</td></tr><tr><td>线性Datalog [25]</td><td>布尔(Boolean)</td><td>IDB元数 $\leq  k$</td><td>$\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1}{m}^{\text{subw} - 1} \cdot  \left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right) }\right)$</td></tr><tr><td>Datalog</td><td>布尔(Boolean)</td><td>IDB元数 $\leq  k$</td><td>$\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right) }\right)$</td></tr></tbody></table>

<!-- Media -->

This transformation increases the size of the system by at most a factor of two. Indeed, the monomial contributes $n$ to $\left| G\right|$ ,and the new equations contribute $1 + 2\left( {n - 2}\right)  \leq  {2n}$ .

该转换最多使系统规模扩大至两倍。实际上，单项式对$n$贡献了$\left| G\right|$，而新方程贡献了$1 + 2\left( {n - 2}\right)  \leq  {2n}$。

After the first step, we are left with equations that are either a product of two elements, or a sum of the form $x = {x}_{1} \oplus  {x}_{2} \oplus  \ldots {x}_{n}$ (w.l.o.g.,we can assume no equations of the form $x = y$ ). Here, we apply the same idea as above, replacing multiplication with addition. This transformation will increase the size of the system by another factor of two using the same argument as before. The equivalence of the new grounding follows from the associativity property of both $\oplus  , \otimes$ .

第一步处理后，剩下的方程要么是两个元素的乘积，要么是形如$x = {x}_{1} \oplus  {x}_{2} \oplus  \ldots {x}_{n}$的和式（不失一般性，可假设不存在$x = y$形式的方程）。此处采用与前述相同的思路，用加法替代乘法。根据相同论证，该转换将使系统规模再次扩大两倍。新基础的等价性源于$\oplus  , \otimes$的结合律性质。

We demostrate Lemma 4.1 with an example.

我们通过示例演示引理4.1。

Example 4.2. Consider the polynomial equation showed up in Section 3: ${x}_{ab} = {e}_{ab} \oplus  {x}_{aa} \otimes  {f}_{a} \otimes  {e}_{ab}$ . Its rewriting (via substitutions) following the proof of Lemma 4.1 is:

例4.2. 考虑第3节出现的多项式方程：${x}_{ab} = {e}_{ab} \oplus  {x}_{aa} \otimes  {f}_{a} \otimes  {e}_{ab}$。根据引理4.1证明过程进行（通过替换）重写后得到：

$$
{x}_{ab} = {e}_{ab} \oplus  {y}_{1},\;{y}_{1} = {x}_{aa} \otimes  {y}_{2},\;{y}_{2} = {f}_{a} \otimes  {e}_{ab}
$$

Here ${y}_{1},{y}_{2}$ are new IDB variables introduced to make the system of polynomial equations 2- canonical.

此处${y}_{1},{y}_{2}$是为使多项式方程组成为2-正则而引入的新IDB变量。

### 4.2 Finite-rank Semirings

### 4.2 有限秩半环

We now present a grounding evaluation algorithm (Algorithm 1) over a semiring of rank $r$ ,which is used to prove Theorem 3.5.

现在我们提出在秩为$r$的半环上的基础求值算法（算法1），该算法用于证明定理3.5。

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 1: Grounding evaluation over a rank $r$ semiring

算法1：秩$r$半环上的基础求值

---

	Input : grounding $G$

	输入：基础$G$

	transform $G$ to be 2-canonical via Lemma 4.1

	通过引理4.1将$G$转换为2-正则形式

2 construct a hash table $E$ ,such that for every variable $x$ in $G,E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$ is the set of all equations that

2 构建哈希表$E$，使得对于$G,E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$中的每个变量$x$，其值为所有在右侧包含

		contain $x$ in the right-hand side ; init an empty queue $q$

		$x$的方程集合；初始化空队列$q$

	foreach EDB variables $e$ in $G$ do $h\left( e\right)  \leftarrow  e$ ;

	对于$G$中的每个EDB变量$e$，执行$h\left( e\right)  \leftarrow  e$；

	foreach IDB variables $x$ in $G$ do $h\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  0$ ;

	对于$G$中的每个IDB变量$x$，执行$h\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  0$；

	foreach EDB variables $e$ in $G$ do insert $e$ into $q$ ;

	对于$G$中的每个EDB变量$e$，将$e$插入$q$；

	while $q$ is not empty do

	当$q$非空时执行

				pop $x$ from $q$

				从$q$弹出$x$

				forall $\left( {y = x \circledast  z}\right)  \in  E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$ do // $\circledast   \in  \{  \oplus  , \otimes  \}$

				对全部$\left( {y = x \circledast  z}\right)  \in  E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$执行 // $\circledast   \in  \{  \oplus  , \otimes  \}$

							if $h\left( y\right)  \neq  h\left( x\right)  \circledast  h\left( z\right)$ then

													若$h\left( y\right)  \neq  h\left( x\right)  \circledast  h\left( z\right)$则

										$h\left( y\right)  \leftarrow  h\left( x\right)  \circledast  h\left( z\right)$ insert $y$ into $q$

																			将$y$插入$q$的$h\left( y\right)  \leftarrow  h\left( x\right)  \circledast  h\left( z\right)$

	return $h\left( \cdot \right)$

	返回$h\left( \cdot \right)$

---

<!-- Media -->

The key idea of the algorithm is to compute the least fixpoint in a fine-grained way. Instead of updating all equations in every iteration, we will carefully choose a subset of equations to update their left-hand side variables. In particular, at every step we will pick a new variable (Line 7) and then only update the equations that contain this variable ${}^{2}$ (Line 8-10). Because the semiring has rank $r$ ,the value of each variable cannot be updated more than $r$ times; hence,we are guaranteed that each equation will be visited only $2 \cdot  r$ times. Moreover,since the grounding is 2-canonical, updating each variable needs only one $\oplus$ or $\otimes$ operation; the latter property would not be possible if we had not previously transformed the grounding into a 2-canonical one.

该算法的核心思想是以细粒度方式计算最小不动点。与每次迭代更新所有方程不同，我们会谨慎选择方程子集来更新其左侧变量。具体而言，在每一步我们会选取一个新变量（第7行），然后仅更新包含该变量的方程（第8-10行）。由于半环的秩为$r$，每个变量的值最多只能更新$r$次；因此可以确保每个方程最多被访问$2 \cdot  r$次。此外，由于基础是2-规范的，更新每个变量仅需一次$\oplus$或$\otimes$运算；若先前未将基础转化为2-规范形式，则无法实现后一特性。

### 4.3 Absorptive Semirings with Total Order

### 4.3 全序吸收半环

We present a Dijkstra-style algorithm (Algorithm 2) for grounding evaluation over an absorptive semiring with $\sqsubseteq$ being a total order to prove Theorem 3.6. It builds upon prior work [39] by further optimizing the original algorithm to achieve the almost-linear runtime.

我们提出了一种Dijkstra风格算法（算法2），用于在全序吸收半环$\sqsubseteq$上进行基础求值以证明定理3.6。该算法基于前人工作[39]，通过进一步优化原始算法实现近线性时间复杂度。

The algorithm follows the same idea as Algorithm 1 by carefully updating only a subset of equations at every step. However there are two key differences. First, while Algorithm 1 is agnostic to the order in which the newly updated variables are propagated to the equations (hence the use of a queue), Algorithm 2 needs to always pick the variable with the current maximum value w.r.t. the total order $\sqsubseteq$ . To achieve this,we need to use a priority queue instead of a queue,which is the reason of the additional logarithmic factor in the runtime. The second difference is that once a variable is updated once, it gets "frozen" and never gets updated again (see Lines 10-11). We show in the detailed proof of correctness (Appendix B) that it is safe to do this and still reach the desired fixpoint.

本算法延续算法1的核心思想，在每步仅谨慎更新方程子集。但存在两个关键差异：其一，算法1不关心新更新变量传播至方程的次序（故使用队列），而算法2需始终选择当前相对于全序$\sqsubseteq$具有最大值的变量。为此需改用优先队列，这导致运行时间中额外的对数因子。其二，变量一旦被更新就会"冻结"且不再更新（见第10-11行）。我们在正确性证明详述（附录B）中表明这种做法是安全的，仍能达到预期不动点。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{2}$ We assume that look-ups,inserts,and deletes on hash tables cost constant time. In practice,hashing can only achieve amortized constant time. Therefore, whenever we claim constant time for hash operations, we mean amortized constant time.

${}^{2}$ 我们假设哈希表的查找、插入和删除操作均为常数时间复杂度。实际应用中哈希只能实现均摊常数时间。因此当我们声称哈希操作是常数时间时，指的是均摊常数时间。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

Algorithm 2: Grounding evaluation over an absorptive semiring with total order

算法2：全序吸收半环上的基础求值

---

		Input : a grounding $G$

		输入：基础$G$

		transform $G$ to be 2-canonical via Lemma 4.1

		通过引理4.1将$G$转化为2-规范形式

2 construct a hash table $E$ ,such that for every variable $x$ in $G,E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$ is the set of all equations that

2 构建哈希表$E$，使得对于基础$G,E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$中的每个变量$x$，该表存储所有在右侧包含$E$的方程集合

			contain $x$ in the right-hand side ; init $\mathcal{F} \leftarrow  \varnothing$

			初始化$\mathcal{F} \leftarrow  \varnothing$包含$x$

		foreach EDB variables $e$ in $G$ do $h\left( e\right)  \leftarrow  e$ ;

		对$G$中的每个EDB变量$e$执行$h\left( e\right)  \leftarrow  e$

		foreach IDB variables $x$ in $G$ do $h\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  0$ ;

		对$G$中的每个IDB变量$x$执行$h\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  0$；

	init an empty priority queue $q$ of decreasing values w.r.t. $\sqsupseteq$

	初始化一个关于$\sqsupseteq$的递减值优先队列$q$

		forall IDB variables $x = y \circledast  z$ in $G$ do // $\circledast   \in  \{  \oplus  , \otimes  \}$

		遍历$G$中的所有IDB变量$x = y \circledast  z$ // $\circledast   \in  \{  \oplus  , \otimes  \}$

					$h\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  h\left( y\right)  \circledast  h\left( z\right)$ ; insert $x$ into $q$ of value $h\left( x\right)$

					$h\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  h\left( y\right)  \circledast  h\left( z\right)$；将$x$按值$h\left( x\right)$插入$q$

		while $q$ is non-empty do

			当$q$非空时循环执行

					pop a variable $x$ of the max value from $q$

					从$q$弹出最大值变量$x$

					$\mathcal{F} \leftarrow  \mathcal{F} \cup  \{ x\}$

					forall $\left( {y = x \circledast  z}\right)  \in  E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack   \land  y \notin  \mathcal{F}$ do

					对所有$\left( {y = x \circledast  z}\right)  \in  E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack   \land  y \notin  \mathcal{F}$执行

								if $h\left( y\right)  \neq  h\left( x\right)  \circledast  h\left( z\right)$ then

															若$h\left( y\right)  \neq  h\left( x\right)  \circledast  h\left( z\right)$成立则

											$h\left( y\right)  \leftarrow  h\left( x\right)  \circledast  h\left( z\right)$

												insert $y$ into $q$ of value $h\left( y\right)$

																							将$y$按值$h\left( y\right)$插入$q$

		return $h\left( \cdot \right)$

			返回$h\left( \cdot \right)$

## 5 GROUNDING OF ACYCLIC DATALOG

## 5 非循环DATALOG的接地实现

In this section, we study how to find an efficient grounding (in terms of space usage and time required) of a Datalog program over a semiring $\sigma$ . First,we introduce rulewise-acyclicity of a program using the notion of tree decompositions.

本节研究如何在半环$\sigma$上找到空间和时间高效的Datalog程序接地方案。首先，我们利用树分解概念引入程序的规则级非循环性。

Example 5.1. We ground the APSP program (3). First, $R\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ produces $O\left( \left| R\right| \right)$ groundings, since it is an EDB. The latter sum-prod query has a $O\left( {\left| R\right|  \cdot  n}\right)$ grounding,since for each tuple $R\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{2}}\right)$ ,we should consider all active domain of ${x}_{1}$ . Hence,the equivalent grounding is of size $O\left( {\left| R\right|  + \left| R\right|  \cdot  n}\right)  = O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right) .$

示例5.1 我们对接地APSP程序(3)。由于是EDB，$R\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$首先生成$O\left( \left| R\right| \right)$个接地实例。该求和-积查询具有$O\left( {\left| R\right|  \cdot  n}\right)$接地规模，因为对于每个元组$R\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{2}}\right)$都需要考虑${x}_{1}$的活跃域，因此等效接地规模为$O\left( {\left| R\right|  + \left| R\right|  \cdot  n}\right)  = O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right) .$

Tree Decompositions. Let $\varphi$ be a sum-prod query (5) and $\left( {\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$ be its associated hypergraph. A tree decomposition of $\varphi$ is a tuple $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ where $\left( i\right) \mathcal{T}$ is an undirected tree,and $\left( {ii}\right) \chi$ is a mapping that assigns to every node $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ a set of variables $\chi \left( t\right)  \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ ,called the bag of $t$ ,such that

树分解。设$\varphi$为求和-积查询(5)，$\left( {\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$为其关联超图。$\varphi$的树分解是元组$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，其中$\left( i\right) \mathcal{T}$是无向树，$\left( {ii}\right) \chi$是将变量集$\chi \left( t\right)  \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$（称为$t$的包）分配给每个节点$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$的映射，满足：

(1) every hyperedge $J \in  \mathcal{E}$ is a subset of some $\chi \left( t\right) ,t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ ;

(1) 每条超边$J \in  \mathcal{E}$都是某个$\chi \left( t\right) ,t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$的子集；

(2) (running intersection property) for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ ,the set $\{ t \mid  i \in  \chi \left( t\right) \}$ is a non-empty (connected) sub-tree of $\mathcal{T}$ .

(2) (运行交集性质) 对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$，集合$\{ t \mid  i \in  \chi \left( t\right) \}$构成$\mathcal{T}$的非空连通子树。

A tree decomposition is a join tree if $\chi \left( t\right)  \in  \mathcal{E}$ for all $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ .

若对所有$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$满足$\chi \left( t\right)  \in  \mathcal{E}$，则树分解称为连接树。

Acyclicity. A sum-prod query $\varphi$ is said to be acyclic if its associated hypergraph admits a join tree. The GYO reduction [51] is a well-known method to construct a join tree for $\varphi$ . We say that a rule of a Datalog program is acyclic if every sum-prod query in its body is acyclic and a program is rulewise-acyclic if it has only acyclic rules.

无环性。当且仅当关联超图存在连接树时，和积查询$\varphi$被称为无环的。GYO归约法[51]是构建$\varphi$连接树的经典方法。若Datalog程序中某规则体内所有和积查询均无环，则该规则为无环规则；若程序仅含无环规则，则称其具有规则级无环性。

We formally prove Theorem 3.2, by introducing a grounding algorithm for rulewise-acyclic programs attaining the desired bounds; the pseudocode is in Algorithm 3 in the appendix.

我们通过引入满足目标复杂度界限的规则无环程序接地算法，严格证明了定理3.2，伪代码详见附录算法3。

Proof of THEOREM 3.2. The $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding $G$ is constructed rule-wise from $P$ . Take any acyclic rule from $P$ with head $T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ . Note that its arity $\left| H\right|  \leq  k$ . We ground each sum-prod query $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ in this rule one by one. To ground a single $\varphi$ (which is of the form (5)),we construct a join tree $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ of $\varphi$ . Each bag in the join tree will correspond to an atom in the body of $\varphi$ . We root $\mathcal{T}$ from any atom that contains at least one of the variables in ${\mathbf{x}}_{H}$ (i.e. head variables),say ${s}_{0}$ . This orients the join tree. For any node $s$ ,define $\left( {{V}_{s},{\mathcal{E}}_{s}}\right)$ ,where ${V}_{s} \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,{\mathcal{E}}_{s} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ ,to be the hypergraph constructed by only taking the bags from the subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ rooted at $t$ as hyperedges. Note that $\left( {{V}_{{s}_{0}},{\mathcal{E}}_{{s}_{0}}}\right)  = \left( {\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is the associated hypergraph of $\varphi$ . Let ${H}_{s} \subseteq  H$ be the head variables that occur in this subtree (so at root, ${H}_{{s}_{0}} = H$ ). Further,rename the head atom $T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ to be ${U}_{{e}_{\left(  \bot  ,{s}_{0}\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ ,with $\bot$ being an imaginary parent node of the root $s$ and ${e}_{\left(  \bot  ,{s}_{0}\right) } = H$ .

定理3.2证明。从$P$逐规则构建$\sigma$等价接地$G$。任取$P$中头部为$T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$的无环规则（其元数为$\left| H\right|  \leq  k$），依次对该规则内每个和积查询$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$进行接地处理。对于形如(5)式的$\varphi$，先构建其连接树$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$——连接树中每个包对应$\varphi$体部一个原子。从包含${\mathbf{x}}_{H}$（即头部变量）中任一变量的原子（如${s}_{0}$）开始扎根$\mathcal{T}$，从而定向连接树。对任意节点$s$，定义$\left( {{V}_{s},{\mathcal{E}}_{s}}\right)$（其中${V}_{s} \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,{\mathcal{E}}_{s} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$）为仅取$\mathcal{T}$以$t$为根子树中包所构成的超图。注意$\left( {{V}_{{s}_{0}},{\mathcal{E}}_{{s}_{0}}}\right)  = \left( {\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$即为$\varphi$的关联超图。令${H}_{s} \subseteq  H$表示该子树出现的头部变量（根节点处${H}_{{s}_{0}} = H$）。最后将头部原子$T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$重命名为${U}_{{e}_{\left(  \bot  ,{s}_{0}\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$，其中$\bot$作为根节点$s$的虚拟父节点，且${e}_{\left(  \bot  ,{s}_{0}\right) } = H$。

Next,we use a recursive method GROUND to ground $\varphi$ . Let $r$ be the parent of $s$ in $\mathcal{T}$ . A GROUND $\left( {s,{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}\right)$ call at a node $s$ grounds the sum-prod query

接着采用递归方法GROUND对$\varphi$进行接地。设$r$是$\mathcal{T}$中$s$的父节点，在节点$s$处调用GROUND$\left( {s,{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}\right)$将对和积查询进行接地处理

$$
{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{s} \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}{\bigotimes }_{J \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{s}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right) . \tag{6}
$$

Hence,calling GROUND $\left( {{s}_{0},{U}_{{e}_{\left(  \bot  ,{s}_{0}\right) }}}\right)$ at ${s}_{0}$ suffices to ground $\varphi$ . We describe the procedure GROUND $\left( {s,{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}\right)$ , which has three steps:

因此在${s}_{0}$处调用GROUND$\left( {{s}_{0},{U}_{{e}_{\left(  \bot  ,{s}_{0}\right) }}}\right)$即可完成$\varphi$接地。GROUND$\left( {s,{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}\right)$过程包含三个步骤：

(i) Refactor. Define for each child $t$ of $s$ (i.e. $\left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) ,E\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ being the directed edges of $\mathcal{T}$ ), ${e}_{\left( s,t\right) } = \left( {\chi \left( s\right)  \cap  \chi \left( t\right) }\right)  \cup  {H}_{t}$ and

(i) 重构。为$s$的每个子节点$t$（即$\mathcal{T}$的有向边$\left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) ,E\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$）定义${e}_{\left( s,t\right) } = \left( {\chi \left( s\right)  \cap  \chi \left( t\right) }\right)  \cup  {H}_{t}$，并

$$
S = \chi \left( s\right)  \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }.
$$

We refactor as follows: ${U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{{V}_{s} \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}{\bigotimes }_{J \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{s}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$

我们按如下方式重构：${U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{{V}_{s} \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}{\bigotimes }_{J \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{s}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$

$$
\overset{\left( 1\right) }{ = }\mathop{\bigoplus }\limits_{{{\mathbf{x}}_{{V}_{s}} \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}{T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \otimes  \mathop{\bigotimes }\limits_{{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}\mathop{\bigotimes }\limits_{{J \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{t}}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right) 
$$

$$
\overset{\left( 2\right) }{ = }{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{S} \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}{T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }\left( {{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{{V}_{t}} \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}{\bigotimes }_{J \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{t}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
\overset{\left( 3\right) }{ = }{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}{T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right) 
$$

where (1) regroups the product into the node $s$ and subtrees rooted at each child $t$ of $s$ ,(2) safely pushes aggregation over every child $t$ (since any $i \in  {V}_{t} \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( s,t\right) }$ only occurs in the subtree rooted at $t$ ,otherwise by the running intersection property, $i \in  \chi \left( s\right)  \cap  \chi \left( t\right)  \subseteq  {e}_{\left( s,t\right) }$ ,a contradiction),and (3) replaces every child sum-prod query by introducing a new IDB ${U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right)$ and a corresponding query:

其中(1)将乘积重组至节点$s$及其每个子节点$t$为根的子树，(2)安全地将聚合操作下推到每个子节点$t$（因为任何$i \in  {V}_{t} \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( s,t\right) }$仅出现在以$t$为根的子树中，否则根据运行交集性质会导致矛盾$i \in  \chi \left( s\right)  \cap  \chi \left( t\right)  \subseteq  {e}_{\left( s,t\right) }$），(3)通过引入新IDB${U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right)$及对应查询替换每个子节点的求和-乘积查询：

$$
{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{t} \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}{\bigotimes }_{J \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{t}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right) . \tag{7}
$$

(ii) Ground. Instead of grounding the sum-prod query (6) as a whole,we ground the $\sigma$ -equivalent (but refactored) query

(ii) 实例化。我们不将求和-乘积查询(6)整体实例化，而是实例化$\sigma$等价（但经过重构的）查询

$$
{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}{T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right) .
$$

Notice that $S$ is exactly the set of variables appear in the body of the refactored query. We ground this query as follows: for each possible tuple (say ${\mathbf{c}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$ ) in ${T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$ ,we add a grounded rule for each tuple (say ${\mathbf{c}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }$ ) that can be formed over the schema ${\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }$ using the active domain of each variable. This is done by taking the attribute values for each variable from ${\mathbf{c}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$ and ${\mathbf{c}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }$ ,and substituting it in every predicate (EDB, IDB, or the head) of the query.

注意$S$正是重构查询体中出现的变量集合。实例化步骤如下：对${T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$中每个可能元组（如${\mathbf{c}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$），我们为能在模式${\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }$上利用各变量活跃域形成的每个元组（如${\mathbf{c}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }$）添加实例化规则。具体实现是从${\mathbf{c}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$和${\mathbf{c}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }$提取各变量属性值，代入查询的每个谓词（EDB、IDB或头部）。

(iii) Recurse. Now every child $t$ of $s$ has an introduced IDB ${U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}$ . For each $t$ ,we call into $\operatorname{GRouND}\left( {t,{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}}\right)$ to recursively ground the new rule (7). If there are no children (i.e. $s$ is a leaf node),the GROUND call on $s$ terminates immediately.

(iii) 递归。现在$s$的每个子节点$t$都有引入的IDB${U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}$。对每个$t$，我们调用$\operatorname{GRouND}\left( {t,{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}}\right)$递归实例化新规则(7)。若无子节点（即$s$为叶节点），对$s$的GROUND调用立即终止。
