Fig. 1. A join tree with its corresponding rewriting.

图1. 带有对应重写操作的连接树

<!-- Media -->

We argue the grounding size and time at the grounding step(ii),for any node $s$ when grounding the refactored query. Recall that $r$ is the parent node of $s$ . We consider all possible tuples over ${\mathbf{x}}_{S}$ (variables in the body of the refactored query). First, ${T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$ is of size $O\left( m\right)$ if it is an EDB,or $O\left( {n}^{k}\right)$ if it is an IDB. Next,for the number of tuples that can be constructed over ${\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }$ ,note that

我们讨论在基础化步骤(ii)中的基础化规模与时间，当对重构查询进行基础化时，针对任意节点$s$。需注意$r$是$s$的父节点。我们考虑所有可能基于${\mathbf{x}}_{S}$（重构查询体中的变量）的元组。首先，若为EDB则${T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$的规模为$O\left( m\right)$，若为IDB则为$O\left( {n}^{k}\right)$。其次，对于可在${\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }$上构建的元组数量，需注意

$$
S = \chi \left( s\right)  \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}{e}_{\left( s,t\right) } = \chi \left( s\right)  \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}{H}_{t} \subseteq  \chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s}
$$

where the inclusion holds since ${H}_{t} \subseteq  {H}_{s}$ if $t$ is a child of $s$ . Observe that if ${H}_{s} \varsubsetneq  H$ ,then $\left| {H}_{s}\right|  \leq  k - 1$ ; otherwise (i.e., ${H}_{s} = H$ ),since we have rooted the join tree to a node that contains at least one head variable,at least one of the head variables in ${H}_{s}$ must belong to $\chi \left( s\right)$ (again by the running intersection property),in which case also $\left| {{H}_{s} \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }\right|  \leq  k - 1$ . Thus, $\left| {S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {\left( {\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s}}\right)  \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }\right|  =$ $\left| {{H}_{s} \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }\right|  \leq  k - 1$ . Hence,the groundings inserted for any intermediate rule is of size (and in time) $O\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right) }\right)$ ,i.e. the product of the cardinality of ${T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$ and the number of possible tuples that can be formed over the schema ${\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( S\right) }$ .

此处包含关系成立是因为当$t$是$s$的子节点时${H}_{t} \subseteq  {H}_{s}$。观察到若${H}_{s} \varsubsetneq  H$，则$\left| {H}_{s}\right|  \leq  k - 1$；否则（即${H}_{s} = H$），由于我们将连接树根节点设为至少包含一个头部变量，${H}_{s}$中至少有一个头部变量必须属于$\chi \left( s\right)$（再次依据运行交集性质），此时$\left| {{H}_{s} \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }\right|  \leq  k - 1$也成立。因此，$\left| {S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {\left( {\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s}}\right)  \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }\right|  =$$\left| {{H}_{s} \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }\right|  \leq  k - 1$。故为任何中间规则插入的基础化规模（及耗时）为$O\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right) }\right)$，即${T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$的基数与可在模式${\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( S\right) }$上形成的可能元组数量的乘积。

Example 5.2. We illustrate Theorem 3.2 with the acyclic rule:

示例5.2. 我们通过以下无环规则演示定理3.2：

$$
T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}{T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{24}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{24}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{34}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{34}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{14}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{14}\right) 
$$

A join tree is drawn in Figure 1 with new intermediate IDBs and rules (one per edge of the join tree) introduced recursively by the proof of Theorem 3.2. In the transformed program, each rule produces $O\left( {m + n}\right)  = O\left( m\right)$ groundings. Hence,its overall size is $O\left( m\right)$ . A step-by-step walk-through of this example (Example C.1) and more applications of Theorem 3.2 are in Appendix C.

图1绘制了连接树，其中新增的中间IDB和规则（连接树每条边对应一个）由定理3.2的证明递归引入。在转换后的程序中，每条规则生成$O\left( {m + n}\right)  = O\left( m\right)$个基础化实例，因此总规模为$O\left( m\right)$。本示例（示例C.1）的逐步详解及定理3.2的更多应用参见附录C。

Free-connexity. Take a tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ of a sum-prod query $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ rooted at a node $r \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ . Let ${\operatorname{TOP}}_{r}\left( x\right)$ be the highest node in $\mathcal{T}$ containing $x$ in its bag. We say that $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is free-connex w.r.t. $r$ if for any $x \in  H$ and $y \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  H,{\operatorname{TOP}}_{r}\left( y\right)$ is not an ancestor of ${\operatorname{TOP}}_{r}\left( x\right)$ [48]. We say that $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is free-connex if it is free-connex w.r.t. some $r \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ . An acyclic query is free-connex if it admits a free-connex join tree. If every sum-prod query in every rule of a program $P$ admits a free-connex join tree,then $P$ is rulewise free-connex acyclic. Theorem 5.3 (see Table 1) shows that in such a case,there is a much tighter bound (dropping the ${n}^{k - 1}$ term).

自由连通性。取一个以节点$r \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$为根的求和积查询$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$的树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$。设${\operatorname{TOP}}_{r}\left( x\right)$为$\mathcal{T}$中包含$x$的最高节点。若对于任意$x \in  H$且$y \in  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  H,{\operatorname{TOP}}_{r}\left( y\right)$不是${\operatorname{TOP}}_{r}\left( x\right)$的祖先节点[48]，则称$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$相对于$r$是自由连通的。若存在某个$r \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$使得$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$自由连通，则称其具有自由连通性。当无环查询允许存在自由连通连接树时，该查询即为自由连通无环查询。若程序$P$中每条规则的所有求和积查询均允许自由连通连接树，则该程序$P$为规则自由连通无环程序。定理5.3（见表1）表明，此类情况下存在更严格的上界（省略${n}^{k - 1}$项）。

Proposition 5.3. Let $P$ be a rulewise free-connex acyclic Datalog program over some semiring $\sigma$ with input size $m$ ,active domain size $n$ ,and arity(P)is at most $k$ . Then,a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding can be constructed in time (and has size) $O\left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right)$ .

命题5.3。设$P$为定义在输入规模$m$、活跃域大小$n$、且元数(P)不超过$k$的半环$\sigma$上的规则自由连通无环Datalog程序。则可在$O\left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right)$时间内（且规模为）构造出$\sigma$等价的基础实例。

Linear Acyclic Programs. If arity $\left( P\right)  \leq  2$ ,Theorem 3.2 states that a rulewise-acyclic Datalog program admits a grounding of size $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ . If the program is also linear (e.g. same generation [5]), we strengthen the upper bound to $O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ (see Table 1). The proof and an example (Example C.4) can be found in Appendix C.

线性无环程序。当元数$\left( P\right)  \leq  2$时，定理3.2指出规则无环Datalog程序允许构造规模为$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$的基础实例。若程序同时为线性（如同代查询[5]），则上界可强化至$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$（见表1）。证明过程及示例（示例C.4）详见附录C。

Proposition 5.4. Let $P$ be a linear rulewise-acyclic Datalog program over some semiring $\sigma$ with input size $m$ ,active domain size $n$ ,and arity(P)is at most 2 . Then,we can construct a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding in time (and has size) $O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ .

命题5.4。设$P$为定义在输入规模$m$、活跃域大小$n$、且元数(P)不超过2的半环$\sigma$上的线性规则无环Datalog程序。则可在$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$时间内（且规模为）构造出$\sigma$等价的基础实例。

## 6 GROUNDING OF GENERAL DATALOG

## 6 通用DATALOG程序的基础化

This section introduces an algorithm for grounding any Datalog program over an arbitary dioid via the PANDA algorithm [29] (or PANDA, for short). PANDA is introduced to evaluate CQs (i.e. sum-prod queries over the Boolean semiring).

本节介绍通过PANDA算法[29]（简称PANDA）在任意双环上基础化通用Datalog程序的算法。PANDA用于评估合取查询（即布尔半环上的求和积查询）。

Submodular width. A function $f : {2}^{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  } \mapsto  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ is a non-negative set function on $\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  \left( {\ell  \geq  1}\right)$ . The set function is monotone if $f\left( X\right)  \leq  f\left( Y\right)$ whenever $X \subseteq  Y$ ,and is submodular if $f\left( {X \cup  Y}\right)  + f\left( {X \cap  Y}\right)  \leq$ $f\left( X\right)  + f\left( Y\right)$ for all $X,Y \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ . A non-negative,monotone,submodular set function $h$ such that $h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$ is a polymatroid. Let $\varphi$ be a sum-prod query (5). Let ${\Gamma }_{\ell }$ be the set of all polymatroids $h$ on $\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ such that $h\left( J\right)  \leq  1$ for all $J \in  \mathcal{E}$ . The submodular width of $\varphi$ is

子模宽度。函数$f : {2}^{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  } \mapsto  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$是定义在$\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  \left( {\ell  \geq  1}\right)$上的非负集函数。当$X \subseteq  Y$时满足$f\left( X\right)  \leq  f\left( Y\right)$则该集函数是单调的；若对所有$X,Y \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$都有$f\left( {X \cup  Y}\right)  + f\left( {X \cap  Y}\right)  \leq$$f\left( X\right)  + f\left( Y\right)$则称为子模函数。满足$h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$的非负、单调、子模集函数$h$称为多拟阵。设$\varphi$为求和-乘积查询(5)，${\Gamma }_{\ell }$表示定义在$\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$上且对所有$J \in  \mathcal{E}$满足$h\left( J\right)  \leq  1$的多拟阵$h$的集合，则$\varphi$的子模宽度定义为

$$
\operatorname{subw}\left( \varphi \right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  {\Gamma }_{\ell }}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \mathcal{F}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}h\left( {\chi \left( t\right) }\right) , \tag{8}
$$

where $\mathcal{F}$ is the set of all non-redundant tree decompositions of $\varphi$ . A tree decomposition is non-redundant if no bag is a subset of another. Abo Khamis et al. [29] proved that non-redundancy ensures that $\mathcal{F}$ is finite,hence the inner minimum is well-defined. We define the free-connex sub-modular width of $\varphi$ ,fsubw $\left( \varphi \right)$ ,by restricting $\mathcal{F}$ to the set of all non-redundant free-connex tree decompositions.

其中$\mathcal{F}$是$\varphi$所有非冗余树分解的集合。若树分解中不存在一个包是另一个包子集的情况，则称该分解为非冗余的。Abo Khamis等人[29]证明非冗余性保证了$\mathcal{F}$的有限性，因此内层最小值是良定义的。我们将$\varphi$的自由连通子模宽度fsubw$\left( \varphi \right)$定义为：将$\mathcal{F}$限制在所有非冗余自由连通树分解集合上时的最小值。

We extend subw and fsubw to Datalog. The submodular width of a program $P$ (i.e. subw(P)) is the maximum subw $\left( \varphi \right)$ across all sum-prod queries in $P$ . Likewise,the free-connex submodular width of $P$ (i.e. fsubw(P)) is the maximum fsubw $\left( \varphi \right)$ over all sum-prod queries in $P$ . If $P$ is rulewise-acyclic, $\operatorname{subw}\left( P\right)  = 1$ .

我们将subw和fsubw扩展到Datalog语言。程序$P$的子模宽度subw(P)是其所有求和-乘积查询中subw$\left( \varphi \right)$的最大值。同理，$P$的自由连通子模宽度fsubw(P)是所有求和-乘积查询中fsubw$\left( \varphi \right)$的最大值。若$P$是规则无环的，则$\operatorname{subw}\left( P\right)  = 1$。

This section describes the key ideas of the algorithm underlying Theorem 3.3 (formally as Algorithm 4). We demonstrate the grounding algorithm step-by-step on the following concrete example.

本节阐述定理3.3所涉算法(正式实现为算法4)的核心思想。我们将通过以下具体示例逐步演示该基础算法。

Example 6.1. The following Datalog computes a diamond-pattern reachability starting from some node a set $U\left( {x}_{1}\right)  : T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  U\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ ,

示例6.1. 下列Datalog程序计算从节点集合$U\left( {x}_{1}\right)  : T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  U\left( {x}_{1}\right)$出发的钻石模式可达性：

$$
T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}T\left( {x}_{3}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{32}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{32}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{21}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{21}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{34}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{34}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{41}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{41}\right) 
$$

We ground the second rule that involves a 4-cycle join. In particular, our grounding algorithm constructs a grounding of size $\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$ .

我们对涉及4环连接的第二条规则进行基础化处理。特别地，基础化算法将构造规模为$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$的基础集。

(i) Refactor. Let $\left( {\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$ be the associated hypergraph of the above cyclic recursive rule,where $\mathcal{E} = \{ \{ 3\} ,\{ 3,2\} ,\{ 2,1\} ,\{ 3,4\} ,\{ 4,1\} \}$ . We construct the non-redundant tree decompositions $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right.$ (there are only two),both having two nodes $\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$ and one edge $\left\{  \left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right) \right\}$ ,where the bags are

(i) 重构。设$\left( {\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$为上述循环递归规则的关联超图，其中$\mathcal{E} = \{ \{ 3\} ,\{ 3,2\} ,\{ 2,1\} ,\{ 3,4\} ,\{ 4,1\} \}$。我们构建了两个非冗余树分解$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right.$（仅存在两种），二者均包含两个节点$\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$和一条边$\left\{  \left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right) \right\}$，其包结构如下

$$
{\chi }_{1}\left( {v}_{1}\right)  = \left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  ,{\chi }_{1}\left( {v}_{2}\right)  = \{ 3,4,1\} ;{\chi }_{2}\left( {v}_{1}\right)  = \{ 4,1,2\} ,{\chi }_{2}\left( {v}_{2}\right)  = \{ 2,3,4\} \text{.}
$$

This allows us to rewrite and factorize the cyclic body into two acyclic sum-prod queries, one for each decomposition:

这使我们能将循环体重写并因式分解为两个无环的和积查询，每种分解对应一个：

$$
\varphi \left( {x}_{1}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}T\left( {x}_{3}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{32} \otimes  {R}_{21} \otimes  {R}_{34} \otimes  {R}_{41}
$$

$$
 = {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}\underset{{B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }\right) }{\underbrace{T \otimes  {R}_{32} \otimes  {R}_{21}}} \otimes  \underset{{B}_{341}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{341}\right) }{\underbrace{{R}_{34} \otimes  {R}_{41}}} \oplus  \underset{{B}_{412}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{412}\right) }{\underbrace{{R}_{21} \otimes  {R}_{41}}} \otimes  \underset{{B}_{234}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{234}\right) }{\underbrace{T \otimes  {R}_{32} \otimes  {R}_{34}}}
$$

where the second line uses the idempotence of $\oplus$ . The underbraces introduce new IDBs and rules (e.g. ${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  } \leftarrow  T \otimes  {R}_{32} \otimes  {R}_{21}$ corresponds to the bag ${\chi }_{1}\left( {v}_{1}\right)$ of ${\mathcal{T}}_{1}$ and likewise for ${B}_{341},{B}_{234}$ and ${B}_{412}$ ).

其中第二行运用了$\oplus$的幂等性。下划线标注处引入了新的IDB及规则（例如${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  } \leftarrow  T \otimes  {R}_{32} \otimes  {R}_{21}$对应${\mathcal{T}}_{1}$的包${\chi }_{1}\left( {v}_{1}\right)$，同理适用于${B}_{341},{B}_{234}$与${B}_{412}$）。

(ii) Grounding Bags. A naïve grounding of (say) ${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }$ is of size $O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ (cartesian product of ${R}_{32}$ with domain of ${x}_{1}$ ),which is suboptimal. Instead,we use PANDA to ground the new IDBs.

(ii) 基础包处理。若对（例如）${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }$进行朴素基础化，其规模将为$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$（${R}_{32}$与${x}_{1}$域的笛卡尔积），此方法非最优。我们改用PANDA工具对新IDB进行基础化。

Let $Q\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }\right)$ be the set of tuples such that a tuple ${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  } \in  Q$ if and only if its annotation $T\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{3}\right)  \otimes$ ${R}_{32}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{32}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{21}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{21}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{34}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{34}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{41}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{41}\right)  \neq  \mathbf{0}$ . Here, ${\mathbf{c}}_{J}$ with $J \subseteq  \left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack$ is a shorthand for the projection of ${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }$ onto the variables in $J$ . The PANDA algorithm outputs one table for every new IDB (to differentiate,denote the PANDA output tables as ${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }^{ * },{B}_{341}^{ * },{B}_{234}^{ * }$ and ${B}_{412}^{ * }$ ) such that: (1) each table is of size $\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$ since subw $\left( P\right)  = 3/2;\left( 2\right) Q\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }\right)$ is equal to the union of two CQs,one for each decomposition, i.e.

设$Q\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }\right)$为满足以下条件的元组集合：元组${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  } \in  Q$当且仅当其标注<b2></b3></b3>。此处${\mathbf{c}}_{J}$与$J \subseteq  \left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack$表示将${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }$投影到$J$变量的简写形式。PANDA算法为每个新IDB输出一个表（为区分，将PANDA输出表记为${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }^{ * },{B}_{341}^{ * },{B}_{234}^{ * }$和${B}_{412}^{ * }$），满足：(1) 每表尺寸为$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$，因subw$\left( P\right)  = 3/2;\left( 2\right) Q\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }\right)$等于两个CQ的并集（每种分解对应一个CQ），即

$$
\left( {{B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }\right)  \bowtie  {B}_{341}^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{341}\right) }\right)  \cup  \left( {{B}_{412}^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{412}\right)  \bowtie  {B}_{234}^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{234}\right) }\right)  = Q\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }\right) 
$$

Using PANDA output tables,we ground the four new IDBs,e.g. for each tuple ${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  } \in  {B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }^{ * }$ ,we add a grounded rule: ${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{3}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{32}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{32}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{21}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{21}\right)$ . The same step is applied to all IDBs in total time and size $\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$ . This ensures that each tuple in $Q\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }\right)$ is present in at least one of ${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  } \otimes  {B}_{341}$ or ${B}_{412} \otimes  {B}_{234}$ ,with the correct annotation being preserved ,i.e. for any ${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  } \in  Q\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }\right)$ ,

利用PANDA输出表，我们对四个新IDB进行实例化，例如对于每个元组${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  } \in  {B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }^{ * }$，我们添加一条实例化规则：${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{3}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{32}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{32}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{21}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{21}\right)$。该步骤以总时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$应用于所有IDB，确保$Q\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }\right)$中的每个元组至少存在于${B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  } \otimes  {B}_{341}$或${B}_{412} \otimes  {B}_{234}$中，并保留正确标注，即对于任意${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  } \in  Q\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack  }\right)$，

$$
T \otimes  {R}_{32} \otimes  {R}_{21} \otimes  {R}_{34} \otimes  {R}_{41} = \left( {{B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  } \otimes  {B}_{341}}\right)  \oplus  \left( {{B}_{412} \otimes  {B}_{234}}\right) .
$$

(iii) Grounding Acyclic Sub-queries. With the grounded bags,we now rewrite $\varphi \left( {x}_{1}\right)$ as a sum of two acyclic sum-prod queries:

(三) 无环子查询实例化。基于实例化的数据包，我们将$\varphi \left( {x}_{1}\right)$重写为两个无环求和-乘积查询的总和：

$$
\varphi \left( {x}_{1}\right)  = \left\lbrack  {{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}{B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  }\right)  \otimes  {B}_{341}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{341}\right) }\right\rbrack   \oplus  \left\lbrack  {{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}{B}_{412} \otimes  {B}_{234}}\right\rbrack  
$$

We ground the two acyclic sub-queries (one per decomposition) using the construction in the proof of Theorem 3.2. For example,if we root both trees at ${v}_{1}$ ,we get a rewriting:

根据定理3.2证明中的构造方法，我们对两个无环子查询（每个分解对应一个）进行实例化。例如，若将两棵树均以${v}_{1}$为根节点，则得到重写式：

$$
{U}_{31}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{31}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{4}}{B}_{341}\;{U}_{24}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{24}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}{B}_{234}
$$

$$
\varphi \left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  \left\lbrack  {{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{23}}{B}_{\left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack  } \otimes  {U}_{31}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{31}\right) }\right\rbrack   \oplus  \left\lbrack  {{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{24}}{B}_{412} \otimes  {U}_{24}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{24}\right) }\right\rbrack  
$$

guaranteeing that every intermediate IDB has a grounding of size $\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$ . Thus,the total size of the grounding for $P$ is $\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$ .

确保每个中间IDB的实例化规模为$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$。因此，$P$的实例化总规模为$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{3/2}\right)$。

We show a free-connex version (Proposition 6.2) by restricting the first refactoring step to use only free-connex decompositions. Though fsubw $\geq$ subw,a ${n}^{k - 1}$ factor is shaved off from the bound.

通过限制首次重构步骤仅使用自由连接分解，我们给出了自由连接版本（命题6.2）。虽然fsubw$\geq$subw，但边界值消减了${n}^{k - 1}$系数。

Proposition 6.2. Let $P$ be a Datalog program with input size $m$ ,active domain size $n$ ,arity $\left( P\right)  \leq  k$ . fsubw is its free-connex submodular width. Let $\sigma$ be a dioid. Then,a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding can be constructed in time (and has size) $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{fsubw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{fsubw }}}\right)$ .

命题6.2。设$P$为输入规模$m$、活跃域规模$n$、元数$\left( P\right)  \leq  k$的Datalog程序，fsubw为其自由连接次模宽度，$\sigma$为双半环。则可构造$\sigma$等价实例化，其时间（及规模）为$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{fsubw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{fsubw }}}\right)$。

Linear Programs. For linear programs, a careful analysis yields the following improved result (proof is in Appendix E.4).

线性规划。对于线性规划，经细致分析可得以下改进结果（证明见附录E.4）。

Proposition 6.3. Let $P$ be a linear Datalog program where

命题6.3。设$P$为满足下列条件的线性Datalog程序：

$\operatorname{arity}\left( P\right)  \leq  k$ ,input size $m$ ,and active domain size $n$ . Let $\sigma$ be a dioid. Then,a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding can be constructed in time (and has size) $\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1}{m}^{\text{subw } - 1} \cdot  \left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right) }\right)$ .

$\operatorname{arity}\left( P\right)  \leq  k$，输入规模$m$，活跃域规模$n$。$\sigma$为双半环。则可构造$\sigma$等价实例化，其时间（及规模）为$\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1}{m}^{\text{subw } - 1} \cdot  \left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right) }\right)$。

Fractional Hypertree-width. So far, all our results only apply to dioids. However, we can extend our results to any semiring by using the InsideOut algorithm [2]. Similar to subw $\left( \varphi \right)$ (8),the fractional hypertree-width of a sum-prod query $\varphi$ ,i.e. fhw $\left( \varphi \right)$ ,is defined as

分数超树宽度(Fractional Hypertree-width)。截至目前，我们的所有结果仅适用于双半环(dioids)。然而，通过使用InsideOut算法[2]，我们可以将结果推广至任意半环。类似于子宽度(subw $\left( \varphi \right)$ (8))，求和-乘积查询(sum-prod query $\varphi$)的分数超树宽度，即fhw $\left( \varphi \right)$，其定义为

$$
\operatorname{fhw}\left( \varphi \right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \mathcal{F}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  {\Gamma }_{\ell }}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}h\left( {\chi \left( t\right) }\right) 
$$

The fractional hypertree-width of a program $P$ is the maximum flw over all sum-prod queries, denoted as fhw(P). We show Proposition 6.4 for the grounding size over any semiring (see Table 1).

程序$P$的分数超树宽度是所有求和-乘积查询中最大分数树宽度(flw)，记作fhw(P)。我们展示了关于任意半环上接地规模(grounding size)的命题6.4（见表1）。

Proposition 6.4. Let $P$ be a Datalog program with input size $m$ ,active domain size $n$ ,and $\operatorname{arity}\left( P\right)  \leq$ k. Let $\sigma$ be a naturally-ordered semiring. Then,a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding can be constructed in time (and has size) $O\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {{m}^{\text{fhw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{fhw }}}\right) }\right)$ .

命题6.4。设$P$为输入规模$m$、活跃域大小(active domain size)$n$且$\operatorname{arity}\left( P\right)  \leq$k的Datalog程序。令$\sigma$为自然有序半环(naturally-ordered semiring)，则可在$O\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {{m}^{\text{fhw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{fhw }}}\right) }\right)$时间内（且具有相应规模）构造出$\sigma$等价接地。

## 7 RELATED WORK

## 7 相关工作

Complexity of Datalog. Data complexity of special fragments of Datalog has been explicitly studied. Gottlob et al. [22] showed that monadic acyclic Datalog can be evaluated in linear time w.r.t the size of the program plus the size of the input data. Gottlob et al. [21] defined the fragment Datalog LITE as the set of all stratified Datalog queries whose rules are either monadic or guarded. The authors showed that this fragment can also be evaluated in linear time as monadic acyclic Datalog. This result also follows from a generalization of Courcelle's theorem [12] by Flum et al. [18]. Our framework subsumes these results as an application. Lutz et al. [34] studied efficient enumeration of ontology-mediated queries that are acyclic and free-connex acyclic. The reader may refer to Green at al. [23] for an in-depth survey of Datalog rewriting, Datalog with disjunctions, and with integrity constraints. However, there is no principled study on general Datalog programs in parameterized complexity despite its prevalence in the literature of join query evaluation [2, 29, ${35},{38}\rbrack$ .

Datalog的复杂性。Datalog特殊片段的数据复杂性已被明确研究。Gottlob等人[22]证明一元无环Datalog(monadic acyclic Datalog)可在程序规模加输入数据规模的线性时间内求值。Gottlob等人[21]将Datalog LITE片段定义为所有分层(stratified)且规则为一元或保护式(guarded)的Datalog查询集合，证明该片段同样能像一元无环Datalog那样线性时间求值。该结论也可从Flum等人[18]对Courcelle定理[12]的推广中得出。我们的框架将这些结果作为应用实例包含其中。Lutz等人[34]研究了无环且自由连接无环(free-connex acyclic)的本体中介查询的高效枚举。关于Datalog重写、带析取的Datalog及含完整性约束的Datalog的深入综述，读者可参阅Green等人[23]。然而尽管在连接查询评估文献[2,29,${35},{38}\rbrack$中普遍存在，目前仍缺乏对一般Datalog程序参数化复杂性的系统性研究。

Datalog over Semirings. Besides Datalog ${}^{ \circ  }$ [28],recent work [25] has looked at the convergence rate for linear Datalog ${}^{ \circ  }$ . The evaluation of Datalog over absorptive semirings with a total order has also been studied [39] where the key idea is to transform the program (and its input) into a weighted hypergraph and use Knuth's algorithm [30] for evaluation. Our paper recovers and extends this result by formalizing the proof of correctness via the concept of asynchronous Kleene chains (Appendix B.1), and showing a precise runtime for evaluating a grounding over such semir-ings. None of the works mentioned above focus on finding the smallest possible grounding, a key ingredient to show the tightest possible bounds.

半环上的Datalog。除Datalog${}^{ \circ  }$[28]外，近期研究[25]探讨了线性Datalog${}^{ \circ  }$的收敛速率。全序吸收半环上的Datalog求值问题已有研究[39]，其核心思想是将程序（及其输入）转换为加权超图并利用Knuth算法[30]进行求值。本文通过异步Kleene链概念（附录B.1）形式化正确性证明，并给出此类半环上实例化求值的精确时间复杂度，从而推广并完善了该结果。上述研究均未聚焦于寻找最小可能实例化——这是证明最严格边界的关键要素。

Datalog Provenance Computation and Circuits. Algorithms for Datalog provenance computation provides an alternative way to think of Datalog evaluation. Deutch et al. [14] initiated the study of circuits for database provenance. They show that for a Datalog program having $\left| G\right|$ groundings for IDBs,a circuit for representing Datalog provenance for the $\operatorname{Sorp}\left( X\right)$ semiring (absorptive semirings are a special case of Sorp) can be built using only $\left| G\right|  + 1$ layers. As an example, for APSP,the circuit construction and evaluation cost $O\left( {n}^{4}\right)$ time. An improvement of the result [27] showed that for APSP,a monotone arithmetic circuit of size $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ can be constructed by mimicking the dynamic programming nature of the Bellman-Ford algorithm. We use this improvement to show a circuit unconditional lower bound on the grounding size (see Appendix D).

Datalog溯源计算与电路。Datalog溯源计算算法提供了另一种Datalog求值思路。Deutch等人[14]开创了数据库溯源电路研究，证明对于具有$\left| G\right|$个IDB实例化的Datalog程序，仅需$\left| G\right|  + 1$层电路即可构建表示$\operatorname{Sorp}\left( X\right)$半环（吸收半环是Sorp的特例）上Datalog溯源的电路。以APSP为例，电路构建与求值耗时$O\left( {n}^{4}\right)$。改进研究[27]表明，通过模拟Bellman-Ford算法的动态规划特性，可构建规模为$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$的单调算术电路。我们利用该改进结果证明了实例化大小的电路无条件下界（见附录D）。

## 8 CONCLUSION

## 8 结论

This paper introduces a general two-phased framework that uses the structure of a Datalog program to construct a tight grounding, and then evaluates it using the algebraic properties of the semiring. Our framework successfully recovers state-of-the-art results for popular programs (e.g. chain programs, APSP), and uncovers new results (e.g. for linear Datalog). We also show a matching lower bound (both for running time and space requirement) for a class of Datalog programs. Future work includes efficient evaluation over broader classes of semirings [28], circuit complexity of Datalog over semirings and general grounding lower bounds.

本文提出通用两阶段框架：利用Datalog程序结构构建紧凑实例化，再结合半环代数性质进行求值。该框架不仅成功复现了经典程序（如链式程序、APSP）的最优结果，还揭示了新发现（如线性Datalog）。我们同时证明了一类Datalog程序在时间与空间复杂度上的匹配下界。未来工作包括扩展半环类的高效求值[28]、半环上Datalog的电路复杂性及通用实例化下界研究。

## 第2节补充材料

LEMMA A.1 ([39]). Let $\sigma$ be a naturally-ordered dioid. Then $\sigma$ is absorptive iff $a \otimes  b \sqsubseteq  a$ for all $a,b \in  D$ .

引理A.1([39]). 设$\sigma$为自然序dioid，则$\sigma$具有吸收性当且仅当对所有$a,b \in  D$满足$a \otimes  b \sqsubseteq  a$。

Proof of Lemma A.1. (⇒) Since $\sigma$ is absorptive, $a \oplus  a \otimes  b = a \otimes  \left( {\mathbf{1} \oplus  b}\right)  = a \otimes  \mathbf{1} = a$ . Thus, $a \otimes  b \sqsubseteq  a$ ;

引理A.1证明. (⇒) 由吸收性可得$a \oplus  a \otimes  b = a \otimes  \left( {\mathbf{1} \oplus  b}\right)  = a \otimes  \mathbf{1} = a$，故$a \otimes  b \sqsubseteq  a$；

$\left(  \Leftarrow  \right)$ For any $a \in  \sigma ,a = \mathbf{1} \otimes  a \sqsubseteq  \mathbf{1}$ (i.e. $\mathbf{1} \oplus  a = \mathbf{1}$ )

$\left(  \Leftarrow  \right)$ 对任意$a \in  \sigma ,a = \mathbf{1} \otimes  a \sqsubseteq  \mathbf{1}$（即$\mathbf{1} \oplus  a = \mathbf{1}$）

### B.1 Asynchronous Fixpoint Evaluation

### B.1 异步不动点求值

The result in this part on asynchronous evaluation for Datalog is folklore, but we formalize it in a rigorous and general way. The definitions and lemmas established are used in the proof of Theorem 3.5 and Theorem 3.6, to show convergence.

关于Datalog异步求值的结论虽属学界共识，但我们以严谨通用方式进行了形式化。这些定义与引理用于定理3.5和定理3.6的收敛性证明。

Let $G$ be a grounding containing $k$ rules over $\sigma$ . Suppose we have $k$ posets ${\mathrm{L}}_{1},{\mathrm{L}}_{2},\ldots ,{\mathrm{L}}_{k}$ with minimum elements ${ \bot  }_{1},\ldots ,{ \bot  }_{k}$ respectively. Let ${\left\{  {f}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ be a family of $k$ motonone functions, where

设$G$为包含$k$条基于$\sigma$的规则的基集。假设我们有$k$个偏序集${\mathrm{L}}_{1},{\mathrm{L}}_{2},\ldots ,{\mathrm{L}}_{k}$，其最小元素分别为${ \bot  }_{1},\ldots ,{ \bot  }_{k}$。令${\left\{  {f}_{i}\right\}  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$是由$k$个单调函数构成的族，其中

$$
{f}_{i} : {\mathrm{L}}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathrm{L}}_{k} \rightarrow  {\mathrm{L}}_{i}
$$

associates to the right-hand side of the $i$ -th rule (the multivariate polynomial) in $G$ . Let $\bot   =$ $\left( {{ \bot  }_{1},\ldots ,{ \bot  }_{k}}\right)$ and the ICO of $G$ is

关联到$G$中第$i$条规则右侧（多元多项式）。设$\bot   =$$\left( {{ \bot  }_{1},\ldots ,{ \bot  }_{k}}\right)$，且$G$的ICO为

$$
f = \left( {{f}_{1},\ldots ,{f}_{k}}\right)  : {\mathbf{L}}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathbf{L}}_{k} \rightarrow  {\mathbf{L}}_{1} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathbf{L}}_{k}.
$$

The Kleene sequence (or ascending Kleene chain) attempts to evaluate the grounding (if there is a fixpoint) through the following (ascending) sequence:

克林序列（或称升序克林链）试图通过以下（升序）序列评估基集（若存在不动点）：

$$
{\mathbf{h}}^{\left( 0\right) } =  \bot  ,\;{\mathbf{h}}^{\left( 1\right) } = \mathbf{f}\left( {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( 0\right) }\right) ,\;{\mathbf{h}}^{\left( 2\right) } = \mathbf{f}\left( {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( 1\right) }\right) ,
$$

Here ${\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) } = \left( {{h}_{1}^{\left( t\right) },\ldots ,{h}_{k}^{\left( t\right) }}\right) ,t \geq  0$ ,is the vector of values for the posets at the $t$ -th iteration (applying $f$ for $t$ times).

此处${\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) } = \left( {{h}_{1}^{\left( t\right) },\ldots ,{h}_{k}^{\left( t\right) }}\right) ,t \geq  0$表示第$t$次迭代时偏序集的值向量（应用$f$共$t$次）。

We say that the Kleene sequence converges in $t$ steps if ${\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t + 1\right) } = {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) }$ . Note that the Kleene sequence updates ${\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) }$ by using the values of the previous iteration simultaneously. We show that we can obtain convergence if ${\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) }$ is updated asynchronously.

若${\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t + 1\right) } = {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) }$，则称克林序列在$t$步内收敛。注意克林序列通过同步使用前次迭代值来更新${\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) }$。我们证明若${\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) }$采用异步更新方式亦可获得收敛性。

Definition B.1. An asynchronous Kleene chain is a chain where ${\hslash }^{\left( 0\right) } =  \bot$ and for any $t \geq  0$ :

定义B.1. 异步克林链是指满足${\hslash }^{\left( 0\right) } =  \bot$且对任意$t \geq  0$：

$$
{\hslash }_{i}^{\left( t + 1\right) } = {f}_{i}\left( {{\hslash }_{1}^{\left( {t}_{1}\right) },{\hslash }_{2}^{\left( {t}_{2}\right) },\ldots ,{\hslash }_{n}^{\left( {t}_{n}\right) }}\right) \text{,for some}0 \leq  {t}_{i} \leq  t\text{.}
$$

In other words, the current values of each variable can be updated by looking at possibly stale values of the other variables. When ${t}_{1} = {t}_{2} = \cdots  = t$ ,we get exactly a Kleene chain. The following proposition tells us that any asynchronous Kleene chain is always below the Kleene chain.

换言之，各变量的当前值可通过可能过时的其他变量值进行更新。当${t}_{1} = {t}_{2} = \cdots  = t$时，即得到标准克林链。下述命题表明任何异步克林链始终位于克林链下方。

Proposition B.2. Let ${\hslash }^{\left( 0\right) },{\hslash }^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots$ be an asynchronous Kleene chain. For every $t \geq  0,{\hslash }^{\left( t\right) } \sqsubseteq  {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) }$ .

命题B.2. 设${\hslash }^{\left( 0\right) },{\hslash }^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots$为异步克林链。对于每个$t \geq  0,{\hslash }^{\left( t\right) } \sqsubseteq  {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( t\right) }$。

Proof. We will use the inductive hypothesis that for every ${t}^{\prime } \leq  t,{\hslash }^{\left( {t}^{\prime }\right) } \sqsubseteq  {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( {t}^{\prime }\right) }$ . For the base case $t = 0$ ,we have ${\hslash }^{\left( 0\right) } =  \bot   = {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( 0\right) }$ . For the inductive case,assume that ${\hslash }^{\left( {t}^{\prime }\right) } \sqsubseteq  {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( {t}^{\prime }\right) }$ for every ${t}^{\prime } \leq  t$ . Then,we can write:

证明. 采用归纳假设：对每个${t}^{\prime } \leq  t,{\hslash }^{\left( {t}^{\prime }\right) } \sqsubseteq  {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( {t}^{\prime }\right) }$。基础情形$t = 0$时，有${\hslash }^{\left( 0\right) } =  \bot   = {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( 0\right) }$。归纳情形中，假设对每个${t}^{\prime } \leq  t$有${\hslash }^{\left( {t}^{\prime }\right) } \sqsubseteq  {\mathbf{h}}^{\left( {t}^{\prime }\right) }$，则可表述：

$$
{\hslash }_{j}^{\left( t + 1\right) } = {f}_{i}\left( {{\hslash }_{1}^{\left( {t}_{1}\right) },{\hslash }_{2}^{\left( {t}_{2}\right) },\ldots ,{\hslash }_{n}^{\left( {t}_{n}\right) }}\right) 
$$

$$
 \sqsubseteq  {f}_{i}\left( {{h}^{\left( {t}_{1}\right) },{h}^{\left( {t}_{2}\right) },\ldots ,{h}^{\left( {t}_{n}\right) }}\right) 
$$

$$
 \sqsubseteq  {f}_{i}\left( {{h}^{\left( t\right) },{h}^{\left( t\right) },\ldots ,{h}^{\left( t\right) }}\right) 
$$

$$
 = {h}_{i}^{\left( t + 1\right) }
$$

The first inequality follows from the inductive hypothesis, and the second inequality is implied by the monotonicity of the Kleene chain. Both inequalities use the monotonicity of the function ${f}_{i}$ .

第一不等式源于归纳假设，第二不等式由克林链单调性导出。两个不等式均利用函数${f}_{i}$的单调性。

### B.2 Proof of Theorem 3.5

### B.2 定理3.5证明

In this part, we complete the proof of the theorem, by showing that Algorithm 1 correctly computes the solution with the desired runtime.

本部分通过证明算法1能以预期运行时正确计算解，从而完成定理的证明。

Proof of THEOREM 3.5. Algorithm 1 terminates when no variable changes value, in which case we have obtained a fixpoint. By Proposition B.2, we have reached the least fixpoint (since we can view the updates as an asynchronous Kleene chain).

定理3.5的证明。当没有变量值发生变化时，算法1终止，此时我们已获得一个不动点。根据命题B.2，我们达到了最小不动点（因为可以将这些更新视为异步Kleene链）。

We now analyze its runtime. The hash table construction and the variable initialization cost time $O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$ . For the while loop,observe that each operation in Line 7-10 costs $O\left( 1\right)$ time. Hence, it suffices to bound the number of times we visit each equation in $G$ . For this,note that to consider the equation $y = x \circledast  z$ ,either $x$ or $z$ needs to be updated. However,since the semiring has rank $r$ ,each variable can be updated at most $r$ times. Hence,an equation can be considered at most ${2r}$ times in the while loop.

现在分析其运行时间。哈希表构建和变量初始化耗时$O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$。对于while循环，观察第7-10行的每个操作耗时$O\left( 1\right)$。因此只需限制在$G$中访问每个方程的次数。注意考虑方程$y = x \circledast  z$时，需满足$x$或$z$被更新。但由于半环的秩为$r$，每个变量最多更新$r$次，故while循环中一个方程最多被考虑${2r}$次。

### B.3 Proof of Theorem 3.6

### B.3 定理3.6的证明

In the following,we assume $G$ to be a 2-canonical grounding after applying Lemma 4.1. First,we establish the following two lemmas throughout the while-loop of Algorithm 2. The first one is the following:

下文假设$G$为应用引理4.1后的2-规范接地。首先，在算法2的while循环中建立以下两个引理。其一是：

LEMMA B.3. For any IDB variable $x$ in $G,\mathbf{0} \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( 1\right) } \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( 2\right) } \sqsubseteq  \cdots$ ,where ${x}^{\left( 0\right) } = \mathbf{0},{x}^{\left( 1\right) },{x}^{\left( 2\right) },\ldots$ are the values of $x$ in the asynchronous Kleene chain constructed from the while-loop of Algorithm 2.

引理B.3。对于$G,\mathbf{0} \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( 1\right) } \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( 2\right) } \sqsubseteq  \cdots$中任意IDB变量$x$，其中${x}^{\left( 0\right) } = \mathbf{0},{x}^{\left( 1\right) },{x}^{\left( 2\right) },\ldots$是从算法2的while循环构造的异步Kleene链中$x$的值。

Proof. The base case is the first pop,say it pops the variable ${x}_{1}$ . Trivially, $\mathbf{0} \sqsubseteq  {x}_{1}^{\left( 1\right) } = {x}_{1}^{\left( 2\right) } = \cdots$ because ${x}_{1}$ is fixed onwards. Then,for an IDB variable $y \notin  E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack  ,{y}^{\left( 1\right) } = {y}^{\left( 2\right) }$ ,else ${y}^{\left( 1\right) } = \mathbf{0} \circledast  {z}^{\left( 0\right) } \sqsubseteq$ ${x}_{1}^{\left( 1\right) } \circledast  {z}^{\left( 1\right) } = {y}^{\left( 2\right) }.$

证明。基本情况是首次弹出变量${x}_{1}$时。显然$\mathbf{0} \sqsubseteq  {x}_{1}^{\left( 1\right) } = {x}_{1}^{\left( 2\right) } = \cdots$成立，因为${x}_{1}$此后固定。对于IDB变量$y \notin  E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack  ,{y}^{\left( 1\right) } = {y}^{\left( 2\right) }$，否则${y}^{\left( 1\right) } = \mathbf{0} \circledast  {z}^{\left( 0\right) } \sqsubseteq$${x}_{1}^{\left( 1\right) } \circledast  {z}^{\left( 1\right) } = {y}^{\left( 2\right) }.$

Now in the inductive case (at the $\left( {i + 1}\right)$ -th pop of the variable ${x}_{i}$ ),the inductive hypothesis is that for each IDB variable $x,\mathbf{0} \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( 1\right) } \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( 2\right) } \sqsubseteq  \cdots  \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( i\right) }$ . Then,for any IDB variable $y$ , ${y}^{\left( i\right) } = {x}_{i}^{\left( i - 1\right) } \circledast  {z}^{\left( i - 1\right) } \sqsubseteq  {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \circledast  {z}^{\left( i\right) } = {y}^{\left( i + 1\right) }$ by monotonicity of both $\oplus  , \otimes$ .

归纳情形下（第$\left( {i + 1}\right)$次弹出变量${x}_{i}$时），归纳假设为：对每个IDB变量$x,\mathbf{0} \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( 1\right) } \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( 2\right) } \sqsubseteq  \cdots  \sqsubseteq  {x}^{\left( i\right) }$成立。那么对任意IDB变量$y$，根据$\oplus  , \otimes$的单调性可得${y}^{\left( i\right) } = {x}_{i}^{\left( i - 1\right) } \circledast  {z}^{\left( i - 1\right) } \sqsubseteq  {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \circledast  {z}^{\left( i\right) } = {y}^{\left( i + 1\right) }$。

The second invariant on the while-loop of Algorithm 2 is stated as the following:

算法2的while循环中第二个不变式表述如下：

LEMMA B.4. The values of IDB variables popped off the queue are non-increasing w.r.t. to $\sqsubseteq$ .

引理B.4。从队列中弹出的IDB变量值关于$\sqsubseteq$是非递增的。

Proof. Let ${x}^{\left( i\right) }\left( {i \geq  1}\right)$ denote the value of variable $x$ at the $i$ -th pop. If $\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\ldots }\right)$ is the sequence of variables that get popped off the queue,then we want to show that $1\exists {x}_{1}^{\left( 1\right) }\exists {x}_{2}^{\left( 2\right) }\exists$ $\cdots  \sqsupseteq  \mathbf{0}$ . We prove it via induction. Observe that ${x}_{i}$ will bind to ${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$ after the $i$ -th pop. Thus, ${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } = {x}_{i}^{\left( j\right) }$ for any $j \geq  i$ .

证明。设${x}^{\left( i\right) }\left( {i \geq  1}\right)$表示第$i$次弹出时变量$x$的值。若$\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\ldots }\right)$为从队列中弹出的变量序列，则需证明$1\exists {x}_{1}^{\left( 1\right) }\exists {x}_{2}^{\left( 2\right) }\exists$$\cdots  \sqsupseteq  \mathbf{0}$。采用归纳法证明。观察到${x}_{i}$在第$i$次弹出后将绑定到${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$。因此，对于任意$j \geq  i$，${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } = {x}_{i}^{\left( j\right) }$成立。

The base case is trivial,since $\mathbf{1} \sqsupseteq  s \sqsupseteq  \mathbf{0}$ holds for any $s \in  D$ . Now,inductively,suppose we pop ${x}_{i}\left( {i \geq  2}\right)$ at some point and $1\exists {x}_{1}^{\left( 1\right) }\exists {x}_{2}^{\left( 2\right) }\exists \cdots \exists {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$ is the sequence of already popped values. We will show that ${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupseteq  {x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i + 1\right) }$ .

基本情况显然成立，因为$\mathbf{1} \sqsupseteq  s \sqsupseteq  \mathbf{0}$对任意$s \in  D$都成立。现归纳假设：假设我们在某次弹出${x}_{i}\left( {i \geq  2}\right)$时，$1\exists {x}_{1}^{\left( 1\right) }\exists {x}_{2}^{\left( 2\right) }\exists \cdots \exists {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$是已弹出的值序列。将证明${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupseteq  {x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i + 1\right) }$成立。

We distinguish two cases. Suppose that ${x}_{i}$ is not in the right-hand side of the equation of ${x}_{i + 1}$ . Then, ${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupseteq  {x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i\right) } = {x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i + 1\right) }$ ,where the first inequality comes from the fact that ${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$ is popped off as a max element in the queue at iteration $i$ .

分两种情况讨论。假设${x}_{i}$不在${x}_{i + 1}$方程的右侧。则${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupseteq  {x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i\right) } = {x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i + 1\right) }$，其中第一个不等式源于${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$在第$i$次迭代时作为队列中的最大值被弹出。

Otherwise,we have in $G$ exactly one equation of the form ${x}_{i + 1} = {x}_{i} \circledast  z$ ( $z$ may be a EDB coefficient). Now there are two cases depending on the operator:

否则，在$G$中恰好存在形如${x}_{i + 1} = {x}_{i} \circledast  z$的方程（$z$可能是EDB系数）。现根据运算符类型分两种情形：

(1) ${x}_{i + 1} = {x}_{i} \otimes  z$ . By superiority of $\otimes  ,{x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupseteq  {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \otimes  {z}^{\left( i\right) } = {x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i + 1\right) }$ .

(1)${x}_{i + 1} = {x}_{i} \otimes  z$。根据$\otimes  ,{x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupseteq  {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \otimes  {z}^{\left( i\right) } = {x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i + 1\right) }$的优越性。

(2) ${x}_{i + 1} = {x}_{i} \oplus  z$ . If ${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupseteq  {z}^{\left( i\right) }$ ,then ${x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i + 1\right) } = {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$ . Otherwise, ${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsubset  {z}^{\left( i\right) }$ . If $z$ is an IDB variable, we get a contradiction since ${x}_{i}$ is not a max value at the $i$ -th pop. If $z$ is an EDB coefficient, then ${z}^{\left( i - 1\right) } = {z}^{\left( i\right) }$ and thus

(2)${x}_{i + 1} = {x}_{i} \oplus  z$。若${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupseteq  {z}^{\left( i\right) }$，则${x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i + 1\right) } = {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$；否则${x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsubset  {z}^{\left( i\right) }$。若$z$是IDB变量，则因${x}_{i}$不是第$i$次弹出的最大值而产生矛盾。若$z$是EDB系数，则${z}^{\left( i - 1\right) } = {z}^{\left( i\right) }$，故

$$
{x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i\right) } = {x}_{i}^{\left( i - 1\right) } \oplus  {z}^{\left( i - 1\right) } = {x}_{i}^{\left( i - 1\right) } \oplus  {z}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupseteq  {z}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupset  {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }
$$

However, ${x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupset  {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$ is a contradiction since then ${x}_{i + 1}$ would have been the $i$ -th pop instead of ${x}_{i}$ .

然而，${x}_{i + 1}^{\left( i\right) } \sqsupset  {x}_{i}^{\left( i\right) }$构成矛盾，因为此时${x}_{i + 1}$本应是第$i$次弹出而非${x}_{i}$。

Now we are ready to present the proof for Theorem 3.6. In particular, we will use the two lemmas established above (Lemmas B.3 and B.4) to show that the algorithm terminates and returns the desired fixpoint. Then we will analyze its runtime, as predicated in Theorem 3.6.

现在我们将展示定理3.6的证明。具体而言，将运用前述两个引理（引理B.3和B.4）来证明算法会终止并返回期望的不动点。随后将如定理3.6所述分析其运行时间。

Proof of THEOREM 3.6. We show that Algorithm 2 terminates and yields a fixpoint, and by Proposition B.2, we get the least fixpoint for free because the Dijkstra-style while-loop is a special case of asynchronous Kleene chain. First, it is easy to see that Algorithm 2 terminates because the size of $q$ always decreases by 1 (popped IDB variables will not be pushed back into the queue, using $\mathcal{F}$ for bookkeeping of already popped variables).

定理3.6证明。我们证明算法2会终止并产生不动点，根据命题B.2，由于Dijkstra式while循环是异步Kleene链的特例，可直接得到最小不动点。首先易见算法2必然终止，因为$q$的大小每次递减1（被弹出的IDB变量不会重新入队，通过$\mathcal{F}$记录已弹出变量）。

We now show that the returned $h\left( \cdot \right)$ is a fixpoint. To prove the fixpoint,we show that once the queue pops off an IDB variable $x$ as some value $h\left( x\right)$ ,the equation with $x$ at the left-hand side in $G$ , say $x = y \circledast  z$ ,always holds from that point onwards. We prove this by contradiction. Suppose that $x = y \circledast  z$ causes the earliest violation in the underlying asynchronous Kleene chain. By Lemma B.3, the values of $y,z$ are non-decreasing w.r.t. $\sqsubseteq$ . Therefore,the only way to violate $x = y \circledast  z$ is that the value of an IDB variable $y$ (or $z$ ) strictly increases after $x$ is popped off the queue. On the other hand,the queue will eventually pop off that IDB variable ( $y$ or $z$ ) $\exists x$ ,since Algorithm 2 always terminates and $y,z$ can not grow smaller by Lemma B.3. Yet,it is a direct contradiction to Lemma B.4.

现证返回的$h\left( \cdot \right)$是不动点。通过反证法：假设队列将IDB变量$x$作为值$h\left( x\right)$弹出后，$G$中左侧为$x$的方程（记作$x = y \circledast  z$）不再成立。根据引理B.3，$y,z$的值关于$\sqsubseteq$单调不减，故违反$x = y \circledast  z$的唯一可能是某IDB变量$y$（或$z$）在$x$弹出后严格递增。但算法2必然终止，由引理B.3可知$y,z$不会减小，队列终将弹出该IDB变量（$y$或$z$）$\exists x$，这与引理B.4直接矛盾。

Lastly, we provide the runtime analysis. Using similar arguments in the proof of Theorem 3.5, Lines 1-7 of Algorithm 2 can execute in $O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$ time. For its while-loop,each iteration binds an IDB variable $x$ and calls the insert operations of the priority queue $q$ at most $\deg \left( x\right)$ times,where $\deg \left( x\right)$ denotes the number of the equations in $E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$ . A 2-canonical grounding has that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{x \in  E}}\deg \left( x\right)  =$ $2\left| G\right|$ . Using a classic max heap implementation of a priority queue,inserts (or updates) of values can run in $O\left( {\log \left| G\right| }\right)$ time. Thus,the overall runtime is

最后分析运行时。类似定理3.5证明，算法2第1-7行可在$O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$时间内执行。while循环每次迭代绑定一个IDB变量$x$，并调用优先队列$q$的插入操作最多$\deg \left( x\right)$次（$\deg \left( x\right)$表示$E\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$中方程数）。2-标准基化满足$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{x \in  E}}\deg \left( x\right)  =$$2\left| G\right|$。采用经典最大堆实现优先队列时，值插入（或更新）耗时$O\left( {\log \left| G\right| }\right)$，故总运行时间为

$$
O\left( {\left| G\right|  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{x \in  E}}\deg \left( x\right)  \cdot  \log \left| G\right| }\right)  = O\left( {\left| G\right|  \cdot  \log \left| G\right| }\right) .
$$

### C.1 Algorithm for Grounding Rulewise-Acyclic Datalog Programs

### C.1 规则非循环Datalog程序基化算法

The formal algorithm for grounding a rulewise-acyclic Datalog program is given in Algorithm 3. We provide a step-by-step walk-through on Example 5.2 to complement Figure 1.

规则非循环Datalog程序的正式基化算法见算法3。我们将通过示例5.2的逐步演示来补充图1内容。

Example C.1. Recall the acyclic rule presented in Example 5.2:

示例C.1. 回顾示例5.2中的非循环规则：

$$
T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}{T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{24}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{24}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{34}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{34}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{14}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{14}\right) 
$$

We now show how the proof of Theorem 3.2 (or equivalently, the GROUND(   ) method of Algorithm 3) constructs the intermediate rules drawn in Figure 1. For succinctness, we call the nodes in the join tree by their corresponding atoms. Following the rooted join tree in Figure 1, we start from calling GROUND at the root node ${R}_{14}$ ,i.e. GROUND $\left( {{R}_{14},T\left( {x}_{1}\right) }\right)$ :

现在展示定理3.2的证明（等价于算法3的GROUND()方法）如何构建图1中的中间规则。为简洁起见，我们用对应原子命名连接树中的节点。根据图1的有根连接树，我们从根节点${R}_{14}$调用GROUND开始，即GROUND$\left( {{R}_{14},T\left( {x}_{1}\right) }\right)$：

(i) (Refactor) The root node ${R}_{14}$ has two children and thus we introduce two new IDBs ${U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$ for the node ${R}_{24}$ and ${V}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$ for the node ${R}_{34}$ . This corresponding to the following refactoring of the original rule:

(i)（重构）根节点${R}_{14}$有两个子节点，因此我们为节点${R}_{24}$引入新IDB${U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$，为节点${R}_{34}$引入${V}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$。这对应于原始规则的以下重构：

$$
T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}{T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{34}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{14}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right) 
$$

along with two new rules for the intermediate IDBs:

同时为中间IDB新增两条规则：

$$
{U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right) 
$$

$$
{V}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}{R}_{34}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right) .
$$

(ii) (Ground) We ground the refactored rule $T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{4}}{R}_{14}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \otimes  {V}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$ (i.e. the top rule shown in Figure 1) by taking all tuples in the EDB ${R}_{14}$ and that naturally subsumes all possible tuples with non-zero annotations (i.e. ${R}_{14}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \otimes  {V}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \neq$ 0 ). Recall that tuples not in the EDB ${R}_{24}$ has an implicit annotation of 0 and thus do not contribute to the sum-product. This gives a grounding of size $O\left( \left| {R}_{14}\right| \right)  = O\left( m\right)$ .

(ii)（实例化）通过获取EDB${R}_{14}$中所有元组来实例化重构后的规则$T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{4}}{R}_{14}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \otimes  {V}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$（即图1顶部规则），这自然包含所有可能具有非零注释的元组（即${R}_{14}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \otimes  {V}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \neq$0）。注意不在EDB${R}_{24}$中的元组隐含注释为0，因此不影响和积运算。最终得到规模为$O\left( \left| {R}_{14}\right| \right)  = O\left( m\right)$的实例化结果。

(iii) (Recurse) We recursively call Ground on the children nodes,i.e. GROUND $\left( {{R}_{24},{U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$ and GROUND $\left( {{R}_{34},{V}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$ . To avoid repeatence,we only demonstrate GROUND $\left( {{R}_{24},{U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$ next.

(iii)（递归）我们对子节点递归调用Ground方法，即GROUND$\left( {{R}_{24},{U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$和GROUND$\left( {{R}_{34},{V}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$。为避免重复，接下来仅演示GROUND$\left( {{R}_{24},{U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$。

Again,following the join tree rooted at ${R}_{24}$ ,we call $\operatorname{Ground}\left( {{R}_{24},{U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$ :

再次遵循以${R}_{24}$为根的连接树，我们调用$\operatorname{Ground}\left( {{R}_{24},{U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$：

(i) (Refactor) The node ${R}_{24}$ has one child ${T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ and thus we introduce a new IDB ${U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ . This corresponds to the following refactoring of the original rule:

(i)（重构）节点${R}_{24}$有一个子节点${T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$，因此引入新IDB${U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$。这对应于原始规则的以下重构：

$$
{U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right) 
$$

$$
 = {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right) \;\text{(introduce a new IDB)}
$$

算法3：在半环$\sigma$上实例化规则无环的Datalog程序

令$G = \varnothing$；

			foreach sum-prod query $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \in  T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ do

						construct a join tree $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ of $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ rooted at a node $s$ ,where its corresponding

											构建$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$的以节点$s$为根的连接树$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，其中其对应

							atom ${T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$ contains the most head variables;

							atom ${T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }$ 包含最多头部变量；

						let ${H}_{t} \subseteq  H$ be the set of head variables that occur in the subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ rooted at

						设 ${H}_{t} \subseteq  H$ 为在 $\mathcal{T}$ 子树中出现的头部变量集合

							$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ ;

						let $T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  = {U}_{{e}_{\left( \varnothing ,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( \varnothing ,s\right) }}\right)$ ; // ${e}_{\left( \varnothing ,s\right) } = {H}_{s} = H$

						设 $T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  = {U}_{{e}_{\left( \varnothing ,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( \varnothing ,s\right) }}\right)$ ； // ${e}_{\left( \varnothing ,s\right) } = {H}_{s} = H$

						$\operatorname{Ground}\left( {s,{U}_{{e}_{\left( 0,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( 0,s\right) }}\right) }\right)$ ;

Method Ground $\left( {s,{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right) }\right)$ // $r$ is the parent of $s$

方法 Ground $\left( {s,{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right) }\right)$ // $r$ 是 $s$ 的父节点

			// (i) Refactor the subtree rooted at $s$ by introducing one new IDB per child node t

			// (i) 通过为每个子节点t引入一个新IDB来重构以 $s$ 为根的子树

			foreach $\left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ do

			对于每个 $\left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ 执行

						introduce a new IDB ${U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right)$ where ${e}_{\left( s,t\right) } = \left( {\chi \left( s\right)  \cap  \chi \left( t\right) }\right)  \cup  {H}_{t}$ ;

						引入新IDB ${U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right)$ 其中 ${e}_{\left( s,t\right) } = \left( {\chi \left( s\right)  \cap  \chi \left( t\right) }\right)  \cup  {H}_{t}$ ；

			// (ii) Ground the passed-in IDB

				${U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  {e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}}{T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right)$

			foreach ${\mathbf{c}}_{\chi \left( s\right) } \in  {T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)$ do $//O\left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right)$

			对于每个 ${\mathbf{c}}_{\chi \left( s\right) } \in  {T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)$ 执行 $//O\left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right)$

						foreach ${\mathbf{c}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) } \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  \chi \left( s\right) }\right)$ do $//O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$

																	若 ${U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {{\Pi }_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}{\mathbf{c}}_{S}}\right)  \notin  G$ 则 $//{\Pi }_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}{\mathbf{c}}_{S}$ 是 ${\mathbf{c}}_{S}$ 到 ${\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}$ 的投影

												insert into $G$ a new grounded rule

																							向 $G$ 插入一条新接地规则

													${U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {{\Pi }_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}{\mathbf{c}}_{S}}\right)  \leftarrow  {T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {{\Pi }_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}{\mathbf{c}}_{S}}\right) ;$

									else

																							通过以下方式向 ${U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {{\Pi }_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}{\mathbf{c}}_{\operatorname{vars}\left( {\phi }_{s}\right) }}\right)$ 的主体添加

													${T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {{\Pi }_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}{\mathbf{c}}_{S}}\right) ;$

			// (iii) Recursively call Ground on each child node $t$ (pass in the new IDB ${U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}$ )

			foreach $\left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ do

						$\operatorname{Ground}\left( {t,{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right) }\right)$ ;

along with a new rule for the intermediate IDB:

同时为中间IDB新增规则：

$$
{U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \leftarrow  {T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right) .
$$

(ii) (Ground) We ground the refactored rule ${U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ by taking all tuples in the EDB ${R}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)$ . This gives a grounding of size $O\left( \left| {R}_{24}\right| \right)  = O\left( m\right)$ .

(ii) (Ground) 通过获取EDB${R}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)$中的所有元组，对重构后的规则${U}_{4}\left( {x}_{4}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$进行实例化。这将生成规模为$O\left( \left| {R}_{24}\right| \right)  = O\left( m\right)$的实例。

(iii) (Recurse) We recursively call Ground $\left( {{T}_{2},{U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right)$ next.

(iii) (Recurse) 接下来我们递归调用Ground函数$\left( {{T}_{2},{U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right)$。

Finally,we call $\operatorname{Ground}\left( {{T}_{2},{U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right)$ . However,since ${T}_{2}$ is a leaf node,there is no refactoring and further recursion. We simply ground the rule: ${U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \leftarrow  {T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ by taking all values in the active domain of ${x}_{2}$ . This gives a grounding of size $O\left( {n\text{ (recall }n \leq  m}\right)$ .

最后调用$\operatorname{Ground}\left( {{T}_{2},{U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right)$。由于${T}_{2}$是叶节点，无需重构和继续递归。我们仅通过获取${x}_{2}$活跃域中的所有值来实例化规则：${U}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \leftarrow  {T}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$，生成规模为$O\left( {n\text{ (recall }n \leq  m}\right)$的实例。

### C.2 Two Additional Applications of Theorem 3.2

### C.2 定理3.2的两个附加应用

We show two applications (Example C.2 and Example C.3) to illustrate the grounding technique for acyclic Datalog programs in Theorem 3.2.

通过示例C.2和示例C.3，我们将展示定理3.2中无环Datalog程序的实例化技术应用。

Example C.2. Andersen's analysis can be captured by the following acyclic Datalog program of arity 2 :

示例C.2. Andersen指针分析可通过以下元数为2的无环Datalog程序表示：

$$
T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \leftarrow  \operatorname{AddressOf}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \vee  \left( {\mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{x}_{3}}T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right)  \land  \operatorname{Assign}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{2}}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 \vee  \left( {\mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{\mathbf{x}}_{34}}T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \land  T\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{3}}\right)  \land  \operatorname{Load}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{2}}\right) }\right)  \vee  \left( {\mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{\mathbf{x}}_{34}}T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \land  \operatorname{Store}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{3}}\right)  \land  T\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) }\right) .
$$

By Theorem 3.2,we can obtain an equivalent grounded Datalog program of size $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ in time $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ since for binary EDB predicates, $O\left( {m + {n}^{2}}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ .

根据定理3.2，我们可在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内获得一个规模为$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$的等价接地Datalog程序，因为对于二元EDB谓词，$O\left( {m + {n}^{2}}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$。

Next,we ground an acyclic Datalog Program for the language ${a}^{p}{b}^{p}{c}^{p}$ ,where $p \geq  1$ . Note that this language is not context-free.

接下来，我们为语言${a}^{p}{b}^{p}{c}^{p}$接地一个非循环Datalog程序，其中$p \geq  1$。需注意该语言不是上下文无关的。

Example C.3. Suppose we have a directed labeled graph, where each directed edge is labeled as $a,b$ ,or $c$ (possibly more than one labels for a single edge). We construct a Datalog program over the natural number semiring $\mathbb{N} = \left( {\mathbb{N},+,\cdot ,0,1}\right)$ to compute: for every pair of node $\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{c}}\right)$ in this graph,the number of directed labeled paths from ${x}_{a}$ to ${y}_{c}$ that satisfies the expression ${a}^{p}{b}^{p}{c}^{p}$ for $p \geq  1$ . The target in the following program (of arity 6) is the IDB $Q$ and the EDB predicates are $a,b,c$ ,where for each tuple $\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a}}\right)$ in the EDB $a$ ,its natural number annotation $a\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a}}\right)  = 1$ (since it means that there is a directed edge from node ${x}_{a}$ to node ${y}_{a}$ labeled as $a$ in the graph),and similarly for EDBs $b$ and $c$ . Tuples absent from the EDB predicates are assumed to have annotation 0 (i.e. no such labeled edge in the graph). The program is as follows:

示例C.3. 假设我们有一个带标签的有向图，其中每条有向边被标记为$a,b$或$c$（单条边可能具有多个标签）。我们在自然数半环$\mathbb{N} = \left( {\mathbb{N},+,\cdot ,0,1}\right)$上构造一个Datalog程序来计算：对于图中每对节点$\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{c}}\right)$，从${x}_{a}$到${y}_{c}$且满足表达式${a}^{p}{b}^{p}{c}^{p}$（针对$p \geq  1$）的带标签有向路径数量。下述程序（元数为6）中的目标是IDB$Q$，EDB谓词为$a,b,c$，其中对于EDB$a$中的每个元组$\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a}}\right)$，其自然数标注为$a\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a}}\right)  = 1$（因为这意味着图中存在一条从节点${x}_{a}$到节点${y}_{a}$、标记为$a$的有向边），EDB$b$和$c$同理。EDB谓词中不存在的元组默认标注为0（即图中不存在该标记边）。程序如下：

$$
T\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{x}_{b},{y}_{b},{x}_{c},{y}_{c}}\right)  \leftarrow  a\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a}}\right)  \cdot  b\left( {{x}_{b},{y}_{b}}\right)  \cdot  c\left( {{x}_{c},{y}_{c}}\right)  + 
$$

$$
\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{y}_{a}^{\prime },{y}_{b}^{\prime },{y}_{c}^{\prime }}}T\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a}^{\prime },{x}_{b},{y}_{b}^{\prime },{x}_{c},{y}_{c}^{\prime }}\right)  \cdot  a\left( {{y}_{a}^{\prime },{y}_{a}}\right)  \cdot  b\left( {{y}_{b}^{\prime },{y}_{b}}\right)  \cdot  c\left( {{y}_{c}^{\prime },{y}_{c}}\right) }\right) .
$$

$$
{T}_{\text{path }}\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{y}_{b},{y}_{c}}\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{x}_{b},{y}_{b},{x}_{c},{y}_{c}}\right) ,{y}_{a} = {x}_{b},{y}_{b} = {x}_{c}\text{.}
$$

$$
Q\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{c}}\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{y}_{a},{y}_{b}}}{T}_{\text{path }}\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{y}_{b},{y}_{c}}\right) .
$$

We will use our grounding method to bound the cost of evaluating this Datalog program. The third rule obviously has a grounding of size ${n}^{4}$ . For the second rule that involves selection predicates, one can rewrite it as the acyclic rule

我们将使用接地方法来限定评估该Datalog程序的成本。第三条规则的接地规模显然为${n}^{4}$。对于涉及选择谓词的第二条规则，可将其重写为非循环规则

$$
{T}_{\text{path }}\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{y}_{b},{y}_{c}}\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{x}_{b},{y}_{b},{x}_{c},{y}_{c}}\right) ,I\left( {{y}_{a},{x}_{b}}\right) ,I\left( {{y}_{b},{x}_{c}}\right) .
$$

where $I\left( {y,x}\right)$ are new EDB predicates that represent the identity function over the active domain, i.e. $I\left( {y,x}\right)  = 1$ if $y = x$ and 0 otherwise. Hence, $I\left( {{y}_{a},{x}_{b}}\right)$ and $I\left( {{y}_{b},{x}_{c}}\right)$ are of size $O\left( n\right)$ and this rule has a grounding of size $O\left( {{n}^{2} \cdot  n \cdot  n}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{4}\right)$ .

其中$I\left( {y,x}\right)$是表示活跃域上恒等函数的新EDB谓词，即当$y = x$时$I\left( {y,x}\right)  = 1$，否则为0。因此，$I\left( {{y}_{a},{x}_{b}}\right)$和$I\left( {{y}_{b},{x}_{c}}\right)$的规模为$O\left( n\right)$，该规则的接地规模为$O\left( {{n}^{2} \cdot  n \cdot  n}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{4}\right)$。

Now we ground the first rule (also acyclic) as follows: the number of groundings of the first sum-prod query $a\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a}}\right)  \cdot  b\left( {{x}_{b},{y}_{b}}\right)  \cdot  c\left( {{x}_{c},{y}_{c}}\right)$ is $m \cdot  {n}^{4}$ . For the second sum-prod query,we can decompose it as follows:

现在我们对第一条规则（同样是非循环的）进行如下接地：第一个求和-积查询$a\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a}}\right)  \cdot  b\left( {{x}_{b},{y}_{b}}\right)  \cdot  c\left( {{x}_{c},{y}_{c}}\right)$的接地数量为$m \cdot  {n}^{4}$。对于第二个求和-积查询，可作如下分解：

$$
{T}_{1}\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{x}_{b},{y}_{b}^{\prime },{x}_{c},{y}_{c}^{\prime }}\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y}_{a}^{\prime }}T\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a}^{\prime },{x}_{b},{y}_{b}^{\prime },{x}_{c},{y}_{c}^{\prime }}\right)  \cdot  a\left( {{y}_{a}^{\prime },{y}_{a}}\right) .
$$

$$
{T}_{2}\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{x}_{b},{y}_{b},{x}_{c},{y}_{c}^{\prime }}\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y}_{b}^{\prime }}{T}_{1}\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{x}_{b},{y}_{b}^{\prime },{x}_{c},{y}_{c}^{\prime }}\right)  \cdot  b\left( {{y}_{b}^{\prime },{y}_{b}}\right) .
$$

$$
T\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{x}_{b},{y}_{b},{x}_{c},{y}_{c}}\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y}_{c}^{\prime }}{T}_{2}\left( {{x}_{a},{y}_{a},{x}_{b},{y}_{b},{x}_{c},{y}_{c}^{\prime }}\right)  \cdot  c\left( {{y}_{c}^{\prime },{y}_{c}}\right) .
$$

It is easy to see that the groundings for each rule are bounded by $m \cdot  {n}^{5}$ . Hence,the overall grounding is of size $O\left( {{n}^{4} + m \cdot  {n}^{4} + m \cdot  {n}^{5}}\right)  = O\left( {m \cdot  {n}^{5}}\right)$ .

易见每条规则的接地规模上限为$m \cdot  {n}^{5}$。因此，总体接地规模为$O\left( {{n}^{4} + m \cdot  {n}^{4} + m \cdot  {n}^{5}}\right)  = O\left( {m \cdot  {n}^{5}}\right)$。

### C.3 Rulewise free-connex acyclic Datalog programs

### C.3 规则级自由连接非循环Datalog程序

Proof of Proposition 5.3. The construction is similar to that of Algorithm 3, except that we root the join tree at the node $r$ ,where the tree is free-connex w.r.t. $r$ . Now,we give a more fine-grained argument on the grounding size at Line 12-20 of Algorithm 3 when Ground $\left( {s,{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right) }\right)$ is called. Recall that we want to ground the intermediate rule

命题5.3的证明。该构造过程与算法3类似，不同之处在于我们将连接树根植于节点$r$，该树相对于$r$是自由连接的。现在，我们将对算法3第12-20行在调用Ground$\left( {s,{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right) }\right)$时的基础化规模给出更细粒度的论证。需注意我们需要对中间规则进行基础化

$$
{U}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( r,s\right) }}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{S \smallsetminus  H}}{T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{t : \left( {s,t}\right)  \in  E\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }{U}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{e}_{\left( s,t\right) }}\right) 
$$

where ${e}_{\left( r,s\right) } = \left( {\chi \left( r\right)  \cap  \chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \cup  {H}_{s}$ . Follow the proof of Theorem 3.2,it suffices to cover $\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s}$ and for that, we split into the following two cases:

其中${e}_{\left( r,s\right) } = \left( {\chi \left( r\right)  \cap  \chi \left( s\right) }\right)  \cup  {H}_{s}$。根据定理3.2的证明，只需覆盖$\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s}$即可，为此我们分为以下两种情况：

- If $s \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ contains only head variables (i.e. $\chi \left( s\right)  \subseteq  H$ ). Then, $\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s} \subseteq  H$ and taking all instantiations in the active domain of $\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s}$ suffices to ground the body for the above rule. There are $O\left( {n}^{k}\right)$ such instantiations since $\left| H\right|  \leq  k$ .

- 若$s \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$仅包含头部变量（即$\chi \left( s\right)  \subseteq  H$）。则$\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s} \subseteq  H$，且在$\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s}$的活跃域中获取所有实例化即可满足上述规则体的基础化。由于$\left| H\right|  \leq  k$，共存在$O\left( {n}^{k}\right)$个此类实例化。

- Otherwise, $s$ contains at least one non-head variable (say $i$ ). Then, ${H}_{s} \subseteq  \chi \left( s\right)$ since if there is a head variable $j \notin  \chi \left( s\right)$ ,but it occurs in the subtree rooted at $s$ ,then ${\mathrm{{TOP}}}_{r}\left( i\right)$ must be an ancestor of ${\mathrm{{TOP}}}_{r}\left( j\right)$ ,which contradicts the fact that the join tree is free-connex w.r.t. $r$ . Thus, $\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s} = \chi \left( s\right)$ and we can trivially cover $\chi \left( s\right)$ by $O\left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right)$ groundings.

- 否则，$s$至少包含一个非头部变量（设为$i$）。则${H}_{s} \subseteq  \chi \left( s\right)$，因为若存在头部变量$j \notin  \chi \left( s\right)$但出现在以$s$为根的子树中，那么${\mathrm{{TOP}}}_{r}\left( i\right)$必是${\mathrm{{TOP}}}_{r}\left( j\right)$的祖先，这与连接树相对于$r$自由连接的性质矛盾。因此$\chi \left( s\right)  \cup  {H}_{s} = \chi \left( s\right)$，我们可以通过$O\left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right)$次基础化简单覆盖$\chi \left( s\right)$。

For both cases,the size of the groundings inserted into $G$ for the intermediate rule is $O\left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right)$ .

两种情况下，为中间规则插入$G$的基础化规模均为$O\left( {m + {n}^{k}}\right)$。

### C.4 Linear rulewise-acyclic Datalog programs of arity at most 2

### C.4 元数不超过2的线性规则无环Datalog程序

Proof of Proposition 5.4. We illustrate the grounding technique for a single acyclic sum-prod $\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$ containing at most one IDB. If $\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$ has no IDB (so essentially a sum-prod query),then there is a grounding of size $O\left( {n \cdot  m}\right)$ that can be constructed in time $O\left( {n \cdot  m}\right)$ ,since now every atom has a grounding of $O\left( m\right)$ instead of $O\left( {m + {n}^{2}}\right)$ in the proof of Theorem 3.3. Thus,we only analyze the case where $\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$ contains exactly one binary IDB $T$ . We also assume that no EDB atom contains both variables of $T$ (otherwise,we can ground the IDB $T$ by $O\left( m\right)$ and reduce back to the sum-prod query case).

命题5.4的证明。我们针对仅含最多一个IDB的单个无环和积$\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$说明基础化技术。若$\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$不含IDB（实质为和积查询），则可构造规模为$O\left( {n \cdot  m}\right)$的基础化，耗时$O\left( {n \cdot  m}\right)$，因为此时每个原子的基础化规模为$O\left( m\right)$而非定理3.3证明中的$O\left( {m + {n}^{2}}\right)$。因此我们仅分析$\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$恰好含有一个二元IDB$T$的情况，并假设没有EDB原子同时包含$T$的两个变量（否则可通过$O\left( m\right)$对IDB$T$进行基础化，回归到和积查询情形）。

Similar to Algorithm 3,we construct a join tree $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ rooted at a node $r$ containing the head variable $x$ . If now the IDB $T$ is a leaf node,then we simply follow Algorithm 3 to get a grounding of size $O\left( {m \cdot  n + {n}^{2}}\right)  = O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ . Otherwise,let ${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$ be the subtree rooted at $t$ ,where $\chi \left( t\right)$ corresponds to the IDB atom $T$ ,and ${H}_{t}$ be the set of head variables that occur in ${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$ . If $y \in  \chi \left( t\right)$ ,or if $y \notin  {H}_{t}$ , then we subsitute ${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$ in $\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$ by a new IDB ${T}^{\prime }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( t\right) }\right)$ and ground a new free-connex acyclic rule

类似于算法3，我们构建一棵以节点$r$为根的连接树$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，该节点包含头变量$x$。若此时IDB$T$为叶节点，则直接沿用算法3获得规模为$O\left( {m \cdot  n + {n}^{2}}\right)  = O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$的接地。否则，设${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$为以$t$为根的子树（其中$\chi \left( t\right)$对应IDB原子$T$），${H}_{t}$为出现在${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$中的头变量集合。若$y \in  \chi \left( t\right)$或$y \notin  {H}_{t}$成立，则将$\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$中的${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$替换为新IDB${T}^{\prime }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( t\right) }\right)$，并接地新的自由连接无环规则

$$
{T}^{\prime }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( t\right) }\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \chi \left( t\right) }}{\bigotimes }_{s \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{t}\right) }{T}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( s\right) }\right) . \tag{9}
$$

By Theorem 5.3,we can construct for this rule a grounding of size $O\left( {m + {n}^{2}}\right)$ in time $O\left( {m + {n}^{2}}\right)$ . Then,we reduce back to the previous case when we ground $\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$ since the only IDB ${T}^{\prime }$ becomes a leaf node.

根据定理5.3，我们可在$O\left( {m + {n}^{2}}\right)$时间内为该规则构建规模为$O\left( {m + {n}^{2}}\right)$的接地。随后，当对$\varphi \left( {x,y}\right)$进行接地时，由于唯一IDB${T}^{\prime }$变为叶节点，问题即简化为前一种情形。

The trickier case is when $y \in  {H}_{t} \smallsetminus  \chi \left( t\right)$ . First,we identify the joining variable between $T$ and its parent atom. Observe that there can not be more than one join variables because we assumed that no atom contains $\chi \left( t\right)$ . There are two cases: $x \in  \chi \left( T\right)$ ,then $x$ must be the join variable since $x \in  \chi \left( r\right)$ ,i.e.,the IDB is $T\left( {x,w}\right)$ ; or $x \notin  \chi \left( T\right)$ (hence $x \notin  {H}_{t}$ ). Let the IDB be $T\left( {z,w}\right)$ ,and w.l.o.g., let $z$ be the only join variable with its parent EDB.

更复杂的情形是当$y \in  {H}_{t} \smallsetminus  \chi \left( t\right)$时。首先确定$T$与其父原子间的连接变量。注意连接变量不超过一个，因我们假设不存在包含$\chi \left( t\right)$的原子。两种情形：若$x \in  \chi \left( T\right)$，则$x$必为连接变量（因$x \in  \chi \left( r\right)$，即该IDB为$T\left( {x,w}\right)$）；或$x \notin  \chi \left( T\right)$（故$x \notin  {H}_{t}$）。设该IDB为$T\left( {z,w}\right)$，且不妨令$z$为与其父EDB唯一的连接变量。

We only analyze the $T\left( {z,w}\right)$ case. The $T\left( {x,w}\right)$ case is identical. We find the node ${t}_{y} = {\mathrm{{TOP}}}_{r}\left( y\right)$ in ${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$ and the path from $t$ to ${t}_{y}$ . First,by the exact substitution as (9),we can construct a grounding of size $O\left( m\right)$ in time $O\left( m\right)$ for ${\mathcal{T}}_{{t}_{y}}$ (i.e.,the subtree rooted at $\left. {t}_{y}\right)$ and replace it with a new EDB ${R}_{\chi \left( {t}_{u}\right) }^{\prime }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( {t}_{y}\right) }\right)$ . Then,for every edge $\left( {p,q}\right)  \in  E\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{t}\right)$ along the path from $t$ to ${t}_{y}$ ,we substitute the EDB ${R}_{\chi \left( p\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( p\right) }\right)$ corresponds to $p$ by a new EDB ${R}_{\chi \left( p\right) }^{\prime }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( p\right) }\right)$ via the following free-connex acyclic sum-prod query

我们仅分析$T\left( {z,w}\right)$情形，$T\left( {x,w}\right)$情形同理。在${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$中定位节点${t}_{y} = {\mathrm{{TOP}}}_{r}\left( y\right)$及从$t$到${t}_{y}$的路径。首先通过精确替换如式(9)，可在$O\left( m\right)$时间内为${\mathcal{T}}_{{t}_{y}}$（即以$\left. {t}_{y}\right)$为根的子树）构建规模为$O\left( m\right)$的接地，并用新EDB${R}_{\chi \left( {t}_{u}\right) }^{\prime }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( {t}_{y}\right) }\right)$替换之。随后，对于从$t$到${t}_{y}$路径上的每条边$\left( {p,q}\right)  \in  E\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{t}\right)$，通过以下自由连接无环求和积查询，将与$p$对应的EDB${R}_{\chi \left( p\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( p\right) }\right)$替换为新EDB${R}_{\chi \left( p\right) }^{\prime }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( p\right) }\right)$

where the body has no IDB. Thus,the rule has a grounding of size $O\left( m\right)$ obtained in $O\left( m\right)$ time. Such substitutions prune the subtree ${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$ into a path from $t$ to ${t}_{y}$ . Thus we introduce another IDB ${T}^{\prime }\left( {z,y}\right)$ and the linear rule whose body contains only atoms on the path (i.e. $T\left( {z,w}\right)  \otimes  \cdots  \otimes$ ${R}_{\chi \left( {t}_{y}\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( {t}_{y}\right) }\right) )$ . Using a join-project style rewritting,it is easy to see that the rule for ${T}^{\prime }\left( {z,y}\right)$ has a grounding of size $O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ that can be obtained in time $O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ . Finally,we collapse the subtree ${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$ again into a single atom ${T}^{\prime }\left( {z,y}\right)$ at the leaf. Following Algorithm 3,we get a grounding of size $O\left( {m \cdot  n + {n}^{2}}\right)  = O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ .

当规则体不含IDB时，该规则的实例化规模为$O\left( m\right)$，可在$O\left( m\right)$时间内完成。这类替换将子树${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$修剪为从$t$到${t}_{y}$的路径。为此我们引入新的IDB${T}^{\prime }\left( {z,y}\right)$及线性规则，其规则体仅包含路径上的原子（即$T\left( {z,w}\right)  \otimes  \cdots  \otimes$${R}_{\chi \left( {t}_{y}\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( {t}_{y}\right) }\right) )$）。采用连接-投影式重写方法，显然${T}^{\prime }\left( {z,y}\right)$对应的规则可在$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$时间内生成规模为$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$的实例化。最终我们将子树${\mathcal{T}}_{t}$再次折叠为叶节点上的单个原子${T}^{\prime }\left( {z,y}\right)$。根据算法3，得到规模为$O\left( {m \cdot  n + {n}^{2}}\right)  = O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$的实例化。

Next, we demonstrate the application of Proposition 5.4 and how it leads to an improvement over directly applying Algorithm 3.

接下来我们将展示命题5.4的应用，以及它如何实现比直接应用算法3更优的效果。

Example C.4. Consider the following linear rule (essentially a chain query) of a Datalog program with the only IDB $T\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$ in the body:

示例C.4. 考虑以下Datalog程序的线性规则（本质上是链式查询），其规则体中唯一的IDB是$T\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$：

$$
T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{5}}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}{R}_{12}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \otimes  T\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{34}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{45}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{5}}\right) .
$$

Suppose that we elect ${R}_{12}$ as the root bag for the join tree shown in Figure C.4. Algorithm 3 running the join tree is suboptimal: the grounding of the rule ${T}_{25}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}T\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{35}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)$ requires $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ since every possible constant combination of $\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)$ in the active domain must be considered from $T\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{35}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)$ .

假设选择${R}_{12}$作为图C.4所示连接树的根节点包。直接运行算法3处理该连接树并非最优：规则${T}_{25}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}T\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{35}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)$的实例化需要$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$，因为必须考虑来自$T\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{35}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)$的活跃域中$\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)$所有可能的常量组合。

In constrast, Proposition 5.4 avoids the situation where an intermediate rule contains more than one IDB in the body. The algorithm runs in a top-down fashion first from the IDB node and constructs the grounding for ${T}_{25}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)$ via the following:

相比之下，命题5.4避免了中间规则体包含多个IDB的情况。该算法采用自顶向下方式，首先从IDB节点出发，通过以下方式构建${T}_{25}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)$的实例化：

$$
{T}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{3}}T\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{34}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) 
$$

$$
{T}_{25}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{4}}{T}_{24}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{45}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{5}}\right) .
$$

Now both intermediate rules have a grounding of $O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ . Finally,we ground the rule $T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{5}}\right)  \leftarrow$ ${\bigoplus }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{12}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{25}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)$ in $O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$ time and size. Indeed,one can check that it is an $\sigma$ - equivalent rewriting of the program since

现在两条中间规则的实例化规模均为$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$。最终我们在$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$时间内完成规则$T\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{5}}\right)  \leftarrow$${\bigoplus }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{12}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \otimes  {T}_{25}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)$的实例化，其规模同样为$O\left( {m \cdot  n}\right)$。实际上可以验证这是该程序的$\sigma$等价重写，因为
