## D LOWER BOUNDS

## D 下界证明

In this section, we will prove the lower bounds stated in Theorem 3.4. Our lower bounds are based on the following proposition, which constructs a class of Datalog programs with the appropriate width that decides whether a undirected graph $G$ contains a clique.

本节将证明定理3.4所述下界。我们的下界证明基于以下命题，该命题构造了一类具有特定宽度的Datalog程序，用于判定无向图$G$是否包含团。

Proposition D.1. Take an integer $k \geq  2$ and a rational number $w \geq  1$ such that $k \cdot  w$ is an integer. There is a (non-recursive) Datalog program $P$ with arity $k$ such that $\operatorname{subw}\left( P\right)  \leq  w$ ,and the target Boolean IDB predicate $Q\left( \right)$ for $P$ decides whether a undirected graph $G$ has a clique of size $\ell  = \left( {k - 1}\right)  + k \cdot  w$

命题D.1 设整数$k \geq  2$和有理数$w \geq  1$满足$k \cdot  w$为整数。存在一个（非递归的）Datalog程序$P$，其元数为$k$，使得$\operatorname{subw}\left( P\right)  \leq  w$，且该程序的布尔目标IDB谓词$Q\left( \right)$可判定无向图$G$是否包含大小为$\ell  = \left( {k - 1}\right)  + k \cdot  w$的团。

Proof. Let $R\left( {x,y}\right)$ be the binary predicate that represents the edges of $G$ ,and $V\left( x\right)$ be the unary predicate that represents the nodes of $G$ . It will be helpful to distinguish the $\ell$ variables of the clique we want to compute into $k$ variables ${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}$ and the remaining $\ell  - k$ variables ${y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}$ . Note that $\ell  - k = k \cdot  w - 1 \geq  2 - 1 = 1$ ,so there is at least one $y$ -variable.

证明 令$R\left( {x,y}\right)$为表示$G$边集的二元谓词，$V\left( x\right)$为表示$G$节点集的一元谓词。将待计算团的$\ell$个变量划分为$k$个变量${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}$与剩余$\ell  - k$个变量${y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}$将有助于证明。注意到$\ell  - k = k \cdot  w - 1 \geq  2 - 1 = 1$，故至少存在一个$y$变量。

Initially,we compute all cliques of size $k$ in the graph:

初始阶段，我们计算图中所有大小为$k$的团：

$$
T\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k}}\right)  \leftarrow  V\left( {z}_{1}\right)  \otimes  V\left( {z}_{2}\right)  \otimes  \cdots  \otimes  V\left( {z}_{k}\right) .
$$

$$
C\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k}}\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k}}\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{1 \leq  i < j \leq  k}R\left( {{z}_{i},{z}_{j}}\right) .
$$

Both rules are acyclic (so their submodular width is 1) and the arity of the two intensional predicates we introduce is $k$ . Next,we introduce the following rule:

两条规则均为无环规则（故其次模宽度为1），且引入的两个内涵谓词的元数为$k$。接着引入如下规则：

$$
S\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{y}_{1},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}}{U}_{1}\left\lbrack  {{x}_{1},{y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}}\right\rbrack   \otimes  {U}_{2}\left\lbrack  {{x}_{2},{y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}}\right\rbrack   \otimes  \cdots  \otimes  {U}_{k}\left\lbrack  {{x}_{k},{y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}}\right\rbrack  .
$$

where ${U}_{i}\left\lbrack  {{x}_{i},{y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}}\right\rbrack$ stands for the product of the following sequence of atoms:

其中${U}_{i}\left\lbrack  {{x}_{i},{y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}}\right\rbrack$代表以下原子序列的乘积：

$$
\left\{  {C\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k}}\right)  \mid  {z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k}\text{ are all possible sets of size }k\text{ from }\left\{  {{x}_{i},{y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}}\right\}  }\right\}  
$$

Note that this is well-defined,since $\ell  - k + 1 = k \cdot  w$ and $w \geq  1$ . We will argue that the submodular width of this rule for $S\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$ is $w$ . The idea is that every tree decomposition has the exact same set of bags: $\left\{  {{x}_{i},{y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}}\right\}$ for $i = 1,\ldots \ell$ . This follows from [7] and the fact that the clique-graph of the hypergraph is chordal (i.e. any cycle in the clique-graph of length greater than 3 has a chord).

注意该定义是良定的，因为$\ell  - k + 1 = k \cdot  w$且$w \geq  1$。我们将论证该规则对于$S\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)$的次模宽度为$w$。关键在于每个树分解都具有完全相同的袋集合：对$i = 1,\ldots \ell$而言是$\left\{  {{x}_{i},{y}_{1},{y}_{2},\ldots ,{y}_{\ell  - k}}\right\}$。这源于文献[7]及超图的团图是弦图的性质（即团图中长度大于3的任何环均存在弦）。

Now,each bag has $\ell  - k + 1$ variables. Pick a set of $\ell  - k + 1$ hyperedges (of size $k$ ) such that each variable is covered exactly $k$ times (this selection is possible because every possible $k$ -sized hyperedge is in the hypergraph). By assigning a weight of $1/k$ to any such hyperedge,we have a fractional edge cover of size $\left( {\ell  - k + 1}\right) /k = w$ .

现在，每个袋包含$\ell  - k + 1$个变量。选取$\ell  - k + 1$条（大小为$k$的）超边，使得每个变量恰好被覆盖$k$次（该选择是可行的，因为所有可能的$k$大小超边均存在于超图中）。通过为每条此类超边分配$1/k$权重，我们得到大小为$\left( {\ell  - k + 1}\right) /k = w$的分数边覆盖。

Finally,we need to check that each tuple in $S$ forms an $k$ -clique,which can be done through the following rule:

最后，需验证$S$中每个元组是否构成$k$团，这可通过以下规则实现：

$$
Q\left( \right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}S\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{1 \leq  i < j \leq  k}R\left( {{x}_{i},{x}_{j}}\right) .
$$

Observe that the last rule is also acyclic, so its submodular width is 1 .

注意到末条规则同样是无环的，故其次模宽度为1。

### D.1 Missing Proof for Part (1) of Theorem 3.4

### D.1 定理3.4第(1)部分缺失证明

Provenance Polynomials. Consider a non-recursive Datalog program $P$ over a semiring $\sigma$ with a single Boolean target $Q\left( \right)$ . Then,we can generate a provenance polynomial for $P$ by interpreting each EDB fact $R\left( \mathbf{c}\right)$ as a variable ${x}_{\mathbf{c}}$ . We call this the provenance polynomial of $P$ . The non-recursive condition guarantees that the provenance polynomial is finite.

起源多项式。考虑半环$\sigma$上具有单一布尔目标$Q\left( \right)$的非递归Datalog程序$P$。通过将每个EDB事实$R\left( \mathbf{c}\right)$解释为变量${x}_{\mathbf{c}}$，可为$P$生成起源多项式。我们称此为该程序的起源多项式。非递归条件保证了该起源多项式是有限的。

Circuits over Semirings. We define a circuit $F$ over a semiring $\sigma$ to be a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) with input nodes (with fan-in 0 ) variables ${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}$ and the constants $\mathbf{0},\mathbf{1} \in  D$ . Every other node is labelled by $\oplus$ or $\otimes$ and has fan-in 2 ; these nodes are called $\oplus$ -gates and $\otimes$ -gates respectively. An output gate of $F$ is any gate with fanout 0 . The size of the circuit $F$ ,denoted $\left| F\right|$ ,is the number of gates in $F$ . We say that $F$ evaluates a polynomial $f$ over the semiring $\sigma$ if for any input values that the variables ${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}$ take over the domain $D$ of the semiring,the output value of the circuit is the corresponding value of $f$ .

半环上的电路。我们定义半环$\sigma$上的电路$F$为具有输入节点（扇入为0）的有向无环图(DAG)，这些节点对应变量${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}$和常量$\mathbf{0},\mathbf{1} \in  D$。其余节点标记为$\oplus$或$\otimes$且扇入为2，分别称为$\oplus$门和$\otimes$门。电路$F$的输出门是任何扇出为0的门。电路$F$的规模记作$\left| F\right|$，即其中门的数量。当变量${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}$在域$D$上取值时，若电路输出值等于多项式$f$的对应值，则称该电路在半环$\sigma$上计算该多项式。

Proposition D.2. Let $G$ be a non-recursive grounded Datalog program over a semiring $\sigma$ with a single Boolean target $Q\left( \right)$ . Then,there is a circuit $F$ over $\sigma$ that evaluates the provenance polynomial of $G$ and $F$ has size $O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$ .

命题D.2。设$G$是半环$\sigma$上具有单一布尔目标$Q\left( \right)$的非递归接地Datalog程序，则存在计算$G$溯源多项式的半环电路$F$，其规模为$O\left( \left| G\right| \right)$。

Proof. Given $G$ ,we will construct a semiring circuit $F$ as follows. Every EDB fact ${R}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$ maps to a distinct input gate ${z}_{{R}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) }$ of the circuit. For every IDB fact ${S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$ ,we introduce an $\oplus$ -gate ${z}_{{S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) }$ . Take any rule $r$ with head ${S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$ and body ${T}_{{J}_{1}}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{J}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{T}_{{J}_{k}}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{J}_{k}}\right)$ . We then create a subcircuit of the form

证明。给定$G$，按如下方式构造半环电路$F$：每个EDB事实${R}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$映射为电路的独立输入门${z}_{{R}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) }$；对每个IDB事实${S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$，引入$\oplus$门${z}_{{S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) }$。对于以${S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$为头部、${T}_{{J}_{1}}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{J}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{T}_{{J}_{k}}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{J}_{k}}\right)$为体的规则$r$，构建形如

$$
\left( {\left( {\left( {{z}_{{T}_{{J}_{1}}}\left( {\mathrm{c}}_{{J}_{1}}\right)  \otimes  {z}_{{T}_{{J}_{2}}\left( {\mathrm{c}}_{{J}_{2}}\right) }}\right)  \otimes  {z}_{{T}_{{J}_{3}}\left( {\mathrm{c}}_{{J}_{3}}\right) }}\right) \ldots }\right) 
$$

and name the top gate of this subcircuit as ${y}_{{S}_{J}\left( {c}_{J}\right) }^{r}$ . Let ${r}_{1},{r}_{2},\ldots ,{r}_{s}$ be the rules with the same head ${S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$ . Then,we make ${z}_{{S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) }$ be the top gate of the subcircuit

的子电路，并将其顶层门命名为${y}_{{S}_{J}\left( {c}_{J}\right) }^{r}$。设${r}_{1},{r}_{2},\ldots ,{r}_{s}$为具有相同头部${S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$的规则，则令${z}_{{S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) }$作为子电路

$$
\left( {{y}_{{S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) }^{{r}_{1}} \oplus  {y}_{{S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) }^{{r}_{2}}}\right)  \oplus  {y}_{{S}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) }^{{r}_{3}}\cdots 
$$

The gate corresponding to ${z}_{q\left( \right) }$ is the output gate.

的顶层门。对应${z}_{q\left( \right) }$的门即为输出门。

We now bound the size of the circuit $F$ . Note that for every rule with $k$ atoms in the body we introduce $\left( {k - 1}\right)  \otimes$ -gates and one $\oplus$ -gate. This concludes the proof.

现界定电路$F$的规模。注意对于每条含$k$个体原子的规则，需引入$\left( {k - 1}\right)  \otimes$个门和1个$\oplus$门。证毕。

We will use the following proposition, which is an unconditional lower bound on the size of a semiring circuit that computes the $k$ -clique.

我们将使用以下命题，这是计算$k$-团问题的半环电路规模的无条件下限。

Proposition D.3 ([27]). Any semiring circuit that evaluates the $k$ -clique polynomial over ${\mathrm{{Trop}}}^{ + } =$ $\left( {{\mathbb{R}}_{ + }\cup \{ \infty \} ,\min ,+,\infty ,0}\right)$ has size $\Omega \left( {n}^{k}\right) .$

命题D.3（[27]）。任何在${\mathrm{{Trop}}}^{ + } =$$\left( {{\mathbb{R}}_{ + }\cup \{ \infty \} ,\min ,+,\infty ,0}\right)$上计算$k$-团多项式的半环电路，其规模至少为$\Omega \left( {n}^{k}\right) .$

We are now ready to prove the first part of Theorem 3.4.

现在我们可以证明定理3.4的第一部分。

Proof of THEOREM 3.4,PART (1). Consider the program $P$ from Proposition D.1. This program returns the minimum weight of a $k$ -clique in a graph $G$ ,where $k = \ell  - 1 + \ell  \cdot  w$ . Let ${f}_{P}$ be the provenance polynomial for $P$ . From Proposition D.3,we know that any semiring circuit that evaluates ${f}_{P}$ over the Trop ${}^{ + }$ has size $\Omega \left( {n}^{k}\right)$ . Applying finally Proposition D.2,we get that the size of any grounded program equivalent to $P$ over ${\operatorname{Trop}}^{ + }$ must also be $\Omega \left( {n}^{k}\right)$ .

定理3.4第(1)部分的证明。考虑命题D.1中的程序$P$，该程序返回图$G$中$k$-团的最小权重，其中$k = \ell  - 1 + \ell  \cdot  w$。设${f}_{P}$为$P$的溯源多项式。根据命题D.3，任何在Trop${}^{ + }$上计算${f}_{P}$的半环电路，其规模必为$\Omega \left( {n}^{k}\right)$。最后应用命题D.2可得：在${\operatorname{Trop}}^{ + }$上等价于$P$的任何接地程序，其规模也必然为$\Omega \left( {n}^{k}\right)$。

### D.2 Missing Proof for Part (2) of Theorem 3.4

### D.2 定理3.4第(2)部分的缺失证明

Conditional Lower Bounds. Using the construction of Proposition D.1, we can obtain directly a conditional lower bound on the running time using problems from fine-grained complexity. In particular,we consider the min-weight $\ell$ -Clique hypothesis [33]: there is no algorithm that can find a $\ell$ -Clique of minimum total edge weight in an $n$ -node graph with nonnegative integer edge weights in time $O\left( {n}^{\ell  - c}\right)$ for some constant $c > 0$ . Observing that running the Datalog program of Proposition D.1 over the tropical semiring corresponds to solving the min weight $\ell$ -Clique problem, we obtain the following proposition, which proves part (2) of Theorem 3.4.

条件性下界。利用命题D.1的构造，我们可以直接从细粒度复杂度问题中获得运行时间的条件性下界。具体而言，我们考虑最小权重$\ell$-团假设[33]：不存在算法能在$O\left( {n}^{\ell  - c}\right)$时间内，从具有非负整数边权的$n$节点图中找出边权总和最小的$\ell$-团（其中$c > 0$为常数）。注意到在热带半环上运行命题D.1的Datalog程序等价于求解最小权重$\ell$-团问题，由此得到下述命题，该命题证明了定理3.4的第(2)部分。

Proposition D.4. Take any integer $k \geq  2$ and any rational number $w \geq  1$ such that $k \cdot  w$ is an integer. There is a (non-recursive,non-linear) Datalog program $P$ over the tropical semiring with IDB and EDB arities at most $k$ such that $\operatorname{subw}\left( P\right)  = w$ ,such that no algorithm can compute it in time $O\left( {n}^{{kw} + k - 1 - o\left( 1\right) }\right)$ assuming the min-weight $k$ -Clique hypothesis.

命题D.4。任取整数$k \geq  2$和有理数$w \geq  1$使得$k \cdot  w$为整数。存在一个热带半环上的（非递归、非线性）Datalog程序$P$，其IDB和EDB元数至多为$k$，满足$\operatorname{subw}\left( P\right)  = w$，且在最小权重$k$-团假设下，不存在算法能在$O\left( {n}^{{kw} + k - 1 - o\left( 1\right) }\right)$时间内计算该程序。

### E.1 Sum-product query evaluation using the PANDA algorithm

### E.1 使用PANDA算法评估和积查询

The Proof-Assisted Entropic Degree-Aware (PANDA) algorithm [29] is originally introduced to evaluate CQs, i.e. sum-prod queries over the Boolean semiring. This section demonstrates that the PANDA algorithm can be used to evaluate sum-prod queries over any dioid. First, we introduce the concept of assignment functions for tree decompositions.

证明辅助熵度感知（PANDA）算法[29]最初用于评估CQs，即布尔半环上的和积查询。本节论证该算法可推广至任意双半环上的和积查询评估。首先引入树分解的分配函数概念。

Assignment Function. An assignment function $v : \mathcal{E} \mapsto  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ for a tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is a function that maps each hyperedge $J \in  \mathcal{E}$ into a node $t$ in the decomposition such that $J \subseteq  \chi \left( t\right)$ .

分配函数。树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的分配函数$v : \mathcal{E} \mapsto  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$是将每条超边$J \in  \mathcal{E}$映射至分解中节点$t$的函数，且满足$J \subseteq  \chi \left( t\right)$。

Let $T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \leftarrow  {\varphi }_{1}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \oplus  {\varphi }_{2}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \oplus  \ldots$ be a Datalog rule over a dioid $\sigma$ ,where $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  : {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \mid  H}}{\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$ is a sum-prod query in the body of the rule. The $i$ -th ICO freezes ${T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$ and evaluates $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ as a non-recursive sum-prod query over the dioid $\sigma$ to get the output ${\varphi }^{\left( i + 1\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ ,i.e.,

设$T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \leftarrow  {\varphi }_{1}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \oplus  {\varphi }_{2}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \oplus  \ldots$为半环$\sigma$上的Datalog规则，其中$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  : {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \mid  H}}{\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$是规则体中的求和-乘积查询。第$i$次ICO冻结${T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$并将$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$作为半环$\sigma$上的非递归求和-乘积查询求值，得到输出${\varphi }^{\left( i + 1\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$，即

$$
{\varphi }^{\left( i + 1\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  : \;{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  H}H}{\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E}}{T}_{J}^{\left( i\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right) . \tag{10}
$$

PANDA evaluates (10) when $\sigma$ is the Boolean semiring in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} \cdot  \left| {{\varphi }^{\left( i + 1\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right| }\right)$ ,assuming $m$ is the sum of sizes of the input predicates ${T}_{J}^{\left( i\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$ and ${\varphi }^{\left( i + 1\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ is the output (or derived) $\sigma$ -relation.

当$\sigma$为布尔半环时，PANDA在时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} \cdot  \left| {{\varphi }^{\left( i + 1\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right| }\right)$内计算(10)，假设$m$是输入谓词${T}_{J}^{\left( i\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$和${\varphi }^{\left( i + 1\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$（即输出/派生$\sigma$关系）的规模之和。

Now we present the evaluation algorithm for sum-prod query evaluation over any dioid $\sigma$ . The algorithm is as follows:

现在我们提出针对任意半环$\sigma$的求和-乘积查询求值算法，具体如下：

(1) Take every non-redundant tree decomposition of the hypergraph $\left( {\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$ associated with the sum-prod query $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$

(1) 获取与求和-乘积查询$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$相关联的超图$\left( {\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}}\right)$的所有非冗余树分解

$$
\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{M},{\chi }_{M}}\right) 
$$

We assume that there are $M$ of them,where $M$ is finite under data complexity,i.e. independent of the input instance (Proposition 2.9 in [29]).

假设共有$M$个树分解，其中$M$在数据复杂度下是有限的（即与输入实例无关，参见[29]中命题2.9）。

(2) Write a set of disjunctive rules (where the head is a disjunction of output relations)

(2) 编写一组析取规则（其头部是输出关系的析取式）

$$
\rho  : \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }}{B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\right)  \leftarrow  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{J \in  \mathcal{E}}}{B}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)  \tag{11}
$$

where ${B}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$ are $\mathbb{B}$ -relations and a tuple ${\mathbf{c}}_{J} \in  {B}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$ if and only if ${T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)  \neq  \mathbf{0}$ ; and each disjunctive rule $\rho$ picks one node ${t}_{i} \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$ from every $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ to construct its head of $\rho$ . Thus,there are ${\Pi }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }\left| {V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\right|$ such disjunctive rules.

其中${B}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$是$\mathbb{B}$关系，当且仅当${T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)  \neq  \mathbf{0}$时存在元组${\mathbf{c}}_{J} \in  {B}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$；每条析取规则$\rho$从每个$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$中选取一个节点${t}_{i} \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$来构建其规则头$\rho$。因此共有${\Pi }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }\left| {V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\right|$条此类析取规则。

(3) On each disjunctive rule $\rho$ that selected nodes $\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},\cdots ,{t}_{M}}\right)  \in  { \times  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$ ,apply the PANDA algorithm to get a materialized view ${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\right)$ for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack$ . It is shown by [29] that PANDA evaluates any disjunctive rule in (11) in $\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{\text{subw }}\right)$ time and every output view ${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }^{ * }$ is of size $\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{\text{subw }}\right)$ . The reader may refer to [29] for the formal evaluation semantics of a disjunctive rule.

(3) 对每条选中节点$\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},\cdots ,{t}_{M}}\right)  \in  { \times  }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$的析取规则$\rho$，应用PANDA算法为每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack$生成物化视图${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\right)$。文献[29]表明，PANDA可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{\text{subw }}\right)$时间内计算(11)式中任意析取规则，且每个输出视图${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }^{ * }$的大小为$\widetilde{O}\left( {m}^{\text{subw }}\right)$。析取规则的形式化求值语义详见文献[29]。

(4) For each node $t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$ in each $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ ,evaluate the sum-prod query

(4) 对于每个$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$中的节点$t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$，计算其和积查询

$$
\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  \leftarrow  \overline{{B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  \otimes  \mathop{\bigotimes }\limits_{{J \in  \mathcal{E} : {v}_{i}\left( J\right)  = t}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right) ,
$$

where the input $\sigma$ -relation $\overline{{B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$ stores a tuple ${\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }$ as value 1 if the tuple is in the union over all ${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$ emitted by (possibly multiple) PANDA invocations in step (3) across all disjunctive rules. Indeed,observe that each node $t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$ participates in

其中输入$\sigma$-关系$\overline{{B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$将元组${\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }$存储为值1的条件是：该元组存在于步骤(3)中所有析取规则上（可能多次）调用PANDA所生成${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$的并集内。值得注意的是，每个节点$t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$会参与

${\Pi }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  i}\left| {V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{j}\right) }\right|$ disjunctive rules. Here,let ${v}_{i} : \mathcal{E} \rightarrow  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$ be an arbitary assignment function for $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ that assigns each $J \in  \mathcal{E}$ to a node $t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$ such that $J \subseteq  {\chi }_{i}\left( t\right)$ . Such a ${v}_{i}$ is always possible by the definition of a tree decomposition.

${\Pi }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  i}\left| {V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{j}\right) }\right|$条析取规则。此处设${v}_{i} : \mathcal{E} \rightarrow  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$为$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$的任意分配函数，它将每个$J \in  \mathcal{E}$分配给满足$J \subseteq  {\chi }_{i}\left( t\right)$的节点$t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right)$。根据树分解的定义，这样的${v}_{i}$始终存在。

(5) For every $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ ,evaluate the acyclic sum-prod query

(5) 对每个$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$，计算无环和积查询

$$
{\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  } \smallsetminus  H}{\bigotimes }_{t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\right)  \tag{12}
$$

using the InsideOut algorithm [2] in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {{\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right| }\right)$ ,where $\left| {\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right|$ is the size of input relation.

使用InsideOut算法[2]在$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {{\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right| }\right)$时间内完成计算，其中$\left| {\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right|$为输入关系的大小。

(5) Get the final query result $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ by ${\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }{\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ .

(5) 通过${\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }{\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$获取最终查询结果$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$。

The correctness of the algorithm over the Boolean semiring and when $H = \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ or $\varnothing$ is established in Corollary 7.13 of [29]. We lift the corollary to a dioid,and over arbitrary $\varnothing  \subseteq  H \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$ .

该算法在布尔半环上的正确性及当$H = \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$或$\varnothing$时的情况已在文献[29]推论7.13中确立。我们将该推论推广至双半环，并适用于任意$\varnothing  \subseteq  H \subseteq  \left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack$。

LEMMA E.1. $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }{\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ ,where $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ is the target sum-prod query (10) and ${\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ is an acyclic query (12) from the $i$ -th tree decomposition $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ .

引理E.1. $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }{\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$，其中$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$为目标求和-乘积查询(10)，${\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$为来自第$i$个树分解$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$的无环查询(12)。

Proof. By Corollary 7.13 of [29] (the correctness over the Boolean semiring), for any constant full tuple ${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  },\varphi \left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)  \neq  \mathbf{0}$ if and only if there is at least one decomposition $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$ ,where $i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack$ ,such that ${\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bigotimes }_{t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  \neq  \mathbf{0}$ . Here,we use ${\mathbf{c}}_{J}$ as a shorthand for the projection of ${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$ on the variables in ${\mathbf{x}}_{J}$ . In fact,for any such $i$ where ${\phi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}}$ ${\bigotimes }_{t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  \neq  \mathbf{0}$ (possibly many of them),

证明。根据文献[29]推论7.13（布尔半环上的正确性），对于任意完整常量元组${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  },\varphi \left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)  \neq  \mathbf{0}$，当且仅当存在至少一个分解$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\chi }_{i}}\right)$（其中$i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack$）使得${\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\bigotimes }_{t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  \neq  \mathbf{0}$。此处使用${\mathbf{c}}_{J}$作为${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$在变量集${\mathbf{x}}_{J}$上投影的简写。实际上，对于所有满足${\phi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}}$${\bigotimes }_{t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  \neq  \mathbf{0}$的$i$（可能存在多个）而言，

$$
{\phi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\bigotimes }_{t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\overline{{T}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 = {\bigotimes }_{t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\overline{{B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E} : {v}_{i}\left( J\right)  = t}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) 
$$

$$
 = {\bigotimes }_{t \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\mathbf{1} \otimes  {\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E} : {v}_{i}\left( J\right)  = t}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) 
$$

$$
 = {\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right) 
$$

where the third line uses the fact that $\overline{{B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  = \mathbf{1}$ because otherwise, ${\phi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  = \mathbf{0}$ (a contradiction). Thus,by idempotence of $\oplus  ,{\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }{\phi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$ for any ${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$ . Now for an arbitary tuple ${\mathbf{c}}_{H}$ ,

第三行利用了$\overline{{B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  = \mathbf{1}$的性质，否则会导致${\phi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  = \mathbf{0}$（矛盾）。因此，根据$\oplus  ,{\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }{\phi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }\right)  = {\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E}}{T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{J}\right)$的幂等性，对于任意${\mathbf{c}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$都成立。现考虑任意元组${\mathbf{c}}_{H}$，

$$
{\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }{\varphi }_{i}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{H}\right)  = {\bigoplus }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  M\right\rbrack  }{\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  } \smallsetminus  H}{\phi }_{i}\left( {{\mathbf{c}}_{H},{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  H}}\right) 
$$

(change aggregation order)

（改变聚合顺序）

$$
 = {\bigoplus }_{\mathbf{x}\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  H}\left( {{\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E}}{T}_{J}\left( {{\Pi }_{J}\left( {{\mathbf{c}}_{H},{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  H}}\right) }\right) }\right) 
$$

(by idempotence of $\oplus$ )

（基于$\oplus$的幂等性）

$$
 = \varphi \left( {\mathbf{c}}_{H}\right) 
$$

where $\left( {{\mathbf{c}}_{H},{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  H}}\right)$ denotes an arbitary (full) tuple over the active domain of ${\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$ where its projection on ${\mathbf{x}}_{H}$ agrees with ${\mathbf{c}}_{H}$ .

其中$\left( {{\mathbf{c}}_{H},{\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  H}}\right)$表示${\mathbf{x}}_{\left\lbrack  \ell \right\rbrack  }$活动域上的任意（完整）元组，其在${\mathbf{x}}_{H}$上的投影与${\mathbf{c}}_{H}$一致。

As a result of the Lemma E. 1 and Theorem 1.7 of [29], we have the following theorem.

根据文献[29]中的引理E.1和定理1.7，我们得到以下定理。

THEOREM E.2. The above evaluation algorithm evaluates a sum-prod query over a dioid $\sigma$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} \cdot  \left| {\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right| }\right)$ ,where subw is the submodular width of the query, $m$ is the sum of sizes of the input $\sigma$ -relations ${T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$ and $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ is the output $\sigma$ -relation.

定理E.2。上述评估算法在时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} \cdot  \left| {\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right| }\right)$内计算双半环$\sigma$上的求和-乘积查询，其中subw表示查询的子模宽度，${T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$是输入$\sigma$关系的大小总和，$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$是输出$\sigma$关系。

### E.2 Continuing Example 6.1

### E.2 续例6.1

Example E.3. In Example 6.1,we ground the following Datalog program $P$ that finds nodes that are reachable via a dimond-pattern from some node in a set $U$ :

例E.3。在例6.1中，我们实例化了以下Datalog程序$P$，该程序查找通过菱形模式从集合$U$中某个节点可达的节点：

$$
T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  U\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \oplus  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{234}}T\left( {x}_{3}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{32}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{2}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{21}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{1}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{34}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{41}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right) 
$$

A simple grounding is to ground the 4-cycle join first, materializing the opposite nodes of the cycle, i.e.

简单的实例化方法是先实例化4环连接，具体化环的对向节点，即

$$
\operatorname{Cycle}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{24}}{R}_{32}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{2}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{21}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{1}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{34}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \otimes  {R}_{41}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right) 
$$

$$
T\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  U\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \oplus  {\bigoplus }_{{\mathbf{x}}_{3}}T\left( {x}_{3}\right)  \otimes  \operatorname{Cycle}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right) 
$$

PANDA evaluates the first rule (essentially a non-recursive sum-prod query) in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{3/2} + {n}^{2}}\right)$ , since $\operatorname{Cycle}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right)$ has a grounding (or output) of size $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ .

PANDA在时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{3/2} + {n}^{2}}\right)$内评估第一条规则（本质上是非递归求和-乘积查询），因为$\operatorname{Cycle}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\right)$具有大小为$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$的实例化（或输出）。

Thus,grounding each bag ${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$ by its corresponding view ${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$ suffices,i.e. we insert into $G$ a rule:

因此，通过对应的视图${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$对每个包${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$进行接地处理即可，即我们向$G$插入一条规则：

$$
{B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)  \leftarrow  {\bigotimes }_{J \in  \mathcal{E} : {v}_{i}\left( J\right)  = t}{T}_{J}\left( {{\Pi }_{J}\left( {\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right) }\right) 
$$

for every ${\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) } \in  {B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$ . Finally,to ground $T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ ,we simply call Algorithm 3 and since the size of ${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$ is $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right)$ ,by Theorem 3.2,we can construct a grounding for $T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right) }\right)$ of size $\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right) }\right)$ .

对于每个${\mathbf{c}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) } \in  {B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$。最后，为了接地处理$T\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$，我们直接调用算法3，由于${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$的大小为$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right)$，根据定理3.2，我们可以在$\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right) }\right)$时间内构建一个大小为$\widetilde{O}\left( {{n}^{k - 1} \cdot  \left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right) }\right)$的接地结果。

### E.4 Missing details from Section 6: Free-connexity, Linear Programs and Fractional Hypertree-width.

### E.4 第6章节遗漏细节：自由连接性、线性规划与分数超树宽度

Proof of Proposition 6.2. The proof follows directly from that of Theorem 3.3 and Theorem 5.3, by constraining Algorithm 4 to only use non-redundant free-connex tree decompositions.

命题6.2的证明。该证明直接源自定理3.3和定理5.3，通过约束算法4仅使用非冗余的自由连接树分解。

Degree Constraints. In fact,beyond the input size $m$ ,the evaluation algorithm presented in Appendix E. 1 can incorporate a broader class of constraints called degree constraints [29] to evaluate a sum-prod query over a dioid more efficiently. A degree constraint [29] is an assertion on an atom ${T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$ and $V \subset  V \subseteq  J$ of the form

度约束。实际上，除了输入规模$m$外，附录E.1中提出的评估算法还能整合一类更广泛的约束（称为度约束[29]），以更高效地在双半环上评估求和乘积查询。度约束[29]是对原子${T}_{J}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{J}\right)$和$V \subset  V \subseteq  J$形式的一种断言

$$
\deg \left( {{\mathbf{x}}_{U} \mid  {\mathbf{x}}_{V}}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{{\mathbf{c}}_{V} \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{V}\right) }}\left| {{\Pi }_{U}T\left( {{\mathbf{x}}_{J \smallsetminus  V},{\mathbf{c}}_{V}}\right) }\right|  \leq  {m}_{U \mid  V}, \tag{13}
$$

where ${\Pi }_{U}T\left( {{\mathbf{x}}_{J \smallsetminus  V},{\mathbf{c}}_{V}}\right)$ is the relation obtained by first selecting tuples that agree with the tuple ${\mathbf{c}}_{V}$ on ${\mathbf{x}}_{V}$ and then projecting on ${\mathbf{x}}_{U}$ and ${m}_{U \mid  V}$ is a natural number. A cardinality constraint in our setting is therefore a degree constraint with ${m}_{J \mid  \varnothing } = m$ . Similarly,the active domain of $n$ for a variable ${x}_{i}\left( {i \in  J}\right)$ is a degree constraint with ${m}_{\{ i\}  \mid  \varnothing } = n$ . In fact,Proposition 6.3 is an improved grounding results for linear Datalog, using the constraints of active domains on each variable.

其中${\Pi }_{U}T\left( {{\mathbf{x}}_{J \smallsetminus  V},{\mathbf{c}}_{V}}\right)$是通过首先筛选与元组${\mathbf{c}}_{V}$在${\mathbf{x}}_{V}$上一致的元组，然后投影到${\mathbf{x}}_{U}$上得到的关系，而${m}_{U \mid  V}$是一个自然数。因此，在我们的设定中，基数约束就是${m}_{J \mid  \varnothing } = m$的度约束。类似地，变量${x}_{i}\left( {i \in  J}\right)$的活跃域$n$是${m}_{\{ i\}  \mid  \varnothing } = n$的度约束。实际上，命题6.3是针对线性Datalog的改进接地结果，利用了每个变量上的活跃域约束。

In addition,a functional dependency $V \rightarrow  U$ is a degree constraint with ${m}_{U \cup  V \mid  V} = 1$ . This can capture primary-key constraints EDBs,and repeated variables in IDBs (e.g. $T\left( {x,x,y}\right)$ essentially says, the first variable decides the second).

此外，函数依赖$V \rightarrow  U$是一种带有${m}_{U \cup  V \mid  V} = 1$的度数约束。它能捕获主键约束EDB，以及IDB中的重复变量（例如$T\left( {x,x,y}\right)$本质上表示第一个变量决定第二个变量）。

The evaluation algorithm presented in Appendix E. 1 uses PANDA as a black-box, thus it naturally inherits the capability of handling degree constraints as (13) from the original PANDA algorithm,to evaluate a sum-prod query $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ over a dioid more efficiently. Its runtime is shown to be predicated by the degree-aware submodular width. We refer the reader to [29] for the formal definition of degree-aware submodular width and in-depth analysis.

附录E.1给出的评估算法将PANDA作为黑盒使用，因此自然继承了原始PANDA算法处理如(13)式度数约束的能力，能更高效地计算在双半环上的求和-乘积查询$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$。其运行时间被证明取决于度数感知次模宽度。关于度数感知次模宽度的正式定义及深入分析，请参阅文献[29]。

Using PANDA as a subroutine, our grounding algorithm (Algorithm 4) also handles degree constraints for tighter groundings. For example, we show an improved grounding for linear Datalog, using the refined constraint that each variable in an IDB has an active domain of $n$ (see Table 1), instead of $O\left( {n}^{k}\right)$ for every IDB predicate.

以PANDA作为子程序，我们的接地算法（算法4）也能处理度数约束以获得更紧凑的接地。例如，我们展示了线性Datalog的改进接地方案，采用IDB中每个变量的活跃域为$n$的精细化约束（见表1），而非为每个IDB谓词设置$O\left( {n}^{k}\right)$。

Proof of Proposition 6.3. We follow the exact proof of Theorem 3.3. However, instead of the crude $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right)$ bound for each view ${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$ obtained at Line 9 of Algorithm 4,we show a tighter bound of $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} \cdot  \left( {{n}^{k}/m + 1}\right) }\right)$ .

命题6.3的证明。我们沿用定理3.3的证明框架。但不同于算法4第9行获得的视图<b1></b0>的粗略$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{subw }}}\right)$上界，我们给出了更精确的$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{\text{subw }} \cdot  \left( {{n}^{k}/m + 1}\right) }\right)$上界。

By the properties of PANDA [29] and the fact that all rules are linear, the fine-grained cardinality constraints are: (i) at most one IDB in each sum-prod query with an active domain of $O\left( n\right)$ for each of its variable,(ii) other EDB atoms are of size $O\left( m\right)$ . Note that(i)is a stronger constraint than the IDB cardinality constraint of $O\left( {n}^{k}\right)$ .

根据PANDA[29]的特性和所有规则均为线性的事实，细粒度基数约束为：(i)每个求和-乘积查询中至多一个IDB，其每个变量的活跃域为$O\left( n\right)$；(ii)其他EDB原子的大小为$O\left( m\right)$。注意(i)是比IDB基数约束$O\left( {n}^{k}\right)$更强的限制条件。

We can assume that PANDA terminates in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{w - {w}_{i}} \cdot  {n}^{k{w}_{i}}}\right)$ and the size of each resulting view ${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$ has a more accurate size bound of $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{w - {w}_{i}} \cdot  {n}^{k{w}_{i}}}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{w} \cdot  {\left( {n}^{k}/m\right) }^{{w}_{i}}}\right)$ ,where $w \leq$ subw and ${w}_{i} \leq  1$ . One may refer to Lemma 5.3 of [29] for the primal and dual linear programs that justifies the assumed bound.

我们可以假设PANDA在$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{w - {w}_{i}} \cdot  {n}^{k{w}_{i}}}\right)$时间内终止，且每个结果视图${B}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }^{ * }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{{\chi }_{i}\left( t\right) }\right)$具有更精确的$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{w - {w}_{i}} \cdot  {n}^{k{w}_{i}}}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{w} \cdot  {\left( {n}^{k}/m\right) }^{{w}_{i}}}\right)$大小上界，其中$w \leq$subw且${w}_{i} \leq  1$。关于支持该假设边界的原始与对偶线性规划，可参阅文献[29]的引理5.3。

We now split into two cases: if ${n}^{k} \leq  m$ ,then ${\left( {n}^{k}/m\right) }^{{w}_{i}} \leq  1$ ; otherwise, ${\left( {n}^{k}/m\right) }^{{w}_{i}} \leq  {n}^{k}/m$ . Hence, we get $\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{w} \cdot  \left( {{n}^{k}/m + 1}\right) }\right)$ ,where $w \leq$ subw for groundings intermediate bags.

现在分两种情况讨论：若${n}^{k} \leq  m$，则${\left( {n}^{k}/m\right) }^{{w}_{i}} \leq  1$；否则${\left( {n}^{k}/m\right) }^{{w}_{i}} \leq  {n}^{k}/m$。因此我们得到$\widetilde{O}\left( {{m}^{w} \cdot  \left( {{n}^{k}/m + 1}\right) }\right)$，其中$w \leq$subw表示接地中间包的次模宽度。

Proof of Proposition 6.4. We still use Algorithm 4 to ground $P$ ,with two slight modifications: (i) for each sum-prod query $\varphi$ ,pick one tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ that attains fhw $\left( \varphi \right)$ ; and(ii) use the InsideOut algorithm instead of PANDA on when grounding the bags ${B}_{\chi \left( t\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( t\right) }\right)$ . Thus, the size of the grounding for each bag is $O\left( {{m}^{\text{fhw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{fhw }}}\right)$ ,without the polylog factor! The rest of the proof is similar to that of Theorem 3.3 without using the idempotence of $\oplus$ ,since only one tree decomposition is used for each sum-prod query $\left( {M = 1\text{in that proof}}\right)$ .

命题6.4的证明。我们仍采用算法4对$P$进行基础化处理，仅作两处微调：(i) 对每个求和-乘积查询$\varphi$，选取达到分数超树宽度(fhw)$\left( \varphi \right)$的树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$；(ii) 在基础化处理包${B}_{\chi \left( t\right) }\left( {\mathbf{x}}_{\chi \left( t\right) }\right)$时使用InsideOut算法替代PANDA。因此每个包的基础化规模为$O\left( {{m}^{\text{fhw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{fhw }}}\right)$（不含多对数因子！）。剩余证明过程与定理3.3类似，但由于每个求和-乘积查询$\left( {M = 1\text{in that proof}}\right)$仅使用单一树分解，故无需利用$\oplus$的幂等性。

Similar to the free-connex submodular-width, the free-connex fractional hypertree-width of a sum-prod query $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ is defined as

与自由连接子模宽度类似，求和-乘积查询$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$的自由连接分数超树宽度定义为

$$
\text{ffhw}\left( {\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  {\mathcal{F}}_{f}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  {\Gamma }_{\ell }}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}h\left( {\chi \left( t\right) }\right) 
$$

where ${\mathcal{F}}_{f}$ is the set of all non-redundant free-connex tree decompositions of $\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$ . Obviously, ffhw $\left( {\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right)  \leq$ fhw $\left( {\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right)$ . We obtain the following corollary directly from Theorem 5.3 and Proposition 6.4.

其中${\mathcal{F}}_{f}$表示$\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right)$所有非冗余自由连接树分解的集合。显然有ffhw$\left( {\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right)  \leq$≤fhw$\left( {\varphi \left( {\mathbf{x}}_{H}\right) }\right)$。根据定理5.3和命题6.4可直接得出以下推论。

COROLLARY E.4. Let $P$ be a Datalog program where arity(P)is at most $k$ . Let ffhw be the free-connex fractional hypertree-width of $P$ . Let $\sigma$ be a naturally-ordered semiring. Then,a $\sigma$ -equivalent grounding can be constructed of size (and time) $O\left( {{m}^{\text{ffhw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{ffhw }}}\right)$ .

推论E.4。设$P$为元数(P)不超过$k$的Datalog程序，ffhw表示$P$的自由连接分数超树宽度，$\sigma$为自然序半环。则可构造规模（及时间复杂度）为$O\left( {{m}^{\text{ffhw }} + {n}^{k \cdot  \text{ffhw }}}\right)$的$\sigma$等价基础化。
